import os
import time
import datetime
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from config import NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP, NIFTY_SPOT_SYMBOL, DATA_DIR

def extract_item_oi(item, default=0):
    if not item:
        return default
    val = item.get("oi")
    if val is not None:
        try:
            return int(val)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass
    return default

def extract_item_oi_chg(item, default=None, strike=None, opt_type=None, prev_corridor=None):
    """
    Extracts Open Interest change for an option strike with a robust 3-tier fallback:
    1. Direct Fyers API key: 'oich', 'change_in_oi', 'oi_chg', 'oichange'
    2. Mathematical derivation from native Fyers fields: int(oi) - int(prev_oi)
    3. Historical corridor JSON boundary diff: current oi - last recorded snapshot oi
    Returns default (None) if truly missing, preventing silent 0-value false positives.
    """
    if not item:
        return default

    # Tier 1: Direct Fyers API key
    for k in ["oich", "change_in_oi", "oi_chg", "oichange"]:
        val = item.get(k)
        if val is not None:
            try:
                return int(val)
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                continue

    # Tier 2: Mathematical derivation from Fyers oi & prev_oi
    oi = item.get("oi")
    prev_oi = item.get("prev_oi")
    if oi is not None and prev_oi is not None:
        try:
            return int(oi) - int(prev_oi)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass

    # Tier 3: Historical Corridor JSON snapshot boundary diff
    if prev_corridor and strike is not None and opt_type is not None:
        try:
            sub_key = "call_strikes" if str(opt_type).upper() == "CE" else "put_strikes"
            prev_strikes = prev_corridor.get(sub_key, {})
            prev_strike_oi = prev_strikes.get(str(int(strike)))
            if oi is not None and prev_strike_oi is not None:
                return int(oi) - int(prev_strike_oi)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return default

def extract_item_ltp(item, default=0.0):
    if not item:
        return default
    val = item.get("ltp", item.get("lp"))
    if val is not None:
        try:
            return float(val)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass
    return default

MONTH_MAP = {
    1: "JAN", 2: "FEB", 3: "MAR", 4: "APR", 5: "MAY", 6: "JUN",
    7: "JUL", 8: "AUG", 9: "SEP", 10: "OCT", 11: "NOV", 12: "DEC"
}

_EXPIRIES_CACHE = {}

def get_fyers_expiries(fyers_client=None, force_refresh=False):
    """
    Fetches real active market expiries from Fyers optionchain API.
    Identifies true monthly expiry by calendar month grouping (last expiry of month)
    and constructs the NIFTY Futures symbol using locale-immune MONTH_MAP.
    Caches result per calendar day to avoid redundant network roundtrips.
    Returns:
        {
            "weekly": {"date": str, "timestamp": str, "dte": int, "label": str},
            "monthly": {"date": str, "timestamp": str, "dte": int, "label": str},
            "futures_symbol": str,
            "all_expiries": list of dicts
        }
    """
    global _EXPIRIES_CACHE
    today = datetime.date.today()
    today_key = today.isoformat()

    if not force_refresh and fyers_client and today_key in _EXPIRIES_CACHE:
        return _EXPIRIES_CACHE[today_key]

    parsed_items = []

    if fyers_client:
        try:
            res = fyers_client.optionchain({"symbol": NIFTY_SPOT_SYMBOL, "strikecount": 1})
            if res.get("s") == "ok" and res.get("data"):
                raw_exp = res["data"].get("expiryData", [])
                for item in raw_exp:
                    d_str = item.get("date")
                    if not d_str:
                        continue
                    try:
                        d_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(d_str, "%d-%m-%Y").date()
                        dte = (d_obj - today).days
                        parsed_items.append((d_obj, d_str, str(item.get("expiry")), dte, item))
                    except Exception:
                        continue
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error fetching expiries from Fyers: {e}")

    # Fallback if Fyers not connected or empty
    if not parsed_items:
        days_ahead = (3 - today.weekday()) % 7
        if days_ahead == 0:
            days_ahead = 7
        w_date = today + datetime.timedelta(days=days_ahead)
        w_str = w_date.strftime("%d-%m-%Y")
        w_ts = str(int(datetime.datetime.combine(w_date, datetime.time(15, 30)).timestamp()))

        m_date = today + datetime.timedelta(days=14)
        m_str = m_date.strftime("%d-%m-%Y")
        m_ts = str(int(datetime.datetime.combine(m_date, datetime.time(15, 30)).timestamp()))

        parsed_items = [
            (w_date, w_str, w_ts, days_ahead, {"expiry_flag": "W"}),
            (m_date, m_str, m_ts, 14, {"expiry_flag": "M"})
        ]

    # 1. Group by (year, month) to accurately find the LAST expiry date of each calendar month (True Monthly Expiry)
    month_groups = {}
    for d_obj, d_str, ts, dte, item in parsed_items:
        ym = (d_obj.year, d_obj.month)
        if ym not in month_groups:
            month_groups[ym] = []
        month_groups[ym].append((d_obj, d_str, ts, dte, item))

    # Set of true monthly expiry dates
    monthly_dates = set()
    monthly_entries = []
    for ym in sorted(month_groups.keys()):
        month_groups[ym].sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        last_in_month = month_groups[ym][-1]
        monthly_dates.add(last_in_month[0])
        monthly_entries.append(last_in_month)

    # 2. Build complete expiry list with 100% accurate Weekly/Monthly flags
    exp_list = []
    for d_obj, d_str, ts, dte, item in parsed_items:
        is_monthly = d_obj in monthly_dates or item.get("expiry_flag") == "M"
        flag = "Monthly" if is_monthly else "Weekly"
        exp_list.append({
            "date": d_str,
            "flag": flag,
            "dte": dte,
            "timestamp": ts,
            "label": f"{flag} ({d_str} • {dte}d)"
        })

    # Nearest weekly expiry (>= today, or first available)
    future_weeklies = [x for x in exp_list if x["flag"] == "Weekly" and x["dte"] >= 0]
    weekly = future_weeklies[0] if future_weeklies else exp_list[0]

    # Nearest active monthly expiry (>= today, or earliest monthly)
    active_monthly_entry = None
    for m_entry in monthly_entries:
        if m_entry[0] >= today:
            active_monthly_entry = m_entry
            break
    if not active_monthly_entry:
        active_monthly_entry = monthly_entries[0]

    d_target = active_monthly_entry[0]
    monthly = next((x for x in exp_list if x["date"] == active_monthly_entry[1]), exp_list[-1])

    # Construct locale-immune NIFTY Futures symbol (NSE:NIFTY{YY}{MMM}FUT)
    yy = str(d_target.year)[-2:]
    mmm = MONTH_MAP.get(d_target.month, "SEP")
    fut_sym = f"NSE:NIFTY{yy}{mmm}FUT"

    res_dict = {
        "weekly": weekly,
        "monthly": monthly,
        "futures_symbol": fut_sym,
        "all_expiries": exp_list
    }

    if fyers_client and parsed_items:
        _EXPIRIES_CACHE[today_key] = res_dict

    return res_dict

def get_nifty_future_symbol(fyers_client=None):
    """
    Dynamically resolves the active near-month NIFTY Futures symbol (e.g. NSE:NIFTY26SEPFUT)
    directly using Fyers monthly expiry data.
    """
    exp_info = get_fyers_expiries(fyers_client)
    return exp_info["futures_symbol"]

def calculate_atm_strike(spot_price, step=NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP):
    """Rounds spot price to the nearest strike step (50 for NIFTY)."""
    return int(round(spot_price / step) * step)

def get_atm_range_strikes(atm_strike, step=NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP, count=2):
    """
    Returns list of strikes: [ATM-2, ATM-1, ATM, ATM+1, ATM+2]
    e.g. for ATM 24500: [24400, 24450, 24500, 24550, 24600]
    """
    strikes = []
    for i in range(-count, count + 1):
        strikes.append(atm_strike + (i * step))
    return strikes

def classify_pure_oi_signal(ce_oi_change, pe_oi_change, spot_change=0.0):
    """
    Classifies market buildup based on Spot price direction and Call vs Put OI changes
    in the 6-strike active writing corridor (ATM..ATM+2 for CE, ATM-2..ATM for PE):
    - Price Up (>=0) & Put OI Up (>0): Long Build-up (Bullish 🟢)
    - Price Up (>=0) & Call OI Down (<0): Short Covering (Bullish 🔵)
    - Price Down (<0) & Call OI Up (>0): Short Build-up (Bearish 🔴)
    - Price Down (<0) & Put OI Down (<0): Long Unwinding (Bearish ⚪)
    """
    # 0. MISSING DATA GUARD
    if ce_oi_change is None or pe_oi_change is None:
        return "Awaiting OI Feed", "Neutral", "⚪"

    # 1. PRICE RISING (BULLISH CANDLE)
    if spot_change > 0:
        # Check if Short Covering is the dominant driver (Call sellers rushing out)
        if ce_oi_change < 0 and (pe_oi_change <= 0 or abs(ce_oi_change) > pe_oi_change):
            return "Short Covering", "Bullish", "🔵"
        # Both positive, but Call writing dominates despite price rise (Resistance capping)
        if ce_oi_change > 0 and pe_oi_change > 0 and ce_oi_change > pe_oi_change:
            return "Short Buildup (Divergence)", "Bearish", "🔴"
        # Genuine Put writing driving the rally
        elif pe_oi_change > 0:
            return "Long Build-up", "Bullish", "🟢"
        # Fallback short covering if call writers unwinding
        elif ce_oi_change < 0:
            return "Short Covering", "Bullish", "🔵"
        # Divergence: Price went up but Call writing increased and Put unwound
        elif ce_oi_change > 0 and pe_oi_change < 0:
            return "Short Buildup (Divergence)", "Bearish", "🔴"
        else:
            return "Consolidation", "Neutral", "⚪"

    # 2. PRICE FALLING (BEARISH CANDLE)
    elif spot_change < 0:
        # Check if Long Unwinding is the dominant driver (Put sellers dumping/stopping out)
        if pe_oi_change < 0 and (ce_oi_change <= 0 or abs(pe_oi_change) > ce_oi_change):
            return "Long Unwinding", "Bearish", "⚪"
        # Both positive, but Put writing dominates despite price drop (Support absorbing)
        if pe_oi_change > 0 and ce_oi_change > 0 and pe_oi_change > ce_oi_change:
            return "Long Buildup (Divergence)", "Bullish", "🟢"
        # Genuine Call writing putting a lid on the market
        elif ce_oi_change > 0:
            return "Short Build-up", "Bearish", "🔴"
        # Fallback long unwinding if put writers unwinding
        elif pe_oi_change < 0:
            return "Long Unwinding", "Bearish", "⚪"
        # Divergence: Price dropped but Put writing held strong and Call unwound
        elif pe_oi_change > 0 and ce_oi_change < 0:
            return "Long Buildup (Divergence)", "Bullish", "🟢"
        else:
            return "Consolidation", "Neutral", "⚪"

    # 3. FLAT CANDLE (spot_change == 0: purely delta-driven)
    else:
        if ce_oi_change < 0 and pe_oi_change < 0:
            if abs(ce_oi_change) >= abs(pe_oi_change):
                return "Short Covering", "Bullish", "🔵"
            else:
                return "Long Unwinding", "Bearish", "⚪"
        if ce_oi_change < 0 and pe_oi_change >= 0:
            return "Short Covering", "Bullish", "🔵"
        if pe_oi_change < 0 and ce_oi_change >= 0:
            return "Long Unwinding", "Bearish", "⚪"
        if pe_oi_change > 0 and ce_oi_change <= 0:
            return "Long Build-up", "Bullish", "🟢"
        if ce_oi_change > 0 and pe_oi_change <= 0:
            return "Short Build-up", "Bearish", "🔴"
        if ce_oi_change > 0 and pe_oi_change > 0:
            if pe_oi_change > ce_oi_change * 1.1:
                return "Long Build-up", "Bullish", "🟢"
            elif ce_oi_change > pe_oi_change * 1.1:
                return "Short Build-up", "Bearish", "🔴"
            else:
                return "Consolidation", "Neutral", "⚪"

    return "Neutral", "Neutral", "⚪"

def classify_smart_buildup_signal(ce_oi_change, pe_oi_change, spot_change=0.0, fut_buy_pct=50.0, net_delta=0.0):
    """
    Advanced Institutional Order-Flow & Trap Classification (Signal 2):
    - Institutional Bear Trap (Call Squeeze Setup): Price rises & Futures Buy % >= 80.0% but Retail Corridor OI shows Short Buildup/Divergence.
      Flags: '⚡ Bear Trap (XX% Buy)' (Call Squeeze alert)
    - Institutional Bull Trap (Long Liquidation Setup): Price drops & Futures Sell % >= 80.0% (Buy % <= 20.0%) but Retail Corridor OI shows Long Buildup/Divergence.
      Flags: '⚡ Bull Trap (XX% Sell)' (Long Liquidation alert)
    - Otherwise, provides confirmed directional flow:
      🟢 Long Buildup, 🔴 Short Buildup, 🔵 Short Covering, ⚪ Long Unwinding, ⚪ Consolidation
    """
    if ce_oi_change is None or pe_oi_change is None:
        return "—", "Neutral", "⚪"
    try:
        if np.isnan(ce_oi_change) or np.isnan(pe_oi_change):
            return "—", "Neutral", "⚪"
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        bp = float(fut_buy_pct) if fut_buy_pct is not None and not np.isnan(fut_buy_pct) else 50.0
    except Exception:
        bp = 50.0

    net_oi = pe_oi_change - ce_oi_change

    # 1. INSTITUTIONAL BEAR TRAP (Call Squeeze): Strong futures buying (bp >= 75%), while Options are BEARISH (shorts trapped)
    if bp >= 75.0:
        is_bearish_options = (net_oi < 0) or (ce_oi_change > 0 and ce_oi_change >= pe_oi_change) or (pe_oi_change < 0)
        if is_bearish_options:
            return f"⚡ Bear Trap ({bp:.1f}% Buy)", "Strong Bullish Squeeze", "⚡"
        else:
            return "🟢 Long Buildup", "Strong Bullish", "🟢"

    # 2. INSTITUTIONAL BULL TRAP (Long Liquidation): Strong futures selling (bp <= 25%), while Options are BULLISH (longs trapped)
    if bp <= 25.0:
        sp = 100.0 - bp
        is_bullish_options = (net_oi > 0) or (pe_oi_change > 0 and pe_oi_change >= ce_oi_change) or (ce_oi_change < 0)
        if is_bullish_options:
            return f"⚡ Bull Trap ({sp:.1f}% Sell)", "Strong Bearish Trap", "⚡"
        else:
            return "🔴 Short Buildup", "Strong Bearish", "🔴"

    # 3. CONFIRMED DIRECTIONAL FLOW
    if spot_change > 0:
        if ce_oi_change < 0 and (pe_oi_change <= 0 or abs(ce_oi_change) > pe_oi_change):
            return "🔵 Short Covering", "Bullish", "🔵"
        elif pe_oi_change > 0:
            return "🟢 Long Buildup", "Bullish", "🟢"
        elif ce_oi_change < 0:
            return "🔵 Short Covering", "Bullish", "🔵"
        else:
            return "🟢 Long Buildup", "Bullish", "🟢"
    elif spot_change < 0:
        if pe_oi_change < 0 and (ce_oi_change <= 0 or abs(pe_oi_change) > ce_oi_change):
            return "⚪ Long Unwinding", "Bearish", "⚪"
        elif ce_oi_change > 0:
            return "🔴 Short Buildup", "Bearish", "🔴"
        elif pe_oi_change < 0:
            return "⚪ Long Unwinding", "Bearish", "⚪"
        else:
            return "🔴 Short Buildup", "Bearish", "🔴"
    else:
        if ce_oi_change < 0:
            return "🔵 Short Covering", "Bullish", "🔵"
        elif pe_oi_change < 0:
            return "⚪ Long Unwinding", "Bearish", "⚪"
        elif pe_oi_change > 0:
            return "🟢 Long Buildup", "Bullish", "🟢"
        elif ce_oi_change > 0:
            return "🔴 Short Buildup", "Bearish", "🔴"
        return "⚪ Consolidation", "Neutral", "⚪"

class OIEngine:
    get_fyers_expiries = staticmethod(get_fyers_expiries)
    get_nifty_future_symbol = staticmethod(get_nifty_future_symbol)
    classify_pure_oi_signal = staticmethod(classify_pure_oi_signal)
    classify_smart_buildup_signal = staticmethod(classify_smart_buildup_signal)

    def __init__(self):
        self.last_atm = None
        self.shift_history = []  # [{timestamp, old_atm, new_atm, spot}]
        self.cached_spot = 24528.50
        self.prev_spot_close = 24480.00
        self.cached_candles = pd.DataFrame()

    def process_nifty_data(self, fyers_client=None, manual_spot=None, expiry_timestamp=None):
        """
        Fetches live data from Fyers or generates realistic simulated data.
        Returns:
            spot_data: {ltp, change, pct_change, atm, strikes}
            oi_table_df: DataFrame with ATM +-2 strikes and signals
            summary: {net_ce_oi_chg, net_pe_oi_chg, net_delta, overall_signal, pcr}
            shift_event: dict if ATM shifted, else None
        """
        now_ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        
        # 1. Get Spot & Futures Price
        spot_price = None
        prev_close = self.prev_spot_close
        fut_price = None
        fut_change = None
        fut_symbol = None

        exp_info = get_fyers_expiries(fyers_client)
        fut_symbol = exp_info.get("futures_symbol", "NSE:NIFTY26SEPFUT")

        if fyers_client:
            try:
                symbols_to_quote = f"{NIFTY_SPOT_SYMBOL},{fut_symbol}"
                res = fyers_client.quotes({"symbols": symbols_to_quote})
                if res.get("s") == "ok" and res.get("d"):
                    for item in res["d"]:
                        v = item.get("v", {})
                        sym = item.get("n", "")
                        if sym == NIFTY_SPOT_SYMBOL:
                            spot_price = float(v.get("lp", v.get("open_price", 24500.0)))
                            prev_close = float(v.get("prev_close_price", spot_price))
                        elif sym == fut_symbol or "FUT" in sym:
                            fut_price = float(v.get("lp", v.get("open_price", 24500.0)))
                            f_prev = float(v.get("prev_close_price", fut_price))
                            fut_change = round(fut_price - f_prev, 2)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error fetching live spot/fut quotes: {e}")

        if spot_price is None:
            if manual_spot is not None:
                spot_price = float(manual_spot)
            elif self.cached_spot is not None:
                spot_price = round(self.cached_spot, 2)
            else:
                spot_price = 24500.0
        
        self.cached_spot = spot_price
        self.prev_spot_close = prev_close
        spot_change = round(spot_price - prev_close, 2) if prev_close else 0.0
        spot_pct = round((spot_change / prev_close) * 100, 2) if prev_close else 0.0

        if fut_price is None:
            fut_price = spot_price
            fut_change = spot_change

        basis_diff = round(fut_price - spot_price, 2)
        basis_type = "Premium" if basis_diff >= 0 else "Discount"
        atm_strike = calculate_atm_strike(spot_price)
        shift_event = None

        if self.last_atm is not None and self.last_atm != atm_strike:
            shift_event = {
                "timestamp": now_ts,
                "old_atm": self.last_atm,
                "new_atm": atm_strike,
                "spot": spot_price,
                "direction": "UP ↗" if atm_strike > self.last_atm else "DOWN ↘"
            }
            self.shift_history.insert(0, shift_event)
            # Keep last 50 shift logs
            if len(self.shift_history) > 50:
                self.shift_history.pop()

        self.last_atm = atm_strike
        tracked_strikes = get_atm_range_strikes(atm_strike, step=NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP, count=2)

        # 3. Fetch or Simulate Option Chain for the 5 Strikes
        rows = []
        total_ce_oi = 0
        total_pe_oi = 0
        total_ce_oi_chg = 0
        total_pe_oi_chg = 0

        # Attempt to get real option chain if Fyers connected
        live_chain = None
        if fyers_client:
            try:
                # Fyers optionchain API with optional selected expiry timestamp
                req_chain = {"symbol": NIFTY_SPOT_SYMBOL, "strikecount": 15}
                if expiry_timestamp:
                    req_chain["timestamp"] = str(expiry_timestamp)
                res_opt = fyers_client.optionchain(req_chain)
                if res_opt.get("s") == "ok":
                    live_chain = res_opt.get("data", {})
                else:
                    print(f"⚠️ Fyers optionchain returned: {res_opt}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ Fyers option chain error: {e}")

        # Retrieve latest recorded corridor snapshot for Tier 3 boundary diff fallback (filtered to post-09:15 AM only)
        last_corridor_snap = None
        corridor_data = self._seed_corridor_from_csv("NIFTY")
        if corridor_data and isinstance(corridor_data, dict):
            market_open_time = datetime.time(9, 15, 0)
            valid_snaps = []
            for ts_str, snap in corridor_data.items():
                try:
                    parts = [int(p) for p in str(ts_str).split(":")]
                    if len(parts) >= 2:
                        t_obj = datetime.time(parts[0], parts[1], parts[2] if len(parts) > 2 else 0)
                        if t_obj >= market_open_time:
                            valid_snaps.append(snap)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if valid_snaps:
                last_corridor_snap = valid_snaps[-1]

        for strike in tracked_strikes:
            label = "ATM" if strike == atm_strike else (
                f"ATM +{int((strike - atm_strike) / NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP)}" if strike > atm_strike
                else f"ATM {int((strike - atm_strike) / NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP)}"
            )
            
            # CE & PE OI values
            if live_chain and "optionsChain" in live_chain:
                # Parse real chain
                ce_item = next((x for x in live_chain["optionsChain"] if x.get("strike_price") == strike and x.get("option_type") == "CE"), None)
                pe_item = next((x for x in live_chain["optionsChain"] if x.get("strike_price") == strike and x.get("option_type") == "PE"), None)
                ce_oi = extract_item_oi(ce_item, default=0)
                pe_oi = extract_item_oi(pe_item, default=0)
                ce_oi_chg = extract_item_oi_chg(ce_item, default=None, strike=strike, opt_type="CE", prev_corridor=last_corridor_snap)
                pe_oi_chg = extract_item_oi_chg(pe_item, default=None, strike=strike, opt_type="PE", prev_corridor=last_corridor_snap)
                ce_ltp = extract_item_ltp(ce_item, default=0.0)
                pe_ltp = extract_item_ltp(pe_item, default=0.0)
            else:
                # 100% Pure Fyers data mode: never generate synthetic fake numbers
                ce_oi = 0
                pe_oi = 0
                ce_oi_chg = None
                pe_oi_chg = None
                ce_ltp = 0.0
                pe_ltp = 0.0

            if ce_oi_chg is not None and pe_oi_chg is not None:
                delta_oi = pe_oi_chg - ce_oi_chg
            elif pe_oi_chg is not None:
                delta_oi = pe_oi_chg
            elif ce_oi_chg is not None:
                delta_oi = -ce_oi_chg
            else:
                delta_oi = 0

            signal_name, bias, icon = classify_pure_oi_signal(ce_oi_chg, pe_oi_chg, spot_change=spot_change)

            total_ce_oi += ce_oi
            total_pe_oi += pe_oi
            if ce_oi_chg is not None:
                total_ce_oi_chg += ce_oi_chg
            if pe_oi_chg is not None:
                total_pe_oi_chg += pe_oi_chg

            rows.append({
                "Tag": label,
                "Strike": strike,
                "CE LTP": ce_ltp,
                "CE OI": ce_oi,
                "CE OI Chg": 0 if ce_oi_chg is None else ce_oi_chg,
                "PE OI Chg": 0 if pe_oi_chg is None else pe_oi_chg,
                "PE OI": pe_oi,
                "PE LTP": pe_ltp,
                "Net Delta (PE-CE)": delta_oi,
                "Signal": f"{icon} {signal_name}",
                "Bias": bias,
                "Timestamp": now_ts
            })

        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)

        # 6-strike active writing corridor:
        # Calls: ATM, ATM+1, ATM+2 (Resistance writing zone)
        # Puts:  ATM-2, ATM-1, ATM (Support writing zone)
        call_corridor_strikes = [atm_strike, atm_strike + NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP, atm_strike + 2 * NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP]
        put_corridor_strikes  = [atm_strike - 2 * NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP, atm_strike - NIFTY_STRIKE_STEP, atm_strike]

        active_ce_oi_chg = sum(r["CE OI Chg"] for r in rows if r["Strike"] in call_corridor_strikes)
        active_pe_oi_chg = sum(r["PE OI Chg"] for r in rows if r["Strike"] in put_corridor_strikes)
        net_basket_delta = active_pe_oi_chg - active_ce_oi_chg

        overall_sig, overall_bias, overall_icon = classify_pure_oi_signal(
            active_ce_oi_chg, active_pe_oi_chg, spot_change=spot_change
        )
        # Compute Full Chain Market-Wide PCR (if optionsChain available) to avoid boundary whipsaws
        if live_chain and "optionsChain" in live_chain and live_chain["optionsChain"]:
            full_ce_oi = sum(extract_item_oi(x) for x in live_chain["optionsChain"] if x.get("option_type") == "CE")
            full_pe_oi = sum(extract_item_oi(x) for x in live_chain["optionsChain"] if x.get("option_type") == "PE")
            pcr = round(full_pe_oi / full_ce_oi, 2) if full_ce_oi > 0 else 1.0
        else:
            pcr = round(total_pe_oi / total_ce_oi, 2) if total_ce_oi else 1.0

        summary = {
            "atm_strike": atm_strike,
            "spot_price": spot_price,
            "spot_change": spot_change,
            "spot_pct": spot_pct,
            "fut_price": fut_price,
            "fut_change": fut_change,
            "fut_symbol": fut_symbol,
            "basis_diff": basis_diff,
            "basis_type": basis_type,
            "call_oi_chg": active_ce_oi_chg,
            "put_oi_chg": active_pe_oi_chg,
            "total_ce_oi_chg": active_ce_oi_chg,
            "total_pe_oi_chg": active_pe_oi_chg,
            "tracked_5_strikes_ce_oi_chg": total_ce_oi_chg,
            "tracked_5_strikes_pe_oi_chg": total_pe_oi_chg,
            "net_basket_delta": net_basket_delta,
            "overall_signal": f"{overall_icon} {overall_sig}",
            "overall_bias": overall_bias,
            "pcr": pcr,
            "timestamp": now_ts,
            "expiry_info": exp_info,
            "raw_options_chain": live_chain.get("optionsChain", []) if live_chain else []
        }

        return summary, df, shift_event

    def _get_corridor_file_path(self, symbol_name="NIFTY", target_date=None):
        if target_date is None:
            today_str = datetime.date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        elif isinstance(target_date, str):
            today_str = target_date
        elif hasattr(target_date, 'strftime'):
            today_str = target_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        else:
            today_str = str(target_date)
        sym_short = "NIFTY" if "NIFTY" in str(symbol_name).upper() else str(symbol_name)
        return DATA_DIR / f"corridor_oi_{sym_short}_{today_str}.json"

    def _seed_corridor_from_csv(self, symbol_name="NIFTY", target_date=None):
        if target_date is None:
            today_str = datetime.date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        elif isinstance(target_date, str):
            today_str = target_date
        elif hasattr(target_date, 'strftime'):
            today_str = target_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        else:
            today_str = str(target_date)
        corridor_file = self._get_corridor_file_path(symbol_name, target_date=today_str)
        csv_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_OI_{today_str}.csv"

        if corridor_file.exists():
            try:
                with open(corridor_file, "r") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    if data:
                        return data
            except Exception:
                pass

        if not csv_file.exists():
            return {}

        try:
            df_csv = pd.read_csv(csv_file)
            if df_csv.empty or "Time" not in df_csv.columns:
                return {}

            log = {}
            for t_str, group in df_csv.groupby("Time"):
                calls = {}
                puts = {}
                for _, row in group.iterrows():
                    strike_str = str(int(row["Strike"]))
                    if "CE OI" in row and pd.notna(row["CE OI"]):
                        calls[strike_str] = int(row["CE OI"])
                    if "PE OI" in row and pd.notna(row["PE OI"]):
                        puts[strike_str] = int(row["PE OI"])
                log[str(t_str)] = {
                    "call_strikes": calls,
                    "put_strikes": puts
                }

            corridor_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with open(corridor_file, "w") as f:
                json.dump(log, f, indent=2)
            return log
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error seeding corridor JSON from CSV: {e}")
            return {}

    def snapshot_corridor_oi(self, df, spot_price, symbol_name="NIFTY", tf_minutes=None):
        """Per-strike OI snapshot for the 5-strike ATM corridor, timestamped."""
        if df is None or not isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame) or df.empty or spot_price <= 0:
            return

        today_str = datetime.date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        sym_short = "NIFTY" if "NIFTY" in str(symbol_name).upper() else str(symbol_name)
        corridor_file = self._get_corridor_file_path(symbol_name)

        call_oi_by_strike = {}
        put_oi_by_strike = {}

        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            strike_val = int(row.get("Strike", row.get("strike", 0)))
            s_key = str(strike_val)
            ce_val = row.get("CE OI", row.get("ce_oi", row.get("OI", 0)))
            if pd.notna(ce_val):
                call_oi_by_strike[s_key] = int(ce_val)
            pe_val = row.get("PE OI", row.get("pe_oi", row.get("OI", 0)))
            if pd.notna(pe_val):
                put_oi_by_strike[s_key] = int(pe_val)

        ts_key = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        cache_key = f"{sym_short}_{today_str}"

        if not hasattr(self, "corridor_session_log"):
            self.corridor_session_log = {}

        if cache_key not in self.corridor_session_log or not self.corridor_session_log[cache_key]:
            self.corridor_session_log[cache_key] = self._seed_corridor_from_csv(sym_short, target_date=today_str)

        self.corridor_session_log[cache_key][ts_key] = {
            "call_strikes": call_oi_by_strike,
            "put_strikes": put_oi_by_strike,
        }

        try:
            DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            tmp_file = str(corridor_file) + ".tmp"
            with open(tmp_file, "w") as f:
                json.dump(self.corridor_session_log[cache_key], f)
            os.replace(tmp_file, corridor_file)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error persisting corridor snapshot: {e}")

    @staticmethod
    def get_bar_boundary_oi_reading(readings, boundary_time, market_open=None):
        """
        Find the OI snapshot at or immediately before `boundary_time`.
        readings: sorted list of (datetime, call_strikes_dict, put_strikes_dict)

        FIX v2: Excludes pre-market (before market_open) snapshots — same reasoning
        as before. BUT: for the very first market-open boundary (boundary_time ==
        market_open), no real snapshot will ever land at the EXACT wall-clock second
        of market_open — it'll always be a few seconds later (09:15:03, 09:15:08, etc).
        So for that specific boundary, use the EARLIEST available post-market-open
        snapshot as the baseline, instead of requiring t <= boundary_time strictly
        (which would make the 9:15 bar permanently blank, forever).
        """
        if market_open is None:
            market_open = boundary_time.replace(hour=9, minute=15, second=0, microsecond=0)

        # Filter out pre-market snapshots once
        valid_readings = [(t, c, p) for t, c, p in readings if t >= market_open]
        if not valid_readings:
            return None, None

        if boundary_time <= market_open:
            # FIX: this is the session-open boundary itself — use the earliest
            # available post-open snapshot as the baseline, not a strict t<=boundary match
            t0, c0, p0 = valid_readings[0]
            return (c0, p0)

        result = None
        for t, calls, puts in valid_readings:
            if t <= boundary_time:
                result = (calls, puts)
            else:
                break
        return result if result else (None, None)

    def get_time_bucket_oi_delta(self, timestamps, tf_minutes, symbol_name="NIFTY", target_date=None):
        """
        For each bar, computes: ΔOI = OI(bar_end_boundary) − OI(bar_start_boundary)
        E.g. 5m timeframe, bar at 9:15 → covers 9:15 to 9:20 → shows exactly how much OI
        changed in that 5-minute window, using real market-hours snapshot readings
        closest to each boundary.

        FIX: Automatically resolves target session date from timestamps so that both live sessions
        and historical past-date replays use the exact date's corridor snapshots.
        """
        if not timestamps:
            return None, None

        sym_short = "NIFTY" if "NIFTY" in str(symbol_name).upper() else str(symbol_name)

        # Standardize timestamps to timezone-naive datetimes for boundary comparisons
        std_timestamps = []
        for t in timestamps:
            if hasattr(t, "tz") and t.tz is not None:
                std_timestamps.append(t.tz_localize(None))
            elif isinstance(t, pd.Timestamp):
                std_timestamps.append(t.to_pydatetime().replace(tzinfo=None))
            elif hasattr(t, "tzinfo") and t.tzinfo is not None:
                std_timestamps.append(t.replace(tzinfo=None))
            else:
                std_timestamps.append(t)

        # Dynamically determine target session date
        if target_date is None:
            first_ts = std_timestamps[0]
            if hasattr(first_ts, "date"):
                target_date = first_ts.date()
            else:
                target_date = datetime.date.today()
        elif isinstance(target_date, str):
            try:
                target_date = datetime.date.fromisoformat(target_date)
            except Exception:
                target_date = datetime.date.today()

        today_str = target_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        cache_key = f"{sym_short}_{today_str}"

        if not hasattr(self, "corridor_session_log"):
            self.corridor_session_log = {}

        corridor_file = self._get_corridor_file_path(sym_short, target_date=today_str)
        mtime = corridor_file.stat().st_mtime if corridor_file.exists() else 0
        last_mtime = getattr(self, "_corridor_mtime", {}).get(cache_key, 0)

        if cache_key not in self.corridor_session_log or not self.corridor_session_log[cache_key] or mtime > last_mtime:
            self.corridor_session_log[cache_key] = self._seed_corridor_from_csv(sym_short, target_date=today_str)
            if not hasattr(self, "_corridor_mtime"):
                self._corridor_mtime = {}
            self._corridor_mtime[cache_key] = mtime

        log = self.corridor_session_log.get(cache_key, {})
        if not log:
            return None, None

        readings = []
        for k, v in log.items():
            try:
                t = datetime.datetime.combine(target_date, datetime.datetime.strptime(k, "%H:%M:%S").time())
                if "call_strikes" not in v or "put_strikes" not in v:
                    continue
                c_strikes = v["call_strikes"]
                p_strikes = v["put_strikes"]
                # Reject corrupted, dummy 0-OI, or simulation snapshots
                if sum(c_strikes.values()) == 0 or sum(p_strikes.values()) == 0:
                    continue
                readings.append((t, c_strikes, p_strikes))
            except Exception:
                continue
        readings.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        if not readings:
            return None, None

        n = len(timestamps)
        call_delta_lakhs = np.full(n, np.nan)
        put_delta_lakhs = np.full(n, np.nan)

        market_open = datetime.datetime.combine(target_date, datetime.time(9, 15))

        for i, bar_start in enumerate(std_timestamps):
            bar_end = bar_start + datetime.timedelta(minutes=tf_minutes)

            start_calls, start_puts = self.get_bar_boundary_oi_reading(readings, bar_start, market_open)  # FIX
            end_calls, end_puts     = self.get_bar_boundary_oi_reading(readings, bar_end, market_open)      # FIX

            if start_calls is None or end_calls is None:
                continue  # NaN — pre-market OR genuinely no post-9:15 data yet (NSE's own ~3-min feed lag)

            common_calls = [s for s in end_calls if s in start_calls]
            common_puts  = [s for s in end_puts if s in start_puts]

            if common_calls:
                call_delta = sum(end_calls[s] - start_calls[s] for s in common_calls)
                call_delta_lakhs[i] = round(call_delta / 1e5, 2)

            if common_puts:
                put_delta = sum(end_puts[s] - start_puts[s] for s in common_puts)
                put_delta_lakhs[i] = round(put_delta / 1e5, 2)

        return call_delta_lakhs, put_delta_lakhs

    def _load_today_oi_snapshots(self):
        """
        Loads all real logged snapshots from today's CSV file and aggregates
        them by timestamp with true Net Delta and PCR.

        FIX: Excludes pre-market (before 9:15 AM) rows — same reasoning as
        get_bar_boundary_oi_reading(): NSE options have no pre-open auction,
        so anything before 9:15 is the PREVIOUS DAY's closing OI, not fresh data.
        """
        today_str = datetime.date.today().isoformat()
        today_csv = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_OI_{today_str}.csv"
        if not today_csv.exists():
            return None
        try:
            df_csv = pd.read_csv(today_csv)
            if df_csv.empty or "Time" not in df_csv.columns:
                return None
            
            today_d = datetime.date.today()
            df_csv["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(
                today_d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") + " " + df_csv["Time"].astype(str),
                errors="coerce"
            )
            df_csv = df_csv.dropna(subset=["datetime"])

            # FIX: drop any row timestamped before market open (9:15 AM)
            market_open = datetime.datetime.combine(today_d, datetime.time(9, 15))
            df_csv = df_csv[df_csv["datetime"] >= market_open]

            if df_csv.empty:
                return None

            agg = df_csv.groupby("datetime").agg({
                "Net Delta (PE-CE)": "sum",
                "PE OI": "sum",
                "CE OI": "sum"
            }).reset_index()

            agg["pcr"] = np.where(agg["CE OI"] > 0, agg["PE OI"] / agg["CE OI"], 1.0)

            # Prefer Full Option Chain PCR if NIFTY_FULL_OPTIONS exists for true market-wide PCR
            full_opt_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_FULL_OPTIONS_{today_str}.csv"
            if full_opt_file.exists():
                try:
                    df_full = pd.read_csv(full_opt_file)
                    if not df_full.empty and "Time" in df_full.columns and "OI" in df_full.columns and "Option_Type" in df_full.columns:
                        df_full["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(
                            today_d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") + " " + df_full["Time"].astype(str),
                            errors="coerce"
                        )
                        df_full = df_full.dropna(subset=["datetime"])
                        p_pcr = df_full.groupby(["datetime", "Option_Type"])["OI"].sum().unstack()
                        if "PE" in p_pcr.columns and "CE" in p_pcr.columns:
                            p_pcr["pcr_full"] = np.where(p_pcr["CE"] > 0, (p_pcr["PE"] / p_pcr["CE"]).round(2), 1.0)
                            p_pcr = p_pcr.reset_index().sort_values("datetime")
                            agg = pd.merge_asof(agg.sort_values("datetime"), p_pcr[["datetime", "pcr_full"]], on="datetime", direction="backward")
                            if "pcr_full" in agg.columns:
                                agg["pcr"] = agg["pcr_full"].fillna(agg["pcr"])
                except Exception as ex_full:
                    pass

            agg = agg.sort_values("datetime")
            return agg
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error loading today's OI snapshots: {e}")
            return None

    def enrich_futures_cvd_and_buildup(self, df, summary=None, lot_size=65, tf_minutes=5):
        """
        Enriches candlestick dataframe with:
        - PineScript v5 Buy/Sell Volume split
        - Net Volume Delta & Cumulative Volume Delta (CVD)
        - ATM +/- 2 Strikes Corridor OI Delta (from true boundary-diffed snapshots or live summary)
        - Build-up & Unwinding tags (Long Build-up, Short Covering, Short Build-up, Long Unwinding)
        - Real historical bar-by-bar PCR Trend
        """
        if df.empty:
            return df

        # Safeguard: strictly deduplicate rows by epoch/datetime to prevent duplicate bars & double CVD calculation
        if "epoch" in df.columns:
            df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last").sort_values("epoch").reset_index(drop=True)
        elif "datetime" in df.columns:
            df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["datetime"], keep="last").sort_values("datetime").reset_index(drop=True)

        # Check if Futures specific columns exist, else fall back to spot/synthetic volume
        f_vols = df["fut_volume"].values.astype(float) if "fut_volume" in df.columns else df["volume"].values.astype(float)
        f_highs = df["fut_high"].values.astype(float) if "fut_high" in df.columns else df["high"].values.astype(float)
        f_lows = df["fut_low"].values.astype(float) if "fut_low" in df.columns else df["low"].values.astype(float)
        f_closes = df["fut_close"].values.astype(float) if "fut_close" in df.columns else df["close"].values.astype(float)
        closes = df["close"].values.astype(float)
        opens = df["open"].values.astype(float)

        # If futures volume is absent or non-positive, fallback to real spot volume or 0, NEVER random numbers
        if np.all(f_vols <= 0) and "volume" in df.columns:
            f_vols = df["volume"].values.astype(float)
            df["fut_volume"] = f_vols

        # PineScript v5 Intra-bar Buy/Sell Volume Split:
        # buyVol  = (high == low) ? 0 : volume * (close - low)  / (high - low)
        # sellVol = (high == low) ? 0 : volume * (high - close) / (high - low)
        hl_diff = np.maximum(1e-5, f_highs - f_lows)
        clipped_closes = np.clip(f_closes, f_lows, f_highs)
        buy_vols = np.where(f_highs == f_lows, 0.0, f_vols * (clipped_closes - f_lows) / hl_diff)
        sell_vols = np.where(f_highs == f_lows, 0.0, f_vols * (f_highs - clipped_closes) / hl_diff)
        tot_vols = buy_vols + sell_vols

        df["fut_buy_vol"] = buy_vols
        df["fut_sell_vol"] = sell_vols
        df["fut_tot_vol"] = tot_vols
        df["fut_buy_pct"] = np.where(tot_vols > 0, 100.0 * buy_vols / tot_vols, 50.0)
        df["fut_sell_pct"] = np.where(tot_vols > 0, 100.0 * sell_vols / tot_vols, 50.0)

        # 30-period SMA of volume (ta.sma(volume, 30))
        s_vol = pd.Series(f_vols)
        avg_vol = s_vol.rolling(window=30, min_periods=1).mean().values
        df["fut_avg_vol"] = avg_vol
        df["fut_vol_index"] = np.where(avg_vol > 0, f_vols / avg_vol, 1.0)

        # Mask bars that lack real Nifty Futures data — show NaN instead of spot-derived values
        if "has_real_fut" in df.columns:
            no_fut = ~df["has_real_fut"].astype(bool)
            if no_fut.any():
                df.loc[no_fut, "fut_buy_vol"] = np.nan
                df.loc[no_fut, "fut_sell_vol"] = np.nan
                df.loc[no_fut, "fut_tot_vol"] = np.nan
                df.loc[no_fut, "fut_buy_pct"] = np.nan
                df.loc[no_fut, "fut_sell_pct"] = np.nan
                df.loc[no_fut, "fut_vol_index"] = np.nan

        df["buy_vol"] = buy_vols
        df["sell_vol"] = sell_vols
        df["net_delta"] = buy_vols - sell_vols

        # Session CVD resets at the start of each daily trading session (09:15 AM)
        if "datetime" in df.columns:
            try:
                date_series = pd.to_datetime(df["datetime"]).dt.date
                df["session_cvd"] = df["net_delta"].groupby(date_series).cumsum().values
            except Exception:
                df["session_cvd"] = np.cumsum(df["net_delta"].values)
        else:
            df["session_cvd"] = np.cumsum(df["net_delta"].values)

        # 6-Strike Core Liquidity Corridor OI Change & PCR Trend
        base_delta = summary.get("net_basket_delta", 0) if summary else 0
        base_pcr = float(summary.get("pcr", 1.0)) if summary else 1.0
        n_bars = len(df)

        corridor_oi = np.full(n_bars, np.nan)
        pcr_vals = [base_pcr] * n_bars

        # 1. First attempt to compute exact bar boundary deltas from corridor JSON
        call_deltas = None
        put_deltas = None
        if "datetime" in df.columns:
            try:
                c_dt_list = pd.to_datetime(df["datetime"]).tolist()
                call_deltas, put_deltas = self.get_time_bucket_oi_delta(c_dt_list, tf_minutes, "NIFTY")
                if call_deltas is not None and not np.all(np.isnan(call_deltas)):
                    for i in range(n_bars):
                        cd = call_deltas[i]
                        pd_val = put_deltas[i]
                        if not np.isnan(cd) and not np.isnan(pd_val):
                            corridor_oi[i] = (pd_val - cd) * 100000.0
            except Exception as ex:
                print(f"Error computing time bucket OI delta in enrich: {ex}")

        # 2. Fallback to today's CSV snapshots if corridor JSON had no readings yet
        snapshots = self._load_today_oi_snapshots()
        if snapshots is not None and not snapshots.empty and "datetime" in df.columns:
            try:
                c_dt = pd.to_datetime(df["datetime"])
                if hasattr(c_dt.dt, "tz") and c_dt.dt.tz is not None:
                    c_dt = c_dt.dt.tz_localize(None)
                snap_dt = pd.to_datetime(snapshots["datetime"])
                if hasattr(snap_dt.dt, "tz") and snap_dt.dt.tz is not None:
                    snap_dt = snap_dt.dt.tz_localize(None)

                for i in range(n_bars):
                    t_bar = c_dt.iloc[i]
                    valid = snapshots[snap_dt <= t_bar]
                    if not valid.empty:
                        last_match = valid.iloc[-1]
                        if np.isnan(corridor_oi[i]):
                            corridor_oi[i] = float(last_match["Net Delta (PE-CE)"])
                        pcr_vals[i] = round(float(last_match["pcr"]), 2)
            except Exception as ex:
                print(f"Error matching snapshots to candles: {ex}")

        # Ensure the latest bar strictly uses the current live market summary
        if base_delta != 0:
            corridor_oi[-1] = float(base_delta)
        pcr_vals[-1] = round(base_pcr, 2)

        s_pcr = pd.Series(pcr_vals).ffill().bfill().fillna(base_pcr)
        df["pcr"] = s_pcr.values
        df["corridor_oi_chg"] = corridor_oi

        # Buildup Tagging & Palette (strictly based on Spot price direction & 6-strike Call/Put OI change)
        tags = []
        tags2 = []
        colors = []
        for i in range(n_bars):
            o_chg = corridor_oi[i]
            if np.isnan(o_chg):
                tags.append("—")
                tags2.append("—")
                colors.append("rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.4)")
                continue

            p_chg = closes[i] - opens[i]
            if call_deltas is not None and not np.isnan(call_deltas[i]):
                c_chg = call_deltas[i] * 100000.0
                p_oi_chg = put_deltas[i] * 100000.0
            elif i == n_bars - 1 and summary:
                c_chg = summary.get("total_ce_oi_chg", 0)
                p_oi_chg = summary.get("total_pe_oi_chg", 0)
            else:
                c_chg = -o_chg if o_chg < 0 else 0
                p_oi_chg = o_chg if o_chg > 0 else 0

            sig, bias, icon = classify_pure_oi_signal(c_chg, p_oi_chg, spot_change=p_chg)
            tags.append(sig)

            # Order Flow & 98% Trap Enhanced Signal (Signal 2)
            bp_val = df["fut_buy_pct"].iloc[i] if "fut_buy_pct" in df.columns else 50.0
            nd_val = df["net_delta"].iloc[i] if "net_delta" in df.columns else 0.0
            sig2, _, _ = classify_smart_buildup_signal(c_chg, p_oi_chg, spot_change=p_chg, fut_buy_pct=bp_val, net_delta=nd_val)
            tags2.append(sig2)

            if "Long Build-up" in sig or "Long Buildup" in sig:
                colors.append("#089981")  # Emerald Green
            elif "Short Covering" in sig:
                colors.append("#2563eb")  # Royal Blue
            elif "Short Build-up" in sig or "Short Buildup" in sig:
                colors.append("#f23645")  # Crimson Red
            elif "Long Unwinding" in sig:
                colors.append("#ffffff")  # White
            else:
                colors.append("#94a3b8")  # Slate Gray

        df["buildup_tag"] = tags
        df["buildup_tag_2"] = tags2
        df["buildup_color"] = colors

        return df

    def get_candlestick_data(self, timeframe="5m", count=50, fyers_client=None, summary=None):
        """
        Returns enriched OHLCV dataframe for NIFTY spot with full 50-pt increments on price levels,
        CVD, Buy/Sell Delta, Corridor OI Buildup tags, and PCR trend.
        """
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        minutes_step = 3 if timeframe == "3m" else (15 if timeframe == "15m" else 5)
        
        # Real historical data if Fyers available
        if fyers_client:
            try:
                res_map = {"3m": "3", "5m": "5", "15m": "15"}
                try:
                    import zoneinfo
                    ist_now = datetime.datetime.now(zoneinfo.ZoneInfo("Asia/Kolkata"))
                except Exception:
                    ist_now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc) + datetime.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)
                today_d = ist_now.date()
                now_time = ist_now.time()

                date_from = (today_d - datetime.timedelta(days=5)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                date_to = today_d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                req_data = {
                    "symbol": NIFTY_SPOT_SYMBOL,
                    "resolution": res_map.get(timeframe, "5"),
                    "date_format": "1",
                    "range_from": date_from,
                    "range_to": date_to,
                    "cont_flag": "1"
                }
                res = fyers_client.history(req_data)
                if res.get("s") == "ok" and res.get("candles"):
                    raw_candles = res["candles"]
                    df_real = pd.DataFrame(raw_candles, columns=["epoch", "open", "high", "low", "close", "volume"])
                    # Convert UTC epoch timestamp to Indian Standard Time (IST - Asia/Kolkata)
                    df_real["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(df_real["epoch"], unit="s", utc=True).dt.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata")
                    # CRITICAL FIX: Eliminate duplicate candles returned by Fyers history API
                    df_real = df_real.drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last").sort_values("epoch").reset_index(drop=True)
                    
                    # Filter for today's session starting from market open (09:15 IST)
                    df_today = df_real[df_real["datetime"].dt.date == today_d]
                    is_trading_hours = (now_time >= datetime.time(9, 15)) and (today_d.weekday() < 5)

                    # During trading hours from 09:15 AM onwards, strictly show today's intraday session (Zero Cross-Day Contamination)
                    if is_trading_hours:
                        df_use = df_today.copy()
                    elif not df_today.empty and len(df_today) >= 1:
                        df_use = df_today.copy()
                    else:
                        # Pre-market / off-hours: show recent history until 09:15 market open
                        df_use = df_real.iloc[-count:].copy()

                    df_use["time_str"] = df_use["datetime"].apply(
                        lambda d: d.strftime("%H:%M") if d.date() == today_d else d.strftime("%d %b %H:%M")
                    )

                    # Fetch real NIFTY Futures history from Fyers
                    fut_symbol = get_nifty_future_symbol(fyers_client)
                    try:
                        req_fut = {
                            "symbol": fut_symbol,
                            "resolution": res_map.get(timeframe, "5"),
                            "date_format": "1",
                            "range_from": date_from,
                            "range_to": date_to,
                            "cont_flag": "1"
                        }
                        res_fut = fyers_client.history(req_fut)
                        if res_fut.get("s") == "ok" and res_fut.get("candles"):
                            df_fut = pd.DataFrame(res_fut["candles"], columns=["epoch", "fut_open", "fut_high", "fut_low", "fut_close", "fut_volume"])
                            df_fut = df_fut.drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last").sort_values("epoch").reset_index(drop=True)
                            df_use = pd.merge(df_use, df_fut, on="epoch", how="left")
                            df_use = df_use.drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last").sort_values("epoch").reset_index(drop=True)
                            # Track which bars have REAL futures data (before any fillna)
                            df_use["has_real_fut"] = df_use["fut_volume"].notna()
                            df_use["fut_symbol"] = fut_symbol
                            # Fill OHLC for chart rendering (needed for price overlays)
                            df_use["fut_open"] = df_use["fut_open"].fillna(df_use["open"])
                            df_use["fut_high"] = df_use["fut_high"].fillna(df_use["high"])
                            df_use["fut_low"] = df_use["fut_low"].fillna(df_use["low"])
                            df_use["fut_close"] = df_use["fut_close"].fillna(df_use["close"])
                            df_use["fut_volume"] = df_use["fut_volume"].fillna(df_use["volume"])
                        else:
                            df_use["has_real_fut"] = False
                            df_use["fut_symbol"] = fut_symbol
                            df_use["fut_open"] = df_use["open"]
                            df_use["fut_high"] = df_use["high"]
                            df_use["fut_low"] = df_use["low"]
                            df_use["fut_close"] = df_use["close"]
                            df_use["fut_volume"] = df_use["volume"]
                    except Exception as ex_fut:
                        print(f"Error fetching NIFTY Futures history: {ex_fut}")
                        df_use["has_real_fut"] = False
                        df_use["fut_symbol"] = fut_symbol
                        df_use["fut_open"] = df_use["open"]
                        df_use["fut_high"] = df_use["high"]
                        df_use["fut_low"] = df_use["low"]
                        df_use["fut_close"] = df_use["close"]
                        df_use["fut_volume"] = df_use["volume"]

                    enriched = self.enrich_futures_cvd_and_buildup(df_use, summary=summary, tf_minutes=minutes_step)
                    self.cached_candles = enriched
                    return enriched
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error fetching real candlestick history: {e}")

        # 100% Pure Real Data Mode: Never generate fake synthetic candles.
        # If API momentarily blips or returns empty, retain the last known real candles.
        if hasattr(self, "cached_candles") and not self.cached_candles.empty:
            return self.cached_candles
        return pd.DataFrame()

def get_ist_now():
    """Returns current datetime strictly in Indian Standard Time (IST / Asia/Kolkata)."""
    try:
        import zoneinfo
        return datetime.datetime.now(zoneinfo.ZoneInfo("Asia/Kolkata"))
    except Exception:
        utc_now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
        return utc_now + datetime.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)

def get_ist_date():
    """Returns current date in IST."""
    return get_ist_now().date()

# Persistent Settings Manager (remembers all user settings across refreshes & relogins)
def load_settings():
    defaults = {
        "timeframe": "5m",
        "auto_refresh": True,
        "refresh_sec": 5,
        "sim_spot_manual": 24528.50
    }
    if SETTINGS_FILE.exists():
        try:
            import json
            with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "r") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                defaults.update(data)
        except Exception:
            pass
    else:
        try:
            import json
            with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "w") as f:
                json.dump(defaults, f, indent=2)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return defaults

def update_setting(key, value):
    settings = load_settings()
    settings[key] = value
    try:
        import json
        with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "w") as f:
            json.dump(settings, f, indent=2)
    except Exception:
        pass

user_settings = load_settings()

# State initialization
auth_mgr = FyersAuthManager()
storage_mgr = StorageManager()
oi_engine = OIEngine()
st.session_state.oi_engine = oi_engine

# Initialize session state with persistent settings if not already set
if "setting_auto_refresh" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["setting_auto_refresh"] = bool(user_settings.get("auto_refresh", True))

if "setting_refresh_sec" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["setting_refresh_sec"] = int(user_settings.get("refresh_sec", 15))

if "setting_timeframe" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["setting_timeframe"] = str(user_settings.get("timeframe", "5m"))

if "sim_spot_manual" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["sim_spot_manual"] = float(user_settings.get("sim_spot_manual", 24528.50))

def on_auto_refresh_change():
    update_setting("auto_refresh", st.session_state.setting_auto_refresh)

def on_refresh_sec_change():
    update_setting("refresh_sec", st.session_state.setting_refresh_sec)

def on_timeframe_change():
    update_setting("timeframe", st.session_state.setting_timeframe)

oi_engine = st.session_state.oi_engine



# --- SIDEBAR: FYERS AUTH & SETTINGS ---
with st.sidebar:
    st.markdown("""
    <div style="padding: 2px 0 10px 0; border-bottom: 1.5px solid #1e293b; margin-bottom: 14px;">
        <div style="font-size: 22px; font-weight: 900; color: #00e5ff; letter-spacing: 0.5px; text-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(0,229,255,0.45); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
            <span>⚡</span> NiftyScrubber
        </div>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

    st.markdown("### 🗓️ Session Date Selector")
    today_dt = get_ist_date()
    is_today_weekday = (today_dt.weekday() < 5)

    def get_latest_valid_trading_date(data_dir=DATA_DIR, today_ref=None) -> datetime.date:
        valid_dates = []
        if data_dir.exists():
            for f in data_dir.glob("NIFTY_OI_*.csv"):
                try:
                    date_str = f.stem.replace("NIFTY_OI_", "")
                    d = datetime.date.fromisoformat(date_str)
                    if d.weekday() < 5 and f.stat().st_size > 1000:
                        valid_dates.append(d)
                except Exception:
                    continue
        if valid_dates:
            return max(valid_dates)
        ref = today_ref if today_ref is not None else get_ist_date()
        days_back = max(1, (ref.weekday() - 4)) if ref.weekday() >= 5 else 0
        return ref - datetime.timedelta(days=days_back)

    latest_trading_dt = get_latest_valid_trading_date()

    # Determine default date: if weekend or non-trading day, strictly default to latest trading date with data
    if not is_today_weekday:
        default_session_date = latest_trading_dt
        # Auto-sanitize session state if it contains a weekend or invalid date
        curr_stored = st.session_state.get("selected_session_date")
        if curr_stored is not None and (curr_stored.weekday() >= 5 or curr_stored > latest_trading_dt):
            st.session_state["selected_session_date"] = latest_trading_dt
    else:
        default_session_date = today_dt

    selected_date = st.date_input(
        "Select Trading Session",
        value=default_session_date,
        max_value=latest_trading_dt if not is_today_weekday else today_dt,
        key="selected_session_date"
    )

    # Force clamp: if user somehow picked a weekend date, fall back to latest trading day
    if selected_date.weekday() >= 5:
        selected_date = latest_trading_dt
        st.session_state["selected_session_date"] = latest_trading_dt

    # Live market hours check (09:15 to 15:35 IST on weekdays)
    ist_now = get_ist_now()
    now_time = ist_now.time()
    is_live_market_hours = is_today_weekday and (datetime.time(9, 15) <= now_time <= datetime.time(15, 35))
    # is_today_data: True when selected_date is today (even after close, so we still load today's files)
    is_today_data = (selected_date == today_dt)
    # is_today: True only during live market hours (controls live API calls & auto-refresh)
    is_today = (selected_date == today_dt and is_live_market_hours)

    if not is_today_weekday or selected_date.weekday() >= 5:
        st.info(f"🏖️ Weekend / Holiday: {selected_date.strftime('%d-%b-%Y')}")
    elif is_today_data and not is_live_market_hours:
        st.success(f"🏁 Market Closed — Session: {selected_date.strftime('%d-%b-%Y')} (Full data available)")
    elif not is_today:
        st.info(f"📜 Replay Mode: {selected_date.strftime('%d-%b-%Y')}")

    st.markdown("---")
    st.markdown("### ⚡ FYERS API Login")
    creds = auth_mgr.get_credentials()
    auth_url = auth_mgr.get_auth_url()

    # Active Client status check
    fyers_client, conn_status = auth_mgr.get_active_client()

    # Live FYERS TOTP widget
    totp_secret = creds.get("totp_key", "").strip().replace(" ", "")
    if totp_secret:
        try:
            import pyotp
            totp_obj = pyotp.TOTP(totp_secret)
            current_totp = totp_obj.now()

            st.markdown(f"""

                    {current_totp[:3]} {current_totp[3:]}
                </div>
            </div>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Cloud Security Check: Hide all credentials and token configuration from cloud viewers
    def _is_cloud_env():
        if Path("/mount/src").exists():
            return True
        try:
            return "FYERS_CLIENT_ID" in st.secrets
        except Exception:
            return False

    is_cloud = _is_cloud_env()

    # Owner Unlock on Cloud: Allow owner to unlock credentials using their FYERS PIN (3159)
    if is_cloud:
        admin_unlocked = st.session_state.get("admin_unlocked", False)
        if admin_unlocked:
            is_cloud = False
        else:
            with st.sidebar.expander("🔐 Owner Unlock (Manage Keys)"):
                pin_attempt = st.text_input("Enter Master PIN", type="password", key="cloud_admin_pin_input")

                if pin_attempt and pin_attempt.strip() == expected_pin:
                    st.session_state["admin_unlocked"] = True
                    st.rerun()
                elif pin_attempt:

    if not is_cloud:

        <a href="{auth_url}" target="_blank" style="text-decoration:none;">
            <button style="width:100%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#00b4d8,#2563eb);color:white;border:none;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:6px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;margin-bottom:10px;">
                🔗 1-Click Authorize with FYERS
            </button>
        </a>
        """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

            client_id_input = st.text_input("App ID", value=creds.get("client_id", "WQBMFNLO92-100"))
            secret_key_input = st.text_input("Secret Key", value=creds.get("secret_key", "U3NOK2HUK0"), type="password")
            fy_id_input = st.text_input("FY_ID", value=creds.get("fy_id", "FAK76098"))

            <div style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:11px;margin-top:2px;">All credentials and keys are protected in Streamlit encrypted secrets.</div>

            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    if st.button("🔄 Sync Today's Data to Drive", width="stretch"):
