    d_color = "pos" if net_delta >= 0 else "neg"
    d_sign = "+" if net_delta >= 0 else ""
    delta_box_cls = "metric-box-spot-green" if net_delta >= 0 else "metric-box-spot-red"
    delta_text_glow = "0 0 12px rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.5)" if net_delta >= 0 else "0 0 12px rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.5)"
    delta_val_color = "#00ff88" if net_delta >= 0 else "#ff2e55"
    st.markdown(f"""
    <div class="metric-box {delta_box_cls}">
        <div class="metric-title" style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; gap:6px;">
            <span style="font-weight:800; font-size:11px; white-space:nowrap; letter-spacing:0.4px;">⚡ NET OI DELTA (PE - CE)</span>
            <span style="font-size:11px; color:#cbd5e1; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.18); padding:2px 7px; border-radius:5px; font-weight:700; white-space:nowrap;">
                6 Liquid Strikes
            </span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:baseline; gap:6px;">
            <span class="metric-value {d_color}" style="font-size:20px; text-shadow: {delta_text_glow}; line-height:1;">{d_sign}{net_delta:,}</span>
            <span style="font-size:13px; font-weight:800; font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace; color:{delta_val_color}; white-space:nowrap;">({net_delta/1e5:+.2f}L)</span>
        </div>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

with k4:
    sig_str = str(summary.get('overall_signal', ''))
    is_bull = any(x in sig_str.lower() for x in ['long build', 'bullish', 'short cov'])
    sig_box_cls = "metric-box-spot-green" if is_bull else "metric-box-spot-red"
    sig_text_glow = "0 0 12px rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.55)" if is_bull else "0 0 12px rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.55)"
    sig_text_color = "#00ff88" if is_bull else "#ff2e55"
    st.markdown(f"""
    <div class="metric-box {sig_box_cls}">
        <div class="metric-title" style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; gap:6px;">
            <span style="font-weight:800; font-size:11px; white-space:nowrap; letter-spacing:0.4px;">🧭 OVERALL BASKET SIGNAL</span>
            <span style="font-size:11px; color:#94a3b8; font-weight:700; font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace; white-space:nowrap;">
                🕒 {summary['timestamp']}
            </span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; gap:6px;">
            <span class="metric-value" style="font-size:17.5px; font-weight:800; color:{sig_text_color}; text-shadow:{sig_text_glow}; white-space:nowrap; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; line-height:1;">{summary['overall_signal']}</span>
            <span style="font-size:11px; font-weight:800; color:{sig_text_color}; background:{'rgba(0,255,136,0.12)' if is_bull else 'rgba(255,46,85,0.12)'}; border:1px solid {sig_text_color}; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:5px; text-transform:uppercase; white-space:nowrap;">{'BULLISH' if is_bull else 'BEARISH'}</span>
        </div>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

# --- EOD RADAR & NEXT-DAY OVERNIGHT BIAS WIDGET (14:30 - 15:30 EOD FLOW) ---
def render_eod_overnight_radar(selected_date, is_today, summary, oi_df=None):
    try:
        target_date_str = datetime.date.today().isoformat() if is_today else selected_date.isoformat()
        oi_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_OI_{target_date_str}.csv"
        df = None
        if oi_file.exists():
            try:
                df = pd.read_csv(oi_file)
            except Exception:
                df = None
        if (df is None or df.empty) and oi_df is not None and not oi_df.empty:
            df = oi_df.copy()
        if df is None or df.empty or "Time" not in df.columns or "Strike" not in df.columns:
            return
        df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["Time", "Strike"], keep="last")
            
        times = df["Time"].unique()
        t_latest = times[-1]
        
        t_1525_list = [t for t in times if "15:23" <= t <= "15:27"]
        if any(t >= "15:25:00" for t in times):
            t_ref = t_1525_list[-1] if t_1525_list else [t for t in times if t >= "15:25:00"][0]
        else:
            t_ref = t_latest
        
        t_1430_list = [t for t in times if "14:28" <= t <= "14:33"]
        t_1430 = t_1430_list[0] if t_1430_list else ([t for t in times if t >= "14:30:00"][0] if any(t >= "14:30:00" for t in times) else None)
        
        sub_ref = df[df["Time"] == t_ref]
        atm = int(sub_ref["ATM"].iloc[0]) if ("ATM" in sub_ref.columns and pd.notna(sub_ref["ATM"].iloc[0])) else 23250
        
        c_strikes = [atm, atm + 50, atm + 100, atm + 150]
        p_strikes = [atm - 150, atm - 100, atm - 50, atm]
        c_str = f"{c_strikes[0]}, {c_strikes[1]}, {c_strikes[2]}, {c_strikes[3]}"
        p_str = f"{p_strikes[0]}, {p_strikes[1]}, {p_strikes[2]}, {p_strikes[3]}"
        
        # Load Full Option Chain early so both Section 1 and Section 2 use the comprehensive multi-strike dataset
        full_opt_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_FULL_OPTIONS_{target_date_str}.csv"
        df_full = None
        if full_opt_file.exists():
            try:
                df_full = pd.read_csv(full_opt_file)
            except Exception:
                df_full = None
                
        fo_ref = df_full[df_full["Time"] == t_ref] if (df_full is not None and not df_full.empty and "Time" in df_full.columns) else None

        if fo_ref is not None and not fo_ref.empty and "OI_Chg" in fo_ref.columns:
            c_tot = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["OI_Chg"].sum()
            p_tot = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["OI_Chg"].sum()
        else:
            c_tot = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)]["CE OI Chg"].sum()
            p_tot = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)]["PE OI Chg"].sum()
            
        net_force = p_tot - c_tot
        
        c_tot_str = f"{c_tot/1e5:+.2f}L"
        p_tot_str = f"{p_tot/1e5:+.2f}L"
        net_str = f"{net_force/1e5:+.2f}L"
        
        c_tot_color = "#ff3b57" if c_tot >= 0 else "#00e5ff"
        p_tot_color = "#00ff88" if p_tot >= 0 else "#ffffff"
        net_color = "#00ff88" if net_force >= 0 else "#ff3b57"
        net_desc = "Put Support Dominant" if net_force >= 0 else "Call Resistance Dominant"
        
        has_closing = (t_1430 is not None and t_ref > t_1430)
        
        if has_closing:
            sub_1430 = df[df["Time"] == t_1430]
            fo_1430 = df_full[df_full["Time"] == t_1430] if (df_full is not None and not df_full.empty and "Option_Type" in df_full.columns) else None
            
            if fo_1430 is not None and not fo_1430.empty and fo_ref is not None and not fo_ref.empty:
                c_1430 = fo_1430[(fo_1430["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)) & (fo_1430["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["OI"].sum()
                c_ref = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["OI"].sum()
                p_1430 = fo_1430[(fo_1430["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)) & (fo_1430["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["OI"].sum()
                p_ref = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["OI"].sum()
            else:
                common_c = [s for s in c_strikes if s in sub_1430["Strike"].values and s in sub_ref["Strike"].values]
                common_p = [s for s in p_strikes if s in sub_1430["Strike"].values and s in sub_ref["Strike"].values]
                c_1430 = sub_1430[sub_1430["Strike"].isin(common_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
                c_ref = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(common_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
                p_1430 = sub_1430[sub_1430["Strike"].isin(common_p)]["PE OI"].sum()
                p_ref = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(common_p)]["PE OI"].sum()
            
            c_diff = c_ref - c_1430
            p_diff = p_ref - p_1430
            c_diff_color = "#ff3b57" if c_diff >= 0 else "#00e5ff"
            p_diff_color = "#00ff88" if p_diff >= 0 else "#ffffff"

            # --- 4-Quadrant Option Flow Confluence (Price vs OI) ---
            # Distinguish True Short Covering (Price RISING) from Long Liquidation (Price FALLING)
            c_price_diff, p_price_diff = 0.0, 0.0
            if fo_1430 is not None and not fo_1430.empty and fo_ref is not None and not fo_ref.empty:
                c_ltp_1430 = fo_1430[(fo_1430["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)) & (fo_1430["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["LTP"].mean()
                c_ltp_ref = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["LTP"].mean()
                c_price_diff = float(c_ltp_ref - c_ltp_1430) if (pd.notna(c_ltp_ref) and pd.notna(c_ltp_1430)) else 0.0

                p_ltp_1430 = fo_1430[(fo_1430["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)) & (fo_1430["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["LTP"].mean()
                p_ltp_ref = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["LTP"].mean()
                p_price_diff = float(p_ltp_ref - p_ltp_1430) if (pd.notna(p_ltp_ref) and pd.notna(p_ltp_1430)) else 0.0

            is_true_call_sqz = (c_diff < -20e5 and c_price_diff > 1.5 and abs(c_diff) >= 1.5 * abs(p_diff))
            is_call_long_liquidation = (c_diff < -15e5 and c_price_diff <= 0.5)

            # --- Late-Session Futures CVD Divergence Check (15:00 to Close) ---
            cvd_late_dump = False
            candles_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_5m_{target_date_str}.csv"
            if candles_file.exists():
                try:
                    df_c = pd.read_csv(candles_file).drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last")
                    if "session_cvd" in df_c.columns and "time_str" in df_c.columns and not df_c.empty:
                        c1500 = df_c[df_c["time_str"] <= "15:00"]
                        if not c1500.empty:
                            cvd_drop = float(df_c["session_cvd"].iloc[-1]) - float(c1500["session_cvd"].iloc[-1])
                            if cvd_drop < -15000:
                                cvd_late_dump = True
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # --- Full Market-Wide Option Chain Disparity & PCR Trend ---
            pcr_closing_collapse = False
            tot_pe_dump = False
            if fo_1430 is not None and not fo_1430.empty and fo_ref is not None and not fo_ref.empty:
                tot_ce_1430 = fo_1430[fo_1430["Option_Type"] == "CE"]["OI"].sum()
                tot_pe_1430 = fo_1430[fo_1430["Option_Type"] == "PE"]["OI"].sum()
                tot_ce_ref = fo_ref[fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "CE"]["OI"].sum()
                tot_pe_ref = fo_ref[fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "PE"]["OI"].sum()
                
                pcr_1430 = tot_pe_1430 / tot_ce_1430 if tot_ce_1430 > 0 else 1.0
                pcr_ref = tot_pe_ref / tot_ce_ref if tot_ce_ref > 0 else 1.0
                if (pcr_ref - pcr_1430) <= -0.04:
                    pcr_closing_collapse = True
                
                tot_pe_diff = tot_pe_ref - tot_pe_1430
                tot_ce_diff = tot_ce_ref - tot_ce_1430
                if tot_pe_diff < -80e5 and (tot_pe_diff < 1.3 * tot_ce_diff):
                    tot_pe_dump = True
            
            # --- ATM Straddle Calculation for Overnight Expected Range ---
            ref_spot = float(sub_ref["Spot"].iloc[0]) if ("Spot" in sub_ref.columns and pd.notna(sub_ref["Spot"].iloc[0])) else float(atm)
            ce_ltp, pe_ltp = 0.0, 0.0
            if df_full is not None and not df_full.empty and fo_ref is not None and not fo_ref.empty:
                ce_row = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"] == atm) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "CE")]
                pe_row = fo_ref[(fo_ref["Strike"] == atm) & (fo_ref["Option_Type"] == "PE")]
                if not ce_row.empty and pd.notna(ce_row["LTP"].iloc[0]):
                    ce_ltp = float(ce_row["LTP"].iloc[0])
                if not pe_row.empty and pd.notna(pe_row["LTP"].iloc[0]):
                    pe_ltp = float(pe_row["LTP"].iloc[0])
            if (ce_ltp == 0.0 or pe_ltp == 0.0) and "CE LTP" in sub_ref.columns and "PE LTP" in sub_ref.columns:
                atm_sub = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"] == atm]
                if not atm_sub.empty:
                    if pd.notna(atm_sub["CE LTP"].iloc[0]): ce_ltp = float(atm_sub["CE LTP"].iloc[0])
                    if pd.notna(atm_sub["PE LTP"].iloc[0]): pe_ltp = float(atm_sub["PE LTP"].iloc[0])

            straddle_prem = ce_ltp + pe_ltp
            upper_target = ref_spot + straddle_prem
            lower_target = ref_spot - straddle_prem
            
            # Dynamic tags based on Price-OI Confluence
            if c_diff >= 30e5:
                c_action_tag = "Fresh Call Writing 🧱"
            elif c_diff >= 0:
                c_action_tag = "Call Writing Added 🧱"
            elif is_true_call_sqz:
                c_action_tag = "Short Covering Squeeze 🚀"
            elif is_call_long_liquidation:
                c_action_tag = "Long Liquidation (Call Exit) ⚠️"
            else:
                c_action_tag = "MIS Trim 🛡️"

            if p_diff >= 0:
                p_action_tag = "Fresh Put Support 🟢"
            elif tot_pe_dump or p_diff <= -30e5:
                p_action_tag = "Panic Support Collapse 🚨"
            else:
                p_action_tag = "MIS Trim 🛡️"
            
            # --- Dynamic Flow Asymmetry & Closing Velocity Calculation ---
            c_spd = c_diff / (55.0 * 1e5)
            p_spd = p_diff / (55.0 * 1e5)
            net_spd = (p_diff - c_diff) / (55.0 * 1e5)

            p_unw_pct = (abs(p_diff) / p_tot * 100.0) if (p_diff < 0 and p_tot > 0) else 0.0
            c_unw_pct = (abs(c_diff) / c_tot * 100.0) if (c_diff < 0 and c_tot > 0) else 0.0

            p_ret_str = f"{100.0 - p_unw_pct:.1f}% day support retained 🛡️" if p_diff < 0 else f"+{p_diff/1e5:.1f}L fresh puts added 🟢"
            c_ret_str = f"{c_unw_pct:.1f}% day resistance cleared 💨" if c_diff < 0 else f"+{c_diff/1e5:.1f}L fresh calls added 🧱"

            if c_diff >= 0 and p_diff >= 0:
                if c_diff >= p_diff:
                    asym_ratio = c_diff / p_diff if p_diff > 0 else (c_diff / 1e5 if c_diff > 0 else 1.0)
                    asym_desc = f"Calls written {asym_ratio:.1f}x faster than Puts (Heavy Overhead Ceiling 🧱)"
                else:
                    asym_ratio = p_diff / c_diff if c_diff > 0 else (p_diff / 1e5 if p_diff > 0 else 1.0)
                    asym_desc = f"Puts written {asym_ratio:.1f}x faster than Calls (Support Base Floor 🟢)"
            elif p_diff < 0 and c_diff < 0:
                ratio_raw = abs(p_diff) / abs(c_diff) if abs(c_diff) > 0 else 1.0
                if is_call_long_liquidation and tot_pe_dump:
                    asym_ratio = ratio_raw
                    asym_desc = f"Bilateral Liquidation (Puts dumped {-p_diff/1e5:.1f}L, Calls dumped {-c_diff/1e5:.1f}L) ⚠️"
                elif ratio_raw >= 1.7:
                    asym_ratio = ratio_raw
                    asym_desc = f"Puts unwound {asym_ratio:.1f}x faster than Calls (Panic Liquidation 🚨)"
                elif (1.0 / ratio_raw) >= 1.7 and is_true_call_sqz:
                    asym_ratio = 1.0 / ratio_raw
                    asym_desc = f"Calls covered {asym_ratio:.1f}x faster than Puts (Aggressive Short Squeeze 🚀)"
                else:
                    asym_ratio = max(ratio_raw, 1.0 / ratio_raw)
                    asym_desc = f"Dual De-risking ({asym_ratio:.1f}x flow speed) | Intraday MIS Square-off ⚖️"
            elif p_diff < 0 and c_diff >= 0:
                asym_ratio = (abs(p_diff) + c_diff) / (abs(p_diff) if abs(p_diff) > 0 else 1.0)
                asym_desc = f"Puts collapsing (-{abs(p_diff)/1e5:.1f}L) while Calls expand (+{c_diff/1e5:.1f}L) 🚨"
            else:
                asym_ratio = (abs(c_diff) + p_diff) / (abs(c_diff) if abs(c_diff) > 0 else 1.0)
                asym_desc = f"Calls covering (-{abs(c_diff)/1e5:.1f}L) while Puts expand (+{p_diff/1e5:.1f}L) 🚀"

            closing_lines = [
                f'<div>• Calls Change: <b style="color:{c_diff_color};">{c_diff/1e5:+.2f}L</b> <span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:11px;">[{c_action_tag} | Speed: <b>{c_spd:+.2f}L/min</b>]</span></div>',
                f'<div>• Puts Change : <b style="color:{p_diff_color};">{p_diff/1e5:+.2f}L</b> <span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:11px;">[{p_action_tag} | Speed: <b>{p_spd:+.2f}L/min</b>]</span></div>',
                f'<div>• Flow Asymmetry: <b style="color:#facc15;">{asym_ratio:.1f}x</b> <span style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:11px;">({asym_desc})</span></div>',
                f'<div>• Net Closing Velocity: <b style="color:{"#00ff88" if net_spd >= 0 else "#ff2e55"};">{net_spd:+.2f}L/min</b> <span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:11px;">(55-min Closing Window 14:30–15:25 IST)</span></div>'
            ]
            if cvd_late_dump:
                closing_lines.append('<div style="color:#ff2e55; font-size:11px;">• Smart Money Futures: 🚨 <b>Aggressive Late Selling Detected (CVD Drained into Close)</b></div>')
            
            # Holiday & Extended Weekend Risk Verification (via market_calendar & FYERS API)
            try:
                from market_calendar import get_next_trading_day_info
                next_sess_date, gap_days, is_extended = get_next_trading_day_info(target_date_str)
                if is_extended:
                    closing_lines.append(f'<div style="color:#facc15; font-size:11px;">📅 <b>Holiday / Extended Hold Notice:</b> Next session is <b>{next_sess_date}</b> ({gap_days}-day carryover hold). Overnight gap risk is elevated.</div>')
            except Exception:
                pass
            
            # --- Comprehensive Institutional Verdict Logic ---
            net_force_eff = net_force + (p_diff - c_diff)

            # 1. Panic Unwinding Trigger (Support Collapsed into Close)
            is_panic_put_dump = (tot_pe_dump or pcr_closing_collapse or (c_diff < 0 and p_diff < 0 and p_diff < -20e5 and (abs(p_diff) >= 1.5 * abs(c_diff) or cvd_late_dump)))
            is_panic_call_sqz = is_true_call_sqz

            # 2. Fresh Writing Wall / Floor Trigger (Valid only if not crushed by contrary macro force or CVD dump)
            is_fresh_call_wall = (c_diff > 15e5 and (c_diff > 1.5 * p_diff or p_diff <= 0) and net_force_eff < 30e5)
            is_fresh_put_floor = (p_diff > 15e5 and (p_diff > 1.5 * c_diff or c_diff <= 0) and net_force_eff > -30e5 and not cvd_late_dump)

            # 3. Macro Institutional Structural Force Trigger
            # Guard: Historical net_force cannot override closing put support drainage, call liquidation, or futures dump!
            has_closing_drain = (is_panic_put_dump or cvd_late_dump or is_call_long_liquidation or (c_diff < -20e5 and p_diff < -20e5))
            is_struct_bearish = (net_force < -60e5 and net_force_eff < -30e5)
            is_struct_bullish = (net_force > 60e5 and net_force_eff > 30e5 and not has_closing_drain)

            # 4. Closing Direct Expansion / Liquidation
            is_closing_bearish = (net_force < 0 and p_diff < 0 and c_diff >= 0)
            is_closing_bullish = (net_force > 0 and c_diff < 0 and p_diff >= 0 and not is_call_long_liquidation)

            # 5. Conflicted Flow Detection (Macro Resistance but Active Closing Support Defense)
            is_defended_floor = (is_struct_bearish and p_diff > 15e5 and c_diff <= 0 and not is_panic_put_dump)
            is_capped_ceiling = (is_struct_bullish and c_diff > 15e5 and p_diff <= 0 and not is_panic_call_sqz)

            is_bearish = (is_panic_put_dump or is_fresh_call_wall or is_struct_bearish or is_closing_bearish) and not is_panic_call_sqz and not is_defended_floor
            is_bullish = (is_panic_call_sqz or is_fresh_put_floor or is_struct_bullish or is_closing_bullish) and not is_panic_put_dump and not is_capped_ceiling

            # Stage-based Verdict Header (14:30–15:25 Window vs 15:25 Final Positioning)
            now_t_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
            if (now_t_str >= "15:25:00" or not is_today):
                v_stage = "FINAL POST-CLOSE VERDICT (15:25 POSITIONING CONFIRMED)"
            else:
                v_stage = "LIVE FORMATION VERDICT (CLOSING 14:30–15:25)"

            base_overnight_move = round(straddle_prem * 0.20, 1) if straddle_prem > 0 else 35.0

            if is_defended_floor:
                verdict_anim_class = "verdict-box-orange-pulse"
                border_color = "#fb923c"
                box_shadow = "0 0 20px rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.35)"
                badge_style = "background:rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.18); border:1px solid #fb923c; color:#fb923c;"
                badge_text = "🟠 MILD GAPDOWN / DEFENDED FLOOR"
                verdict_bg = "rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.12)"
                verdict_border = "#fb923c"
                verdict_icon = "🛡️"
                verdict_title = f"{v_stage}: MILD GAPDOWN / DEFENDED SUPPORT FLOOR"
                verdict_title_color = "#fb923c"
                itm_pe = atm + 50
                straddle_target_str = (
                    f"Overnight Expected Range: <b style='color:#fb923c;'>-15 to -40 pts (Capped by {atm} Put Defense)</b> | Weekly Straddle Breakeven: ±{straddle_prem:.1f} pts"
                    f"<br>🎯 <b>ITM Option Tactical Bias:</b> Buy <b style='color:#fb923c;'>{itm_pe} PE (ITM)</b> only on sharp morning bounces | Target: <b style='color:#00ff88;'>+30 Points</b> | Stoploss: -15 pts"
                )
                verdict_desc = f"Heavy institutional Call Resistance across session ({net_force/1e5:.1f}L Net Force) caps upside, BUT closing Put defense (+{p_diff/1e5:.1f}L) and Call covering (-{abs(c_diff)/1e5:.1f}L) actively cushioned the {atm} floor. Expect modest gap-down or flat-to-weak open (-15 to -40 pts), NOT an unrestricted crash."
            elif is_capped_ceiling:
                verdict_anim_class = "verdict-box-blue-pulse"
                border_color = "#38bdf8"
                box_shadow = "0 0 20px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)"
                badge_style = "background:rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.18); border:1px solid #38bdf8; color:#38bdf8;"
                badge_text = "🔵 MILD GAP-UP / CAPPED CEILING"
                verdict_bg = "rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12)"
                verdict_border = "#38bdf8"
                verdict_icon = "🛡️"
                verdict_title = f"{v_stage}: MILD GAP-UP / CAPPED CEILING RESISTANCE"
                verdict_title_color = "#38bdf8"
                itm_ce = atm - 50
                straddle_target_str = (
                    f"Overnight Expected Range: <b style='color:#38bdf8;'>+15 to +40 pts (Capped by {atm} Call Writing)</b> | Weekly Straddle Breakeven: ±{straddle_prem:.1f} pts"
                    f"<br>🎯 <b>ITM Option Tactical Bias:</b> Buy <b style='color:#38bdf8;'>{itm_ce} CE (ITM)</b> only on morning dips | Target: <b style='color:#00ff88;'>+30 Points</b> | Stoploss: -15 pts"
                )
                verdict_desc = f"Institutional Put Support across session (+{net_force/1e5:.1f}L Net Force) establishes an underlying floor, BUT closing Call resistance (+{c_diff/1e5:.1f}L) and Put trimming (-{abs(p_diff)/1e5:.1f}L) actively cap the ceiling. Expect modest gap-up or flat-to-positive open (+15 to +40 pts)."
            elif is_bearish and not is_bullish:
                verdict_anim_class = "verdict-box-red-pulse"
                border_color = "#ff2e55"
                box_shadow = "0 0 20px rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.35)"
                badge_style = "background:rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.18); border:1px solid #ff2e55; color:#ff2e55;"
                badge_text = "🔴 HIGH GAPDOWN / WEAKNESS BIAS"
                verdict_bg = "rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.12)"
                verdict_border = "#ff2e55"
                verdict_icon = "🚨"
                verdict_title = f"{v_stage}: HIGH PROBABILITY GAPDOWN / WEAKNESS"
                verdict_title_color = "#ff2e55"
                itm_pe = atm + 50
                if is_panic_put_dump:
                    panic_min = round(max(base_overnight_move * 1.25, 55.0), 0)
                    panic_max = round(max(base_overnight_move * 1.85, 90.0), 0)
                    overnight_range_str = f"-{panic_min:.0f} to -{panic_max:.0f} pts (Support Collapsed / Futures Dumped 🚨)"
                    verdict_desc = f"Put writers aggressively liquidated support into the close (-{abs(p_diff)/1e5:.1f}L cut, {asym_ratio:.1f}x faster than Calls) while overhead Call resistance held firm. High risk to carry overnight longs (CE / Buy positions)."
                else:
                    overnight_range_str = f"-{base_overnight_move:.0f} to -{base_overnight_move*1.5:.0f} pts"
                    verdict_desc = f"Aggressive institutional Call dumping ({c_diff/1e5:+.1f}L fresh Calls vs {p_diff/1e5:+.1f}L Puts) established a massive overhead ceiling into the close. Strong overnight downside pressure / gapdown bias."
                straddle_target_str = (
                    f"Overnight Expected Range: <b style='color:#ff2e55;'>{overnight_range_str}</b> | Weekly Straddle Breakeven: ±{straddle_prem:.1f} pts"
                    f"<br>🎯 <b>ITM Option Recommendation:</b> Buy <b style='color:#ff2e55;'>{itm_pe} PE (ITM)</b> | Target: <b style='color:#00ff88;'>+30 Points Profit</b> | Stoploss: -15 pts"
                )
            elif is_bullish and not is_bearish:
                verdict_anim_class = "verdict-box-green-pulse"
                border_color = "#00ff88"
                box_shadow = "0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.35)"
                badge_style = "background:rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.18); border:1px solid #00ff88; color:#00ff88;"
                badge_text = "🟢 HIGH GAP-UP POTENTIAL"
                verdict_bg = "rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.12)"
                verdict_border = "#00ff88"
                verdict_icon = "🚀"
                verdict_title = f"{v_stage}: HIGH PROBABILITY GAP-UP / MOMENTUM"
                verdict_title_color = "#00ff88"
                itm_ce = atm - 50
                overnight_range_str = f"+{base_overnight_move:.0f} to +{base_overnight_move*1.6:.0f} pts (Bull Momentum 🚀)"
                straddle_target_str = (
                    f"Overnight Expected Range: <b style='color:#00ff88;'>{overnight_range_str}</b> | Weekly Straddle Breakeven: ±{straddle_prem:.1f} pts"
                    f"<br>🎯 <b>ITM Option Recommendation:</b> Buy <b style='color:#00ff88;'>{itm_ce} CE (ITM)</b> | Target: <b style='color:#00ff88;'>+30 Points Profit</b> | Stoploss: -15 pts"
                )
                if is_panic_call_sqz:
                    verdict_desc = f"Call writers aggressively covered overhead resistance into the close (-{abs(c_diff)/1e5:.1f}L exit, {asym_ratio:.1f}x faster than Puts) triggering an active short squeeze. Favorable setup for overnight bulls."
                elif is_struct_bullish:
                    verdict_desc = f"Heavy institutional Put Support across session (+{net_force/1e5:.1f}L Net Force) established a rock solid base into the close (Effective Net Force: +{net_force_eff/1e5:.1f}L). Favorable overnight gap-up bias."
                elif p_diff > 0:
                    verdict_desc = f"Heavy institutional Put writing floor ({p_diff/1e5:+.1f}L fresh Puts vs {c_diff/1e5:+.1f}L Calls) established a solid defensive base into the close. Favorable overnight gap-up bias."
                else:
                    verdict_desc = f"Call writers covered overhead resistance (-{abs(c_diff)/1e5:.1f}L exit) while put support held rock-solid across the session (+{net_force/1e5:.1f}L Net Force). Favorable setup for overnight bulls."
            else:
                verdict_anim_class = "verdict-box-gold-pulse"
                border_color = "#facc15"
                box_shadow = "0 0 16px rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.25)"
                badge_style = "background:rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.15); border:1px solid #facc15; color:#facc15;"
                badge_text = "⚪ BALANCED / NEUTRAL"
                verdict_bg = "rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.08)"
                verdict_border = "#facc15"
                verdict_icon = "⚖️"
                verdict_title = f"{v_stage}: BALANCED SETUP / FLAT OPEN"
                verdict_title_color = "#facc15"
                if straddle_prem > 0:
                    straddle_target_str = f"Overnight Straddle Implied Range: <b style='color:#facc15;'>±{straddle_prem:.1f} pts</b> | Range: <b style='color:#fff;'>{lower_target:.1f} – {upper_target:.1f}</b> (Avoid Overnight Option Buying)"
                else:
                    straddle_target_str = ""
                verdict_desc = "Both call and put writers maintained symmetric positions into the close. Expected flat or rangebound opening."
        else:
            verdict_anim_class = "verdict-box-blue-pulse"
            border_color = "rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.5)"
            box_shadow = "0 0 16px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.2)"
            badge_style = "background:rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.12); border:1px solid #00e5ff; color:#00e5ff;"
            badge_text = "🕒 INTRADAY MODE (14:30 LOCK)"
            closing_lines = [
                '<div style="color:#cbd5e1;">• Status: <b style="color:#00e5ff;">Active Intraday Monitoring</b></div>',
                '<div style="color:#94a3b8;">• Baseline: Locks automatically at 14:30 IST</div>',
                '<div style="color:#94a3b8;">• EOD Flow: Activates during the 14:30–15:25 closing window</div>'
            ]
            
        h = []
        h.append('<div class="cyber-box" style="background:#080d16; border:1.8px solid #00e5ff; animation:rgbCyanBorderFlow 4.5s linear infinite; border-radius:8px; padding:11px 15px; margin: 8px 0 12px 0;">')
        h.append('<div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px; margin-bottom:9px; border-bottom:1px solid rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.2); padding-bottom:7px;">')
        h.append('<div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:7px;">')
        h.append('<span style="font-size:15px;">⚡</span>')
        h.append('<span class="rgb-cyan-title-gradient" style="font-weight:900; font-size:13px; letter-spacing:0.4px; text-transform:uppercase;">EOD RADAR & NEXT-DAY OVERNIGHT BIAS (14:30 – 15:25 EOD FLOW)</span>')
        h.append('</div>')
        h.append('<div>')
        h.append(f'<span style="padding:3px 11px; border-radius:14px; font-size:11px; font-weight:700; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; {badge_style}">{badge_text}</span>')
        h.append('</div>')
        h.append('</div>')
        
        h.append('<div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr)); gap:10px; margin-bottom:4px;">')
        h.append('<div class="cyber-sub-box" style="background:#0b1120; border:1px solid #1e293b; border-radius:6px; padding:9px 12px;">')
        h.append('<div style="color:#38bdf8; font-size:11px; font-weight:700; text-transform:uppercase; margin-bottom:5px; letter-spacing:0.3px;">📊 1. Full Session Structure (ATM ± 3 Corridor)</div>')
        h.append('<div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:4px; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; font-size:11.5px;">')
        h.append(f'<div>• Calls Total : <b style="color:{c_tot_color};">{c_tot_str}</b> Writing 🧱 <span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10.5px;">({c_str} CE)</span></div>')
        h.append(f'<div>• Puts Total  : <b style="color:{p_tot_color};">{p_tot_str}</b> Writing 🟢 <span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10.5px;">({p_str} PE)</span></div>')
        h.append(f'<div>• Net Force   : <b style="color:{net_color};">{net_str}</b> <span style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:10.5px;">({net_desc})</span></div>')
        h.append('</div></div>')
        
        h.append('<div class="cyber-sub-box" style="background:#0b1120; border:1px solid #1e293b; border-radius:6px; padding:9px 12px;">')
        h.append('<div style="color:#38bdf8; font-size:11px; font-weight:700; text-transform:uppercase; margin-bottom:5px; letter-spacing:0.3px;">⚡ 2. Closing Window Shift (14:30 to 15:25 EOD)</div>')
        h.append('<div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:4px; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; font-size:11.5px;">')
        h.extend(closing_lines)
        h.append('</div></div></div>')
        
        if has_closing:
            h.append(f'<div class="{verdict_anim_class}" style="background:{verdict_bg}; border:1.8px solid {verdict_border}; border-radius:8px; padding:10px 14px; margin-top:8px; display:flex; align-items:flex-start; gap:11px;">')
            h.append(f'<span style="font-size:18px; line-height:1.2;">{verdict_icon}</span>')
            h.append('<div style="flex:1;">')
            h.append(f'<div style="color:{verdict_title_color}; font-weight:800; font-size:12px; letter-spacing:0.3px; text-transform:uppercase;">{verdict_title}</div>')
            h.append(f'<div style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:11.5px; margin-top:2px; line-height:1.35;">{verdict_desc}</div>')
            if straddle_target_str:
                h.append(f'<div style="margin-top:6px; padding-top:6px; border-top:1px dashed rgba(255,255,255,0.12); font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; font-size:11px; color:#94a3b8;">🎯 {straddle_target_str}</div>')
            
            # --- Optional ML Quant Ensemble Prediction (LightGBM + CatBoost) ---
            try:
                from ml_engine import NiftyMLEngine
                ml_eng = NiftyMLEngine()
                ml_res = ml_eng.predict_latest(target_date_str)
                if ml_res:
                    ml_bias = ml_res.get("bias", "")
                    ml_conf = ml_res.get("confidence", 0.0)
                    ml_eng_name = ml_res.get("engine", "LightGBM + CatBoost")
                    ml_badge_color = "#00ff88" if "GAP-UP" in ml_bias else ("#ff2e55" if "GAP-DOWN" in ml_bias else "#facc15")
                    h.append(f'<div style="margin-top:5px; padding-top:5px; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; font-size:10.5px; color:#94a3b8;">'
                             f'🤖 <b style="color:#00e5ff;">AI Quant Ensemble ({ml_eng_name}):</b> '
                             f'<span style="color:{ml_badge_color}; font-weight:700;">{ml_bias} ({ml_conf:.1f}% Conviction)</span>'
                             f'</div>')
            except Exception:
                pass
            h.append('</div></div>')
        h.append('</div>')
        
        st.markdown("".join(h), unsafe_allow_html=True)
    except Exception as ex_radar:
        st.caption(f"EOD Radar status: Initializing feed ({ex_radar})")

def render_intraday_action_matrix(selected_date, is_today, summary, oi_df=None):
    """
    Renders a dedicated separate card showing Live 15-Minute Dynamic Action Matrix:
    Immediate Resistance, Immediate Support, Rolling 15-Min Flow & Drain Asymmetry, and Tactical Playbook.
    """
    try:
        global DATA_DIR
        oi_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_OI_{selected_date.isoformat()}.csv"
        if not oi_file.exists():
            all_oi = sorted(DATA_DIR.glob("NIFTY_OI_*.csv"))
            if all_oi:
                oi_file = all_oi[-1]
            else:
                return
            
        df = pd.read_csv(oi_file)
        if df.empty or "Time" not in df.columns or "Strike" not in df.columns:
            return
        df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["Time", "Strike"], keep="last")
            
        all_times = sorted(df["Time"].unique().tolist())
        market_times = [t for t in all_times if "09:15:00" <= t <= "15:35:00"]
        if market_times:
            all_times = market_times
        if not all_times:
            return

        # Build 15-minute standard slots from market open
        max_time = all_times[-1]
        slot_list = []
        
        # 1. Market Open Baseline Slot (09:15)
        cand_open = [t for t in all_times if t <= "09:15:59"]
        t_open = cand_open[-1] if cand_open else all_times[0]
        slot_list.append({
            'label': '09:15 (Open)',
            'start_t': t_open,
            'end_t': t_open,
            'candle_code': '09:15',
            'is_live': False,
            'type': 'open_baseline',
            'window_title': '09:15 AM (Market Open Baseline)',
            'tag_text': '🏁 OPEN BASELINE',
            'tag_color': '#38bdf8',
            'tag_bg': 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.15)',
            'desc': 'Market opening baseline reference snapshot at 09:15 AM before the first 15-minute candle formed.'
        })
        
        # 2. Iterate standard 15m intervals throughout session
        h = 9
        m = 15
        while (h < 15) or (h == 15 and m < 30):
            start_str = f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}"
            m_next = m + 15
            h_next = h
            if m_next >= 60:
                h_next += 1
                m_next = 0
            end_str = f"{h_next:02d}:{m_next:02d}"
            
            end_window_end = f"{end_str}:59"
            start_window_end = f"{start_str}:59"
            
            if max_time >= f"{end_str}:00":
                # Completed 15m bar
                c_start = [t for t in all_times if t <= start_window_end]
                c_end = [t for t in all_times if t <= end_window_end]
                if c_start and c_end:
                    slot_list.append({
                        'label': f'{start_str}–{end_str}',
                        'start_t': c_start[-1],
                        'end_t': c_end[-1],
                        'candle_code': start_str,
                        'is_live': False,
                        'type': 'closed_bar',
                        'window_title': f'{start_str} ➔ {end_str} (Closed 15m Bar)',
                        'tag_text': '✅ CLOSED & CONFIRMED',
                        'tag_color': '#00ff88',
                        'tag_bg': 'rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.15)',
                        'desc': f'Finalized 15-minute Option Chain shift, volume asymmetry, and order flow from {start_str} to {end_str}.'
                    })
            elif max_time >= f"{start_str}:00":
                # Current forming live bar
                c_start = [t for t in all_times if t <= start_window_end]
                if c_start:
                    live_label = f'🔴 LIVE [{start_str}–Now]' if is_today else f'🏁 {start_str}–{end_str} (Day Close)'
                    slot_list.append({
                        'label': live_label,
                        'start_t': c_start[-1],
                        'end_t': max_time,
                        'candle_code': start_str,
                        'is_live': is_today,
                        'type': 'live_bar' if is_today else 'closed_bar',
                        'window_title': f'{start_str} ➔ {end_str} (Live In-Progress Bar)' if is_today else f'{start_str} ➔ {end_str} (Session Close)',
                        'tag_text': '🔴 LIVE FORMING BAR' if is_today else '🏁 SESSION CLOSE',
                        'tag_color': '#ff2e55' if is_today else '#facc15',
                        'tag_bg': 'rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.15)' if is_today else 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.15)',
                        'desc': f'Tracking real-time accumulating Option Chain & Order Flow for the active {start_str}–{end_str} candle. Updates every minute.' if is_today else f'Session close finalized data up to {end_str}.'
                    })
                break
            h = h_next
            m = m_next

        if not is_today and slot_list and not slot_list[-1]['is_live']:
            slot_list[-1]['tag_text'] = '🏁 DAY CLOSE'
            slot_list[-1]['tag_color'] = '#facc15'
            slot_list[-1]['tag_bg'] = 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.15)'

        time_labels = [s['label'] for s in slot_list]
        slot_map = {s['label']: s for s in slot_list}

        # Prefer Full Option Chain if available to prevent boundary strike whipsaws during ATM shifts
        full_opt_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_FULL_OPTIONS_{selected_date.isoformat()}.csv"
        if not full_opt_file.exists():
            all_fo = sorted(DATA_DIR.glob("NIFTY_FULL_OPTIONS_*.csv"))
            if all_fo:
                full_opt_file = all_fo[-1]
        df_full = None
        if full_opt_file.exists():
            try:
                df_full = pd.read_csv(full_opt_file)
            except Exception:
                df_full = None

        # 100% Strict Synchronization: Precompute Tactical Card Flow & Asymmetry for all slots
        tactical_flow_map = {}
        _f15_sync = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_15m_{selected_date.isoformat()}.csv"
        _df15_sync = pd.read_csv(_f15_sync) if _f15_sync.exists() else pd.DataFrame()

        for s_item in slot_list:
            cc = s_item.get('candle_code')
            if s_item.get('type') == 'open_baseline':
                tactical_flow_map[cc] = "🏁 SESSION OPEN"
                continue
            _tr = s_item.get('end_t')
            _tb = s_item.get('start_t')
            _sub_r = df[df['Time'] == _tr]
            _atm = int(_sub_r['ATM'].iloc[0]) if (not _sub_r.empty and 'ATM' in _sub_r.columns and pd.notna(_sub_r['ATM'].iloc[0])) else int(summary.get("atm_strike", 22750))
            _c_stks = [_atm, _atm + 50, _atm + 100]
            _p_stks = [_atm - 100, _atm - 50, _atm]
            
            if df_full is not None and not df_full.empty and "Option_Type" in df_full.columns:
                _fb = df_full[df_full['Time'] == _tb]
                _fc = df_full[df_full['Time'] == _tr]
                _cb = _fb[(_fb['Strike'].isin(_c_stks)) & (_fb['Option_Type'] == 'CE')]['OI'].sum()
                _cc = _fc[(_fc['Strike'].isin(_c_stks)) & (_fc['Option_Type'] == 'CE')]['OI'].sum()
                _pb = _fb[(_fb['Strike'].isin(_p_stks)) & (_fb['Option_Type'] == 'PE')]['OI'].sum()
                _pc = _fc[(_fc['Strike'].isin(_p_stks)) & (_fc['Option_Type'] == 'PE')]['OI'].sum()
            else:
                _sb = df[df['Time'] == _tb]
                _com_c = [s for s in _c_stks if s in _sb['Strike'].values and s in _sub_r['Strike'].values]
                _com_p = [s for s in _p_stks if s in _sb['Strike'].values and s in _sub_r['Strike'].values]
                _cb = _sb[_sb['Strike'].isin(_com_c)]['CE OI'].sum()
                _cc = _sub_r[_sub_r['Strike'].isin(_com_c)]['CE OI'].sum()
                _pb = _sb[_sb['Strike'].isin(_com_p)]['PE OI'].sum()
                _pc = _sub_r[_sub_r['Strike'].isin(_com_p)]['PE OI'].sum()
            
            _dce = _cc - _cb
            _dpe = _pc - _pb
            _net = _dpe - _dce
            if abs(_dce) > 0 and abs(_dpe) > 0:
                _asy = abs(_dce) / abs(_dpe) if abs(_dce) >= abs(_dpe) else abs(_dpe) / abs(_dce)
            else:
                _asy = 1.0
            
            # Check 15m candle trap (Bull Trap / Bear Trap priority override)
            _s2_item = ""
            _bp_item = 50.0
            if not _df15_sync.empty and "time_str" in _df15_sync.columns:
                _m_row = _df15_sync[_df15_sync["time_str"] == cc]
                if not _m_row.empty:
                    _s2_item = str(_m_row.iloc[-1].get("buildup_tag_2", ""))
                    _bp_item = float(_m_row.iloc[-1].get("fut_buy_pct", 50.0))

            if "Bull Trap" in _s2_item:
                _sp_item = 100.0 - _bp_item
                tactical_flow_map[cc] = f"⚡ {_sp_item:.1f}% BULL TRAP"
            elif "Bear Trap" in _s2_item:
                tactical_flow_map[cc] = f"⚡ {_bp_item:.1f}% BEAR TRAP"
            elif _asy > 2.0 and _net > 0:
                tactical_flow_map[cc] = f"🚀 {_asy:.1f}x SHORT SQUEEZE"
            elif _asy > 2.0 and _net < 0:
                tactical_flow_map[cc] = f"🚨 {_asy:.1f}x BEARISH DUMP"
            else:
                tactical_flow_map[cc] = f"⚖️ {_asy:.1f}x SYMMETRICAL FLOW"
        st.session_state["tactical_15m_flow_map"] = tactical_flow_map

        # CSS to style the bordered Streamlit container as our cyberpunk Action Matrix box with Vivid Cyan RGB Animation
        st.markdown('''<style>
            @keyframes rgbCyanBorderFlow {
                0% {
                    border-color: #00e5ff;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.50), inset 0 0 8px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.12);
                }
                25% {
                    border-color: #38bdf8;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.55), inset 0 0 10px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.15);
                }
                50% {
                    border-color: #00ff88;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.55), inset 0 0 10px rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.15);
                }
                75% {
                    border-color: #3b82f6;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.50), inset 0 0 8px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.12);
                }
                100% {
                    border-color: #00e5ff;
                    box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.50), inset 0 0 8px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.12);
                }
            }

            @keyframes rgbCyanTextShift {
                0% { background-position: 0% 50%; }
                50% { background-position: 100% 50%; }
                100% { background-position: 0% 50%; }
            }

            .rgb-cyan-title-gradient {
                background: linear-gradient(90deg, #00e5ff, #38bdf8, #00ff88, #60a5fa, #00e5ff);
                background-size: 300% 100%;
                -webkit-background-clip: text !important;
                -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent !important;
                animation: rgbCyanTextShift 4s ease infinite;
                font-weight: 900;
                letter-spacing: 0.4px;
                text-transform: uppercase;
                display: inline-block;
            }

            div.stVerticalBlock div.stVerticalBlock:has(.action-matrix-inner),
            div[data-testid="stVerticalBlock"] div[data-testid="stVerticalBlock"]:has(.action-matrix-inner) {
                background: #080d16 !important;
                border: 1.8px solid #00e5ff !important;
                border-radius: 8px !important;
                animation: rgbCyanBorderFlow 4.5s linear infinite !important;
                box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.50) !important;
                padding: 10px 14px !important;
                margin: 8px 0 12px 0 !important;
                transition: all 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1) !important;
            }
            div.stVerticalBlock div.stVerticalBlock:has(.action-matrix-inner):hover,
            div[data-testid="stVerticalBlock"] div[data-testid="stVerticalBlock"]:has(.action-matrix-inner):hover {
                box-shadow: 0 0 26px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.80), inset 0 0 12px rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.25) !important;
                transform: translateY(-1px) !important;
            }
        </style>''', unsafe_allow_html=True)

        # Single bordered container: matrix HTML + scrubber slider all inside one box
        action_box = st.container(border=True)
        with action_box:
            matrix_placeholder = st.empty()

        slider_key = f"action_matrix_time_{selected_date.isoformat()}"
        default_val = time_labels[-1]
        if slider_key in st.session_state and st.session_state[slider_key] in time_labels:
            default_val = st.session_state[slider_key]

        if len(time_labels) > 1:
            with action_box:
                st.markdown(
                    '<div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-top:6px; margin-bottom:2px; padding:0 4px;">'
                    '<div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:7px;">'
                    '<span style="font-size:15px;">⏱️</span>'
                    '<span class="rgb-cyan-title-gradient" style="font-weight:800; font-size:12px; letter-spacing:0.5px;">15-Min Scrubber (Bar-by-Bar Replay & Live Feed)</span>'
                    '</div>'
                    '<span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10.5px; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace;">Slide to inspect any completed 15m candle or track LIVE feed</span>'
                    '</div>',
                    unsafe_allow_html=True
                )
                selected_slot = st.select_slider(
                    "Select 15-Min Bar to Inspect / Replay",
                    options=time_labels,
                    value=default_val,
                    key=slider_key,
                    label_visibility="collapsed"
                )
        else:
            selected_slot = time_labels[-1]

        cur_slot = slot_map.get(selected_slot, slot_list[-1])
        is_replaying_history = not cur_slot['is_live']
        t_ref = cur_slot['end_t']
        t_base_15m = cur_slot['start_t']
        sub_ref = df[df["Time"] == t_ref]
        
        atm_strike = int(sub_ref["ATM"].iloc[0]) if ("ATM" in sub_ref.columns and pd.notna(sub_ref["ATM"].iloc[0])) else int(summary.get("atm_strike", 23100))
        c_strikes = [atm_strike, atm_strike + 50, atm_strike + 100]
        p_strikes = [atm_strike - 100, atm_strike - 50, atm_strike]

        sub_15m = df[df["Time"] == t_base_15m]
        if df_full is not None and not df_full.empty and "Option_Type" in df_full.columns:
            fo_base = df_full[df_full["Time"] == t_base_15m]
            fo_curr = df_full[df_full["Time"] == t_ref]
            if not fo_base.empty and not fo_curr.empty:
                c_base = fo_base[(fo_base["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)) & (fo_base["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["OI"].sum()
                c_curr = fo_curr[(fo_curr["Strike"].isin(c_strikes)) & (fo_curr["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["OI"].sum()
                p_base = fo_base[(fo_base["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)) & (fo_base["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["OI"].sum()
                p_curr = fo_curr[(fo_curr["Strike"].isin(p_strikes)) & (fo_curr["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["OI"].sum()
            else:
                common_c = [s for s in c_strikes if s in sub_15m["Strike"].values and s in sub_ref["Strike"].values]
                common_p = [s for s in p_strikes if s in sub_15m["Strike"].values and s in sub_ref["Strike"].values]
                c_base = sub_15m[sub_15m["Strike"].isin(common_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
                c_curr = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(common_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
                p_base = sub_15m[sub_15m["Strike"].isin(common_p)]["PE OI"].sum()
                p_curr = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(common_p)]["PE OI"].sum()
        else:
            common_c = [s for s in c_strikes if s in sub_15m["Strike"].values and s in sub_ref["Strike"].values]
            common_p = [s for s in p_strikes if s in sub_15m["Strike"].values and s in sub_ref["Strike"].values]
            c_base = sub_15m[sub_15m["Strike"].isin(common_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
            c_curr = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(common_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
            p_base = sub_15m[sub_15m["Strike"].isin(common_p)]["PE OI"].sum()
            p_curr = sub_ref[sub_ref["Strike"].isin(common_p)]["PE OI"].sum()
        
        d_ce_15m = c_curr - c_base
        d_pe_15m = p_curr - p_base
        net_15m = d_pe_15m - d_ce_15m
        
        if abs(d_ce_15m) > 0 and abs(d_pe_15m) > 0:
            asym_val = abs(d_ce_15m) / abs(d_pe_15m) if abs(d_ce_15m) >= abs(d_pe_15m) else abs(d_pe_15m) / abs(d_ce_15m)
        else:
            asym_val = 1.0
            
        d_ce_color = "#ff3b57" if d_ce_15m >= 0 else "#00e5ff"
        d_pe_color = "#00ff88" if d_pe_15m >= 0 else "#ffffff"
        
        # Dynamic S/R zones from recent 5m candles relative to current spot price
        c5_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_5m_{selected_date.isoformat()}.csv"
        if not c5_file.exists():
            all_c5 = sorted(DATA_DIR.glob("NIFTY_CANDLES_5m_*.csv"))
            if all_c5:
                c5_file = all_c5[-1]
        spot_curr = float(sub_ref["Spot"].iloc[0]) if ("Spot" in sub_ref.columns and pd.notna(sub_ref["Spot"].iloc[0])) else 0.0
        if spot_curr <= 0:
            spot_curr = float(summary.get("spot_price", 0))
        if spot_curr <= 0:
            spot_curr = float(atm_strike)
            
        res_str = f"{atm_strike + 15:.0f} – {atm_strike + 50:.0f}"
        sup_str = f"{atm_strike - 50:.0f} – {atm_strike - 15:.0f}"
        res_desc = "Overhead Resistance Ceiling & Call Cluster"
        sup_desc = f"Active Intraday Floor & {atm_strike} PE Strike Base"
        tactical_setup = "Monitor reaction at strike boundaries"
        
        if c5_file.exists():
            try:
                df5_temp = pd.read_csv(c5_file)
                if not df5_temp.empty and "low" in df5_temp.columns and "high" in df5_temp.columns:
                    # Filter candles strictly up to selected timestamp to eliminate lookahead bias
                    if is_replaying_history and "time_str" in df5_temp.columns:
                        t_cutoff = cur_slot.get('end_t', selected_slot)[:5]
                        df5_hist = df5_temp[df5_temp["time_str"] <= t_cutoff]
                        if not df5_hist.empty:
                            df5_temp = df5_hist
                    day_high = round(float(df5_temp["high"].max()), 0)
                    day_low = round(float(df5_temp["low"].min()), 0)
                    recent_c = df5_temp.tail(12)
                    
                    # 1. Dynamic Resistance: MUST be strictly ABOVE current spot
                    higher_candles = df5_temp[df5_temp["high"] > (spot_curr + 2)]
                    if not higher_candles.empty:
                        recent_highs = higher_candles.tail(6)["high"]
                        near_res = round(float(recent_highs.min()), 0)
                        far_res = round(float(recent_highs.max()), 0)
                        if near_res == far_res:
                            far_res = max(far_res, day_high)
                        if near_res > far_res:
                            near_res, far_res = far_res, near_res
                        if near_res <= spot_curr:
                            near_res = round(spot_curr + 5, 0)
                        res_str = f"{near_res:.0f} – {far_res:.0f}"
                        res_desc = "Recent Swing High & Session Day High Pivot" if far_res >= day_high else "Session Resistance Ceiling & Call Cluster"
                    else:
                        res_str = f"{day_high:.0f} – {day_high + 25:.0f}"
                        res_desc = "Session Day High Breakout Zone"
                        
                    # 2. Dynamic Support: MUST be strictly BELOW current spot
                    lower_candles = df5_temp[df5_temp["low"] < (spot_curr - 2)]
                    if not lower_candles.empty:
                        recent_lows = lower_candles.tail(6)["low"]
                        near_sup = round(float(recent_lows.max()), 0)
                        far_sup = round(float(recent_lows.min()), 0)
                        if near_sup < far_sup:
                            near_sup, far_sup = far_sup, near_sup
                        if near_sup >= spot_curr:
                            near_sup = round(spot_curr - 5, 0)
                        sup_str = f"{far_sup:.0f} – {near_sup:.0f}"
                        sup_desc = f"Active Floor ({near_sup:.0f}) & Frontline Put Base"
                    else:
                        sup_str = f"{day_low:.0f} – {day_low + 20:.0f}"
                        sup_desc = f"Active Day Low ({day_low:.0f}) Support Base"
            except Exception:
                pass
                
        try:
            res_boundary = float(res_str.replace('–', '-').split('-')[0].strip())
            sup_boundary = float(sup_str.replace('–', '-').split('-')[-1].strip())

            # Read buildup_tag_2 from 15m file — Bear/Bull Trap overrides ALL other playbook logic
            _is_bear_trap_playbook = False
            _is_bull_trap_playbook = False
            _bp_15_playbook = 0.0
            try:
                _td_str_pb = selected_date.isoformat() if not is_today_data else datetime.date.today().isoformat()
                _f15_pb = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_15m_{_td_str_pb}.csv"
                if _f15_pb.exists():
                    _df15_pb = pd.read_csv(_f15_pb)
                    _cc_pb = cur_slot.get('candle_code', t_ref[:5])
                    _mp_pb = _cc_pb if (is_replaying_history and _cc_pb in _df15_pb["time_str"].values) else t_ref[:5]
                    _matched_pb = _df15_pb[_df15_pb["time_str"] <= _mp_pb]
                    if not _matched_pb.empty:
                        _s2_pb = str(_matched_pb.iloc[-1].get("buildup_tag_2", ""))
                        _bp_15_playbook = float(_matched_pb.iloc[-1].get("fut_buy_pct", 0.0))
                        _is_bear_trap_playbook = "Bear Trap" in _s2_pb
                        _is_bull_trap_playbook = "Bull Trap" in _s2_pb
            except Exception:
                pass

            if _is_bear_trap_playbook:
                tactical_setup = f"⚡ Bear Trap Active — Call Writers Trapped! Buy Pullbacks into Support ({sup_str}) | Squeeze Target: {res_str}"
            elif _is_bull_trap_playbook:
                tactical_setup = f"⚡ Bull Trap Active — Put Writers Trapped! Sell Rallies into Resistance ({res_str}) | Collapse Target: {sup_str}"
            elif asym_val > 1.8 and net_15m > 0:
                tactical_setup = f"Bullish Flow & Short Squeeze Active | Buy Dips into Support ({sup_str}) | Target: {res_str}"
            elif asym_val > 1.8 and net_15m < 0:
                tactical_setup = f"Bearish Flow & Heavy Call Dump | Sell Rallies into Resistance ({res_str}) | Downside: {sup_str}"
            elif spot_curr >= (res_boundary - 6):
                tactical_setup = f"Testing Resistance Zone ({res_str}) | Watch for Breakout or Exhaustion Pullback"
            elif spot_curr <= (sup_boundary + 6):
                tactical_setup = f"Testing Support Floor ({sup_str}) | Watch for Absorption Bounce"
            else:
                tactical_setup = f"Range Consolidation | Support Floor at {sup_str} | Resistance Ceiling at {res_str}"
        except Exception:
            tactical_setup = f"Range Consolidation | Support Floor at {sup_str} | Resistance Ceiling at {res_str}"
            _is_bear_trap_playbook = False
            _is_bull_trap_playbook = False
            _bp_15_playbook = 0.0

            
        # Badge — Bear/Bull Trap overrides the net_15m-based badge too
        badge_style = "background:rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.12); border:1px solid #00e5ff; color:#00e5ff;"
        if cur_slot.get('type') == 'open_baseline':
            badge_text = "🏁 SESSION OPEN"
            badge_style = "background:rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.18); border:1px solid #38bdf8; color:#38bdf8;"
            tactical_setup = f"Market Opening Baseline at 09:15 AM | Spot Open: {spot_curr:,.2f} | Support: {sup_str} | Resistance: {res_str}"
        elif _is_bear_trap_playbook:
            badge_text = f"⚡ {_bp_15_playbook:.1f}% BEAR TRAP" if _bp_15_playbook > 0 else "⚡ BEAR TRAP DETECTED"
            badge_style = "background:rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.18); border:1px solid #f59e0b; color:#fde047;"
        elif _is_bull_trap_playbook:
            _sp_15_playbook = 100.0 - _bp_15_playbook
            badge_text = f"⚡ {_sp_15_playbook:.1f}% BULL TRAP" if _bp_15_playbook > 0 else "⚡ BULL TRAP DETECTED"
            badge_style = "background:rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.18); border:1px solid #ff2e55; color:#ff2e55;"
        elif asym_val > 2.0 and net_15m < 0:
            badge_text = f"🚨 {asym_val:.1f}x BEARISH DUMP"
            badge_style = "background:rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.18); border:1px solid #ff2e55; color:#ff2e55;"
        elif asym_val > 2.0 and net_15m > 0:
            badge_text = f"🚀 {asym_val:.1f}x SHORT SQUEEZE"
            badge_style = "background:rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.18); border:1px solid #00ff88; color:#00ff88;"
        else:
            badge_text = f"⚖️ {asym_val:.1f}x SYMMETRICAL FLOW"

        h = []
        h.append('<div class="action-matrix-inner">')
        h.append('<div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px; margin-bottom:9px; border-bottom:1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2); padding-bottom:7px;">')
        h.append('<div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:7px;">')
        h.append('<span style="font-size:15px;">💡</span>')
        h.append('<span class="rgb-cyan-title-gradient" style="font-size:13px; font-weight:900; letter-spacing:0.4px; text-transform:uppercase;">15-MIN INTRADAY TRADING IDEA & ACTION MATRIX</span>')
        h.append('<span class="rgb-beacon"></span>')
        h.append('</div>')
        h.append('<div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px;">')
        if is_replaying_history:
            h.append(f'<span style="padding:3px 10px; border-radius:14px; font-size:10.5px; font-weight:700; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; background:rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.18); border:1px solid #facc15; color:#facc15;">🕒 REPLAY: {selected_slot}</span>')
        else:
            live_label_text = "🔴 LIVE BAR" if is_today else "🏁 CLOSE"
            live_color_code = "#00ff88" if is_today else "#38bdf8"
            h.append(f'<span style="padding:3px 10px; border-radius:14px; font-size:10.5px; font-weight:700; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; background:rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12); border:1px solid {live_color_code}; color:{live_color_code};">{live_label_text}</span>')
        h.append(f'<span style="padding:3px 11px; border-radius:14px; font-size:11px; font-weight:700; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; {badge_style}">{badge_text}</span>')
        h.append('</div>')
        h.append('</div>')

        # High-Visibility Active Timeframe Explainer Banner
        window_title = cur_slot.get('window_title', selected_slot)
        t_tag_text = cur_slot.get('tag_text', '15M BAR')
        t_tag_color = cur_slot.get('tag_color', '#00e5ff')
        t_tag_bg = cur_slot.get('tag_bg', 'rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.15)')
        t_desc_text = cur_slot.get('desc', '')
        
        h.append(
f'<div style="background:#070d18; border:1px solid {t_tag_color}; border-radius:6px; padding:8px 12px; margin-bottom:10px; box-shadow:0 0 10px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.08);">'
'<div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px;">'
'<div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; flex-wrap:wrap;">'
'<span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10.5px; font-weight:700; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:0.5px;">Active Window:</span>'
f'<span style="color:#ffffff; font-size:14px; font-weight:900; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace;">{window_title}</span>'
f'<span style="background:{t_tag_bg}; border:1px solid {t_tag_color}; color:{t_tag_color}; font-size:10px; font-weight:800; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:10px; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace;">{t_tag_text}</span>'
'</div>'
f'<div style="font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; font-size:11px; color:#cbd5e1;">'

'</div>'
'</div>'
f'<div style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10.5px; margin-top:3px; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace;">'
f'{t_desc_text}'
'</div>'
'</div>'
        )
        
        # Check for Extreme 98% Institutional Trap strictly on 15-Min Candle
        try:
            target_d_str = selected_date.isoformat() if not is_today_data else datetime.date.today().isoformat()
            f15_path = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_15m_{target_d_str}.csv"
            if f15_path.exists():
                df15_cur = pd.read_csv(f15_path)
                candle_code = cur_slot.get('candle_code', t_ref[:5])
