                df15_matched = df15_cur[df15_cur["time_str"] <= match_prefix]
                if not df15_matched.empty:
                    bar15 = df15_matched.iloc[-1]
                    s2_15 = str(bar15.get("buildup_tag_2", ""))
                    bp_15 = float(bar15.get("fut_buy_pct", 50.0))
                    tot_bv_15 = float(bar15.get("fut_buy_vol", 0.0))
                    tot_sv_15 = float(bar15.get("fut_sell_vol", 0.0))
                    nd_15 = float(bar15.get("net_delta", tot_bv_15 - tot_sv_15))
                    p_diff_15 = float(bar15.get("close", 0)) - float(bar15.get("open", 0))

                    # Only show squeeze/breakdown radar when OI engine EXPLICITLY flags Bear Trap / Bull Trap.
                    # Never trigger on generic high-buy or high-sell bars (those may be clean Long/Short Buildups).
                    if "Bear Trap" in s2_15:
                        h.append(
'<div style="background:#090d16; border:1.5px solid #f59e0b; border-radius:8px; padding:10px 14px; margin-bottom:11px; box-shadow:0 0 16px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25);">'
'<div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:6px; margin-bottom:5px;">'
f'<span style="color:#fde047; font-weight:900; font-size:12px; letter-spacing:0.5px;">⚡ INSTITUTIONAL SQUEEZE RADAR (EXTREME {bp_15:.1f}% BUY TRAP DETECTED)</span>'
'<span style="background:#78350f; color:#fde047; border:1px solid #f59e0b; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:12px; font-size:10px; font-weight:800;">CALL SQUEEZE ALERT</span>'
'</div>'
f'<div style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:11.5px; line-height:1.45; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace;">'
f'• <b>Order Flow:</b> <span style="color:#00ff88; font-weight:700;">{bp_15:.1f}% Aggressive Buy Volume</span> ({tot_bv_15:,.0f} contracts | Net Delta: <b>{nd_15:+,.0f}</b>)<br>'
f'• <b>OI Anomaly:</b> {"Call Short Buildup" if d_ce_15m >= 0 else "Call Short Covering"} (<b>CE: {d_ce_15m/1e5:+.2f}L</b>) + {"Put OI Adding" if d_pe_15m >= 0 else "Put OI Unwinding"} (<b>PE: {d_pe_15m/1e5:+.2f}L</b>) diverging with Price ({"+" if p_diff_15 >= 0 else ""}{p_diff_15:.2f} pts)<br>'
f'• <b>Action:</b> <span style="color:#fde047; font-weight:800;">DO NOT SHORT! Trapped Call Writers facing massive squeeze. Target Breakout: {res_str}</span>'
'</div>'
'</div>'
                        )
                    elif "Bull Trap" in s2_15:
                        sp_15 = 100.0 - bp_15
                        h.append(
'<div style="background:#090d16; border:1.5px solid #ff3b57; border-radius:8px; padding:10px 14px; margin-bottom:11px; box-shadow:0 0 16px rgba(255, 59, 87, 0.25);">'
'<div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:6px; margin-bottom:5px;">'
f'<span style="color:#ff3b57; font-weight:900; font-size:12px; letter-spacing:0.5px;">⚡ INSTITUTIONAL BREAKDOWN RADAR (EXTREME {sp_15:.1f}% SELL TRAP DETECTED)</span>'
'<span style="background:#4c0519; color:#ff3b57; border:1px solid #ff3b57; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:12px; font-size:10px; font-weight:800;">LONG LIQUIDATION ALERT</span>'
'</div>'
f'<div style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:11.5px; line-height:1.45; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace;">'
f'• <b>Order Flow:</b> <span style="color:#ff3b57; font-weight:700;">{sp_15:.1f}% Aggressive Sell Volume</span> ({tot_sv_15:,.0f} contracts | Net Delta: <b>{nd_15:+,.0f}</b>)<br>'
f'• <b>OI Anomaly:</b> Retail Long Buildup (<b>{net_15m/1e5:+.2f}L</b>) Diverging with Falling Price ({p_diff_15:.2f} pts)<br>'
f'• <b>Action:</b> <span style="color:#ff3b57; font-weight:800;">DO NOT BUY! Trapped Put Writers facing support collapse. Target Breakdown: {sup_str}</span>'
'</div>'
'</div>'
                        )
        except Exception:
            pass

        h.append('<div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr)); gap:10px;">')
        
        # Box 1: Dynamic Resistance
        h.append('<div class="cyber-box-red" style="background:#0b1120; border:1px solid rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.3); border-radius:6px; padding:7px 12px;">')
        h.append('<div style="color:#ff3b57; font-size:14px; font-weight:900; text-transform:uppercase; margin-bottom:3px; letter-spacing:0.4px;">🛡️ Immediate Resistance (Ceiling)</div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="font-size:23px; font-weight:900; color:#ff3b57; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; margin-bottom:2px; letter-spacing:0.5px;">{res_str}</div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:13px; font-weight:600;">{res_desc}</div>')
        h.append('</div>')
        
        # Box 2: Dynamic Support
        h.append('<div class="cyber-box-green" style="background:#0b1120; border:1px solid rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.3); border-radius:6px; padding:7px 12px;">')
        h.append('<div style="color:#00ff88; font-size:14px; font-weight:900; text-transform:uppercase; margin-bottom:3px; letter-spacing:0.4px;">🟢 Immediate Support (Floor)</div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="font-size:23px; font-weight:900; color:#00ff88; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; margin-bottom:2px; letter-spacing:0.5px;">{sup_str}</div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:13px; font-weight:600;">{sup_desc}</div>')
        h.append('</div>')
        
        # Box 3: 15-Min Flow & Asymmetry
        h.append('<div class="cyber-sub-box" style="background:#0b1120; border:1.2px solid #00e5ff; box-shadow:0 0 10px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.25); border-radius:6px; padding:7px 12px;">')
        h.append('<div style="color:#00e5ff; font-size:14px; font-weight:900; text-transform:uppercase; margin-bottom:3px; letter-spacing:0.4px;">⚡ 15-Min Flow & Asymmetry</div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="font-size:16px; font-weight:800; color:#f8fafc; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; margin-bottom:2px;">Calls: <span style="color:{d_ce_color};">{d_ce_15m/1e5:+.2f}L</span> | Puts: <span style="color:{d_pe_color};">{d_pe_15m/1e5:+.2f}L</span></div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="color:#facc15; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:800;">Asymmetry: {asym_val:.2f}x <span style="color:#cbd5e1; font-weight:600; font-size:13px;">(Net: {net_15m/1e5:+.2f}L)</span></div>')
        h.append('</div>')
        
        # Box 4: Tactical Action Playbook (15-Min Trading Idea)
        h.append('<div class="cyber-box-gold" style="background:#0b1120; border:1px solid rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.3); border-radius:6px; padding:7px 12px;">')
        h.append('<div style="color:#facc15; font-size:14px; font-weight:900; text-transform:uppercase; margin-bottom:3px; letter-spacing:0.4px;">🎯 15-Min Trading Idea & Action Playbook</div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="font-size:15px; font-weight:800; color:#f1f5f9; line-height:1.4; margin-bottom:2px;">{tactical_setup}</div>')
        h.append(f'<div style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:12.5px; font-weight:500;">Window: {t_base_15m[:5]}–{t_ref[:5]} (15-Min Bar Sync)</div>')
        h.append('</div>')

        # Box 5: AI Quant Multi-Timeframe Confluence (LightGBM + CatBoost)
        try:
            from ml_engine import NiftyMLEngine
            ml_eng = NiftyMLEngine()
            target_date_str = selected_date.isoformat()
            as_of_arg = cur_slot.get('end_t', selected_slot)[:5] if is_replaying_history else None
            ml_intra = ml_eng.predict_latest_intraday(target_date_str, as_of_time=as_of_arg)
            if not ml_intra:
                # If target date has no candles (e.g. weekend or early morning), fallback to latest settled
                all_recorded = sorted(DATA_DIR.glob("NIFTY_OI_*.csv"))
                if all_recorded:
                    last_valid_d = all_recorded[-1].stem.replace("NIFTY_OI_", "")
                    ml_intra = ml_eng.predict_latest_intraday(last_valid_d, as_of_time=as_of_arg)

            if ml_intra:
                sig = ml_intra.get("signal", "⚪ WAIT / HOLD")
                conf = float(ml_intra.get("confidence", 50.0))
                trade = ml_intra.get("itm_trade")
                probs = ml_intra.get("probabilities", {})
                
                # Robust fallback for probabilities if empty
                p_buy = float(probs.get("BUY", 0.0))
                p_sell = float(probs.get("SELL", 0.0))
                p_hold = float(probs.get("HOLD", 0.0))
                if p_buy == 0.0 and p_sell == 0.0 and p_hold == 0.0:
                    if "BUY" in sig:
                        p_buy, p_sell, p_hold = conf, round((100.0 - conf) * 0.3, 1), round((100.0 - conf) * 0.7, 1)
                    elif "SELL" in sig:
                        p_buy, p_sell, p_hold = round((100.0 - conf) * 0.3, 1), conf, round((100.0 - conf) * 0.7, 1)
                    else:
                        rem = round((100.0 - conf) / 2.0, 1)
                        p_buy, p_sell, p_hold = rem, rem, conf

                sig_color = "#00ff88" if "BUY" in sig else ("#ff2e55" if "SELL" in sig else "#facc15")

                trade_html = ""
                if trade:
                    trade_html = (
                        f'<div style="color:#ffffff; font-size:11px; margin-top:2px; font-weight:700;">'
                        f'🎯 {trade["action"]}: <span style="color:{sig_color};">{trade["strike"]}</span></div>'
                        f'<div style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10px;">Target: <b style="color:#00ff88;">{trade["target_gain"]}</b> | '
                        f'SL: {trade["stoploss"]} | Spot: {trade["target_spot_move"]} ({trade.get("risk_reward", "1:2")})</div>'
                    )
                else:
                    trade_html = '<div style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10.5px; margin-top:2px;">Consolidation / Noise Zone — Awaiting high conviction breakout.</div>'

                # High-Visibility Probability Metric Chips
                chips_html = (
                    f'<div style="margin-top:8px; margin-bottom:4px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; gap:6px; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace;">'
                    f'<div style="background:#061a12; border:1px solid rgba(0,255,136,0.35); border-radius:5px; padding:4px 6px; flex:1; text-align:center;">'
                    f'<span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:11px; font-weight:700;">Buy </span><b style="color:#00ff88; font-size:15px; font-weight:900;">{p_buy:.0f}%</b>'
                    f'</div>'
                    f'<div style="background:#1a1705; border:1px solid rgba(250,204,21,0.35); border-radius:5px; padding:4px 6px; flex:1; text-align:center;">'
                    f'<span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:11px; font-weight:700;">Hold </span><b style="color:#facc15; font-size:15px; font-weight:900;">{p_hold:.0f}%</b>'
                    f'</div>'
                    f'<div style="background:#1f090d; border:1px solid rgba(255,46,85,0.35); border-radius:5px; padding:4px 6px; flex:1; text-align:center;">'
                    f'<span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:11px; font-weight:700;">Sell </span><b style="color:#ff2e55; font-size:15px; font-weight:900;">{p_sell:.0f}%</b>'
                    f'</div>'
                    f'</div>'
                )

                # Probability RGB Segmented Bar
                prob_bar_html = (
                    f'<div style="width:100%; height:5px; background:#1e293b; border-radius:3px; margin-top:4px; display:flex; overflow:hidden;">'
                    f'<div style="width:{p_buy:.1f}%; background:#00ff88;" title="Buy: {p_buy:.1f}%"></div>'
                    f'<div style="width:{p_hold:.1f}%; background:#facc15;" title="Hold: {p_hold:.1f}%"></div>'
                    f'<div style="width:{p_sell:.1f}%; background:#ff2e55;" title="Sell: {p_sell:.1f}%"></div>'
                    f'</div>'
                )

                quant_title = f"🤖 AI Quant Signal [{selected_slot}]" if is_replaying_history else "🤖 AI Quant Signal (15m Macro Multi-TF)"
                h.append('<div class="cyber-box-rgb">')
                h.append('<div style="display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; margin-bottom:4px;">')
                h.append(f'<span class="rgb-title-gradient" style="font-size:11px;">{quant_title}</span>')
                h.append('<span class="rgb-beacon"></span>')
                h.append('</div>')
                h.append(f'<div style="font-size:14px; font-weight:800; color:{sig_color}; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; margin-bottom:2px;">{sig} <span style="font-size:11px; color:#cbd5e1;">({conf:.1f}% Conviction)</span></div>')
                h.append(trade_html)
                h.append(chips_html)
                h.append(prob_bar_html)
                h.append('</div>')
        except Exception:
            pass

        h.append('</div>')
        matrix_placeholder.markdown("".join(h), unsafe_allow_html=True)
    except Exception as ex_matrix:
        st.error(f"⚠️ Action Matrix error: {ex_matrix}")

# Render EOD Radar Widget directly below Top KPI cards
render_eod_overnight_radar(selected_date=selected_date, is_today=is_today_data, summary=summary, oi_df=oi_df)
render_intraday_action_matrix(selected_date=selected_date, is_today=is_today, summary=summary, oi_df=oi_df)
# Dedicated Collapsible Panel: 💥 GAMMA BLAST DETECTOR
with st.expander("💥 GAMMA BLAST DETECTOR (OI Velocity & Traded Volume Matrix)", expanded=True):
    render_gamma_blast_panel(selected_date=selected_date, is_today=is_today, summary=summary, oi_df=oi_df, fyers_client=fyers_client)

b_col1, b_col2, b_col3 = st.columns([1.5, 1.8, 4.2])
with b_col1:
    st.markdown("## 🔬 BETA Panel")
with b_col2:
    timeframe = st.radio(
        "Timeframe",
        options=["3m", "5m", "15m"],
        key="setting_timeframe",
        horizontal=True,
        label_visibility="collapsed",
        on_change=on_timeframe_change
    )
with b_col3:
    leg_html = [
        '<div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px; align-items:center; justify-content:flex-end; padding-top:6px;">',
        '<span class="legend-pill legend-pill-green"><b>🟢 Up:</b> Put Writing</span>',
        '<span class="legend-pill legend-pill-red"><b>🔴 Down:</b> Call Writing</span>',
        '<span class="legend-pill legend-pill-blue"><b>🔵 Blue:</b> Call Exit</span>',
        '<span class="legend-pill legend-pill-white"><b>⚪ White:</b> Put Exit</span>',
        '<span class="legend-pill legend-pill-gold"><b>🟡 Diamond:</b> Net ΔOI</span>',
        '</div>'
    ]
    st.markdown("".join(leg_html), unsafe_allow_html=True)



count_map = {"3m": 130, "5m": 80, "15m": 30}
candle_count = count_map.get(timeframe, 80)
chart_height = 1200

if is_today and is_live_market_hours:
    candle_df = oi_engine.get_candlestick_data(timeframe=timeframe, count=candle_count, fyers_client=fyers_client, summary=summary)
    if not candle_df.empty:
        storage_mgr.log_intraday_candles(candle_df, timeframe=timeframe)
else:
    hist_str = selected_date.isoformat()
    hist_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_{timeframe}_{hist_str}.csv"
    if not hist_file.exists():
        hist_file = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_5m_{hist_str}.csv"
    if hist_file.exists():
        try:
            candle_df = pd.read_csv(hist_file)
            if "epoch" in candle_df.columns:
                candle_df = candle_df.drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last").sort_values("epoch").reset_index(drop=True)
            elif "datetime" in candle_df.columns:
                candle_df = candle_df.drop_duplicates(subset=["datetime"], keep="last").sort_values("datetime").reset_index(drop=True)
        except Exception as ex_hist:
            st.error(f"Error loading historical session candles for {hist_str}: {ex_hist}")
            candle_df = pd.DataFrame()
    elif fyers_client is not None:
        # Fallback for Streamlit Cloud when local CSV files are not on disk: fetch directly from FYERS history API
        candle_df = oi_engine.get_candlestick_data(timeframe=timeframe, count=candle_count, fyers_client=fyers_client, summary=summary)
    else:
        st.warning(f"⚠️ No recorded session history found for {selected_date.strftime('%d-%b-%Y')}.")
        candle_df = pd.DataFrame()

if not candle_df.empty:
    # Explicitly enforce Indian Standard Time (Asia/Kolkata) on candle timestamps
    if "epoch" in candle_df.columns:
        candle_df["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(candle_df["epoch"], unit="s", utc=True).dt.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata")
    elif "datetime" in candle_df.columns:
        try:
            if hasattr(candle_df["datetime"].dt, "tz") and candle_df["datetime"].dt.tz is not None:
                candle_df["datetime"] = candle_df["datetime"].dt.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata")
            else:
                candle_df["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(candle_df["datetime"]).dt.tz_localize("UTC").dt.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata")
        except Exception:
            pass
    today_d = datetime.date.today()
    candle_df["time_str"] = candle_df["datetime"].apply(
        lambda d: d.strftime("%H:%M") if d.date() == today_d else d.strftime("%d %b %H:%M")
    )

    fut_sym = candle_df["fut_symbol"].iloc[0] if ("fut_symbol" in candle_df.columns and pd.notna(candle_df["fut_symbol"].iloc[0])) else "NIFTY FUTURES"

    # 4 Stacked Synchronized Subplots (Spot Candles + OI DATA + FUTURE VOL + SESSION CVD)
    fig_beta = make_subplots(
        rows=4, cols=1,
        shared_xaxes=True,
        vertical_spacing=0.05,
        row_heights=[0.35, 0.30, 0.18, 0.17],
        subplot_titles=[
            f"NIFTY 50 SPOT CANDLES ({timeframe.upper()})",
            "6-STRIKE CORE CORRIDOR (ATM ± 2) TIME-BUCKETED OI DATA (CALL ΔOI vs PUT ΔOI • IN LAKHS)  •  [⚠ NSE OI ~3m lag]",
            f"{fut_sym} FUTURE VOL (STACKED BUY / SELL)",
            "SESSION CUMULATIVE VOLUME DELTA (CVD)  •  +ve = BUYERS  |  -ve = SELLERS"
        ]
    )

    # PANEL 1: Spot Price Candlestick
    fig_beta.add_trace(go.Candlestick(
        x=candle_df["time_str"],
        open=candle_df["open"],
        high=candle_df["high"],
        low=candle_df["low"],
        close=candle_df["close"],
        name="NIFTY SPOT",
        increasing_line_color="#00e676",
        decreasing_line_color="#ff3b57",
        increasing_fillcolor="#00e676",
        decreasing_fillcolor="#ff3b57"
    ), row=1, col=1)

    # Panel 1: Squeeze & Trap Radar Markers (⚡) on Spot Candlestick (Strictly on 15m timeframe)
    trap_x, trap_y, trap_custom, trap_symbols, trap_colors = [], [], [], [], []
    if timeframe == "15m":
        for idx_c in range(len(candle_df)):
            s2_val = candle_df["buildup_tag_2"].iloc[idx_c] if "buildup_tag_2" in candle_df.columns else ""
            if "⚡" in str(s2_val) or "Trap" in str(s2_val):
                t_bar = candle_df["time_str"].iloc[idx_c]
                o_bar = candle_df["open"].iloc[idx_c]
                c_bar = candle_df["close"].iloc[idx_c]
                h_bar = candle_df["high"].iloc[idx_c]
                l_bar = candle_df["low"].iloc[idx_c]
                diff_bar = c_bar - o_bar
                bv_bar = candle_df["fut_buy_vol"].iloc[idx_c] if "fut_buy_vol" in candle_df.columns else 0
                sv_bar = candle_df["fut_sell_vol"].iloc[idx_c] if "fut_sell_vol" in candle_df.columns else 0
                bp_bar = candle_df["fut_buy_pct"].iloc[idx_c] if "fut_buy_pct" in candle_df.columns else 50.0
                nd_bar = candle_df["net_delta"].iloc[idx_c] if "net_delta" in candle_df.columns else (bv_bar - sv_bar)
                oi_corr = candle_df["corridor_oi_chg"].iloc[idx_c] / 1e5 if ("corridor_oi_chg" in candle_df.columns and not np.isnan(candle_df["corridor_oi_chg"].iloc[idx_c])) else 0.0

                is_bear_trap = "Bear Trap" in str(s2_val)
                y_pos = h_bar + 14 if is_bear_trap else l_bar - 14
                sym = "triangle-up" if is_bear_trap else "triangle-down"
                col = "#f59e0b" if is_bear_trap else "#ff3b57"
                sig_name = "BEAR TRAP DETECTED" if is_bear_trap else "BULL TRAP DETECTED"
                strike_focus = round(c_bar / 50) * 50
                note = f"Call Sellers trapped at {strike_focus:.0f}! Expect short squeeze." if is_bear_trap else f"Put Writers trapped at {strike_focus:.0f}! Expect breakdown."
                vol_line = f"{bv_bar:,.0f} ({bp_bar:.1f}% BUY!)" if is_bear_trap else f"{sv_bar:,.0f} ({100-bp_bar:.1f}% SELL!)"

                trap_x.append(t_bar)
                trap_y.append(y_pos)
                trap_symbols.append(sym)
                trap_colors.append(col)
                trap_custom.append([
                    f"{c_bar:,.2f} ({diff_bar:+.2f} pts)",
                    vol_line,
                    nd_bar,
                    oi_corr,
                    sig_name,
                    note
                ])

    if trap_x:
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=trap_x,
            y=trap_y,
            mode="markers+text",
            marker=dict(symbol=trap_symbols, size=13, color=trap_colors, line=dict(color="#ffffff", width=1.5)),
            text=["⚡"] * len(trap_x),
            textposition="top center",
            textfont=dict(color="#fde047", size=12, family="JetBrains Mono"),
            name="⚡ TRAP RADAR",
            customdata=trap_custom,
            hovertemplate=(
                "<b>⏰ %{x} (15-Min Bar)</b><br>"
                "⚡ <b style='color:#fde047;'>SIGNAL: %{customdata[4]}</b><br>"
                "────────────────────────────────<br>"
                "• Spot: <b>%{customdata[0]}</b><br>"
                "• Volume: <b>%{customdata[1]}</b><br>"
                "• Net Delta: <b>%{customdata[2]:+,.0f} contracts</b><br>"
                "• Retail Corridor OI: <b>%{customdata[3]:+.2f}L (Divergence)</b><br>"
                "⚠️ <b>NOTE:</b> %{customdata[5]}<extra></extra>"
            )
        ), row=1, col=1)

    cur_spot = summary["spot_price"]

    # Live Spot Price line spanning across the candles
    fig_beta.add_hline(
        y=cur_spot, line_dash="dot", line_color="rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.5)", line_width=1.2,
        row=1, col=1
    )
    # Spot badge placed strictly to the LEFT of the right Y-axis (Strike scale) inside the plot area
    fig_beta.add_annotation(
        xref="paper", yref="y1",
        x=0.995, y=cur_spot,
        xanchor="right", yanchor="middle",
        text=f"SPOT: <span style='color:#facc15; font-weight:900;'>{cur_spot:,.2f}</span>",
        showarrow=False,
        font=dict(color="#cbd5e1", size=10, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        bgcolor="rgba(10, 15, 25, 0.90)",
        bordercolor="#facc15",
        borderwidth=1.2,
        borderpad=3
    )

    min_p = candle_df["low"].min() - 40
    max_p = candle_df["high"].max() + 40
    step_y = 50

    fig_beta.update_yaxes(
        side="right",
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=10, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        showgrid=True,
        gridcolor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
        tickmode="linear",
        dtick=step_y,
        tickformat="d",
        fixedrange=False,
        autorange=True,
        row=1, col=1
    )
    # Add vertical NIFTY 50 label on the left side (matching OI DATA & FUTURE VOL)
    fig_beta.add_annotation(
        xref="paper", yref="y1",
        x=-0.045, y=(min_p + max_p) / 2,
        text="NIFTY 50",
        showarrow=False,
        textangle=-90,
        font=dict(color="#e2e8f0", size=10, family="JetBrains Mono")
    )

    # PANEL 2: 5-Strike Core Liquidity Corridor Time-Bucketed OI Data
    tf_min = 5
    if timeframe == "3m":
        tf_min = 3
    elif timeframe == "15m":
        tf_min = 15

    raw_timestamps = pd.to_datetime(candle_df["datetime"]).tolist()
    call_oi_chg_lakhs, put_oi_chg_lakhs = oi_engine.get_time_bucket_oi_delta(raw_timestamps, tf_min, "NIFTY")

    if call_oi_chg_lakhs is not None and not np.all(np.isnan(call_oi_chg_lakhs)):
        # Above 0: Put ΔOI (Green when adding, Orange when unwinding)
        y_put = [v if not np.isnan(v) else 0 for v in put_oi_chg_lakhs]
        bar_texts_put  = [f"{v:+.2f}L" if not np.isnan(v) else "" for v in put_oi_chg_lakhs]

        # Below 0: Call ΔOI (Inverted: Call adding goes DOWN in Red, Call covering goes UP in Cyan)
        y_call_down = [-v if not np.isnan(v) else 0 for v in call_oi_chg_lakhs]
        bar_texts_call = [f"{v:+.2f}L" if not np.isnan(v) else "" for v in call_oi_chg_lakhs]

        # Unified Single Bar: Put ΔOI (UP / Above 0)
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Bar(
            x=candle_df["time_str"],
            y=y_put,
            name="Put ΔOI (Up)",
            marker_color=["#089981" if v >= 0 else "#ffffff" if not np.isnan(v) else "rgba(0,0,0,0)" for v in put_oi_chg_lakhs],
            text=bar_texts_put,
            textposition="outside",
            textfont=dict(color="#00ff88", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
            customdata=candle_df["buildup_tag"],
            hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>Put ΔOI: <b>%{y:+.2f}L</b><br>Buildup: <b>%{customdata}</b><extra></extra>"
        ), row=2, col=1)

        # Unified Single Bar: Call ΔOI (DOWN / Below 0)
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Bar(
            x=candle_df["time_str"],
            y=y_call_down,
            name="Call ΔOI (Down)",
            marker_color=["#f23645" if v >= 0 else "#2563eb" if not np.isnan(v) else "rgba(0,0,0,0)" for v in call_oi_chg_lakhs],
            text=bar_texts_call,
            textposition="outside",
            textfont=dict(color="#ff3b57", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
            customdata=np.column_stack((call_oi_chg_lakhs, candle_df["buildup_tag"])),
            hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>Call ΔOI: <b>%{customdata[0]:+.2f}L</b><br>Buildup: <b>%{customdata[1]}</b><extra></extra>"
        ), row=2, col=1)

        # Net ΔOI (PE - CE) as a synchronized diamond marker line
        net_oi_lakhs = [
            (p - c) if (not np.isnan(p) and not np.isnan(c)) else np.nan
            for p, c in zip(put_oi_chg_lakhs, call_oi_chg_lakhs)
        ]
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=candle_df["time_str"],
            y=net_oi_lakhs,
            name="Net ΔOI (PE-CE)",
            mode="lines+markers",
            line=dict(color="#facc15", width=1.5, dash="dot"),
            marker=dict(size=5, color="#facc15", symbol="diamond"),
            hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>Net ΔOI (PE-CE): <b>%{y:+.2f}L</b><extra></extra>"
        ), row=2, col=1)
    else:
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Bar(
            x=candle_df["time_str"],
            y=candle_df["corridor_oi_chg"] / 1e5,
            marker_color=candle_df["buildup_color"],
            name="Corridor ΔOI (L)",
            text=candle_df["buildup_tag"],
            textposition="outside",
            hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>Corridor ΔOI: %{y:+.2f}L<br>Status: %{text}<extra></extra>"
        ), row=2, col=1)

    fig_beta.add_hline(y=0, line_color="rgba(255,255,255,0.2)", line_width=1, row=2, col=1)

    # --- Fixed Y-axis range for OI subplot to prevent auto-rescaling on refresh ---
    try:
        _all_oi_vals = [abs(v) for v in y_put if v != 0] + [abs(v) for v in y_call_down if v != 0]
    except NameError:
        # Fallback branch: y_put/y_call_down not defined, use corridor_oi_chg
        _all_oi_vals = [abs(v / 1e5) for v in candle_df["corridor_oi_chg"].dropna() if v != 0]
    if _all_oi_vals:
        _oi_y_max = max(_all_oi_vals) * 1.3
    else:
        _oi_y_max = 100  # fallback
    fig_beta.update_yaxes(
        side="left",
        title=dict(text="OI DATA", font=dict(color="#e2e8f0", size=10)),
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        gridcolor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
        range=[-_oi_y_max, _oi_y_max],
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        row=2, col=1
    )

    # PANEL 3: NIFTY Futures Buy / Sell Volume
    fut_buy_lakhs = candle_df["fut_buy_vol"] / 1e5 if "fut_buy_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.zeros(len(candle_df))
    fut_sell_lakhs = candle_df["fut_sell_vol"] / 1e5 if "fut_sell_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.zeros(len(candle_df))
    fut_buy_pct = candle_df["fut_buy_pct"] if "fut_buy_pct" in candle_df.columns else np.full(len(candle_df), 50.0)
    fut_sell_pct = candle_df["fut_sell_pct"] if "fut_sell_pct" in candle_df.columns else np.full(len(candle_df), 50.0)
    fut_raw_buy = candle_df["fut_buy_vol"] if "fut_buy_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.zeros(len(candle_df))
    fut_raw_sell = candle_df["fut_sell_vol"] if "fut_sell_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.zeros(len(candle_df))

    # Base layer: Sell Volume (Red)
    fig_beta.add_trace(go.Bar(
        x=candle_df["time_str"],
        y=fut_sell_lakhs,
        name="Sell Vol",
        marker=dict(color="rgba(242, 54, 69, 0.85)", line=dict(color="#f23645", width=0.8)),
        customdata=np.column_stack((fut_raw_sell, fut_sell_pct)),
        hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>Sell Vol: <b>%{customdata[0]:,.0f}</b> (%{y:.2f}L)<br>Sell %: <b>%{customdata[1]:.1f}%</b><extra></extra>"
    ), row=3, col=1)

    # Top layer: Buy Volume (Green, stacked naturally via relative barmode)
    fig_beta.add_trace(go.Bar(
        x=candle_df["time_str"],
        y=fut_buy_lakhs,
        name="Buy Vol",
        marker=dict(color="rgba(8, 153, 129, 0.85)", line=dict(color="#089981", width=0.8)),
        customdata=np.column_stack((fut_raw_buy, fut_buy_pct)),
        hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>Buy Vol: <b>%{customdata[0]:,.0f}</b> (%{y:.2f}L)<br>Buy %: <b>%{customdata[1]:.1f}%</b><extra></extra>"
    ), row=3, col=1)

    fig_beta.update_yaxes(
        side="left",
        title=dict(text="FUTURE VOL", font=dict(color="#e2e8f0", size=10)),
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        gridcolor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
        row=3, col=1
    )

    # PANEL 4: Session Cumulative Volume Delta (CVD)
    if "session_cvd" in candle_df.columns:
        cvd_vals = candle_df["session_cvd"] / 1e5  # convert to lakhs
        time_vals = candle_df["time_str"]

        # Split into positive (buyers) and negative (sellers) for dual-color fill
        cvd_pos = cvd_vals.clip(lower=0)
        cvd_neg = cvd_vals.clip(upper=0)

        # Green fill — buyers zone
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=time_vals,
            y=cvd_pos,
            name="CVD +ve (Buyers)",
            mode="lines",
            line=dict(color="#00e676", width=1.5),
            fill="tozeroy",
            fillcolor="rgba(0, 230, 118, 0.18)",
            hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>CVD: <b>%{y:.2f}L</b><extra></extra>"
        ), row=4, col=1)

        # Red fill — sellers zone
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=time_vals,
            y=cvd_neg,
            name="CVD -ve (Sellers)",
            mode="lines",
            line=dict(color="#ff3b57", width=1.5),
            fill="tozeroy",
            fillcolor="rgba(255, 59, 87, 0.18)",
            hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>CVD: <b>%{y:.2f}L</b><extra></extra>"
        ), row=4, col=1)

        # Full CVD line on top for smooth crosshair readability
        fig_beta.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=time_vals,
            y=cvd_vals,
            name="CVD Line",
            mode="lines",
            line=dict(color="#facc15", width=2.0, dash="solid"),
            hovertemplate="<b>%{x}</b><br>Session CVD: <b>%{y:.2f}L</b><extra></extra>"
        ), row=4, col=1)

        # Zero reference line
        fig_beta.add_hline(
            y=0, line_dash="dash", line_color="rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.5)", line_width=1.0,
            row=4, col=1
        )

    fig_beta.update_yaxes(
        side="left",
        title=dict(text="CVD (L)", font=dict(color="#e2e8f0", size=10)),
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        gridcolor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
        zeroline=True,
        zerolinecolor="rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.3)",
        zerolinewidth=1,
        row=4, col=1
    )

    # Time Axis Formatting (Vertical 9:15, 9:20, 9:25... below Spot Chart, Corridor, and Futures)
    step_tick = 1 if len(candle_df) <= 80 else 2
    tick_vals = candle_df["time_str"].iloc[::step_tick].tolist()
    first_time = candle_df["time_str"].iloc[0]
    last_time = candle_df["time_str"].iloc[-1]
    if first_time not in tick_vals:
        tick_vals.insert(0, first_time)
    if last_time not in tick_vals:
        tick_vals.append(last_time)

    # Common crosshair & spike settings for BOTH X (Vertical) and Y (Horizontal)
    fig_beta.update_xaxes(
        type="category",
        categoryorder="trace",
        gridcolor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
        rangeslider=dict(visible=False),
        fixedrange=False,
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        showspikes=True,
        spikemode="across+toaxis",
        spikesnap="cursor",
        spikedash="dot",
        spikethickness=1.5,
        spikecolor="#00e5ff"
    )

    fig_beta.update_yaxes(
        gridcolor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)",
        fixedrange=False,
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        showspikes=True,
        spikemode="across+toaxis",
        spikesnap="cursor",
        spikedash="dot",
        spikethickness=1.5,
        spikecolor="#00e5ff"
    )

    # 1. TIME LABELS DIRECTLY BELOW NIFTY SPOT CHART (ROW 1)
    fig_beta.update_xaxes(
        showticklabels=True,
        tickangle=-90,
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        automargin=True,
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        row=1, col=1
    )

    # 2. TIME LABELS BELOW CORRIDOR ΔOI CHART (ROW 2)
    fig_beta.update_xaxes(
        showticklabels=True,
        tickangle=-90,
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        automargin=True,
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        row=2, col=1
    )

    # 3. TIME LABELS BELOW FUTURES BUY/SELL VOLUME CHART (ROW 3)
    fig_beta.update_xaxes(
        showticklabels=True,
        tickangle=-90,
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        automargin=True,
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        row=3, col=1
    )

    # 4. TIME LABELS BELOW SESSION CVD CHART (ROW 4)
    fig_beta.update_xaxes(
        showticklabels=True,
        title=dict(text="Time (IST - Indian Standard Time)", font=dict(color="#cbd5e1", size=11)),
        tickangle=-90,
        tickfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=9, family="JetBrains Mono"),
        automargin=True,
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        row=4, col=1
    )

    fig_beta.update_layout(
        template="plotly_dark",
        paper_bgcolor="#000000",
        plot_bgcolor="#05080e",
        barmode="relative",
        bargap=0.20,
        height=chart_height,
        uirevision="nifty_terminal_persist_view",
        dragmode="pan",
        hovermode="x",
        spikedistance=1000,
        hoverdistance=100,
        margin=dict(l=75, r=85, t=75, b=80),
        legend=dict(
            orientation="h",
            yanchor="bottom",
            y=1.04,
            xanchor="left",
            x=0.01,
            font=dict(color="#ffffff", size=10, family="JetBrains Mono"),
            bgcolor="rgba(9, 12, 20, 0.75)",
            bordercolor="rgba(37, 47, 68, 0.8)",
            borderwidth=1
        ),
        modebar=dict(
            orientation="h",
            bgcolor="rgba(9, 12, 20, 0.85)",
            color="#94a3b8",
            activecolor="#00e5ff"
        )
    )

    # Ensure subplot titles are clearly readable
    for ann in fig_beta["layout"]["annotations"]:
        ann["font"] = dict(color="#94a3b8", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono")

    st.plotly_chart(
        fig_beta,
        width="stretch",
        config={
            "scrollZoom": True,
            "displayModeBar": True,
            "displaylogo": False,
            "modeBarButtonsToAdd": [
                "drawline", "drawopenpath", "drawrect", "eraseshape",
                "zoomIn2d", "zoomOut2d", "autoScale2d", "resetScale2d"
            ]
        }
    )

    @st.cache_data
    def get_html2canvas_code():
        h2c_path = Path("html2canvas.min.js")
        if h2c_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(h2c_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    return f.read()
            except Exception:
                pass
        return ""

    def render_screenshot_button_html():
        h2c_code = get_html2canvas_code()
        return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<style>
    html, body {{
        margin: 0;
        padding: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        background: transparent;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: flex-end;
        font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
        box-sizing: border-box;
    }}
    #btn-capture {{
        margin: 0;
        background: #09121f;
        border: 1.5px solid rgb(0, 229, 255);
        color: rgb(0, 229, 255);
        padding: 6px 14px;
        border-radius: 6px;
        font-size: 12px;
        font-weight: 800;
        cursor: pointer;
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 7px;
        box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.25);
        transition: all 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
        white-space: nowrap;
        user-select: none;
    }}
    #btn-capture:hover {{
        background: #0f1f33;
        box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.6);
        transform: translateY(-1px);
        border-color: #38bdf8;
    }}
    #btn-capture:active {{
        transform: scale(0.97);
    }}
    svg {{
        stroke: rgb(0, 229, 255);
        transition: stroke 0.2s ease;
    }}
    #btn-capture:hover svg {{
        stroke: #ffffff;
    }}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<button id="btn-capture" onclick="takeTableScreenshot()" title="Download plot as a png (Bar-by-Bar Table)">
    <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
        <path d="M23 19a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H3a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4l2-3h6l2 3h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2z"></path>
        <circle cx="12" cy="13" r="4"></circle>
    </svg>
    <span>Download Table as PNG</span>
</button>

<script>
{h2c_code}

function takeTableScreenshot() {{
    const parentDoc = window.parent.document;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-capture');
    const span = btn.querySelector('span');
    
    const boxes = Array.from(parentDoc.querySelectorAll('.cyan-bar-table-box'));
    const target = boxes.find(b => b.offsetParent !== null) || boxes[0];
    
    if (!target) {{
        span.innerText = 'Table not found';
        setTimeout(() => {{ span.innerText = 'Download Table as PNG'; }}, 2000);
        return;
    }}

    span.innerText = 'Capturing...';
    btn.style.opacity = '0.7';

    const is15m = target.innerText.includes('15m') || target.innerText.includes('15-Min');
    const prefix = is15m ? 'NIFTY_15M_Macro_Table' : 'NIFTY_Bar_by_Bar_Table';
    const now = new Date();
    const timeStr = now.toTimeString().split(' ')[0].replace(/:/g, '-');
    const filename = `${{prefix}}_${{timeStr}}.png`;

    html2canvas(target, {{
        backgroundColor: '#060a12',
        scale: 2,
        useCORS: true,
        logging: false,
        scrollX: 0,
        scrollY: 0
    }}).then(canvas => {{
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.download = filename;
        link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
        link.click();

        span.innerText = '✅ Saved!';
        btn.style.opacity = '1';
        setTimeout(() => {{
            span.innerText = 'Download Table as PNG';
        }}, 2000);
    }}).catch(err => {{
        console.error('Screenshot capture failed:', err);
        span.innerText = '❌ Failed';
        btn.style.opacity = '1';
        setTimeout(() => {{
            span.innerText = 'Download Table as PNG';
        }}, 2000);
    }});
}}
</script>
</body>
</html>"""

    def render_cyan_bar_table(df_table, custom_order=None, col_widths=None, font_size=12, padding_v=8, padding_h=14):
        if df_table is None or df_table.empty:
            st.info("No data available.")
            return

        if custom_order:
            ordered_cols = []
            for c in custom_order:
                if c == "Time":
                    if "Time" in df_table.columns:
                        ordered_cols.append("Time")
                    elif "Time (15m)" in df_table.columns:
                        ordered_cols.append("Time (15m)")
                elif c == "Time (15m)":
                    if "Time (15m)" in df_table.columns:
                        ordered_cols.append("Time (15m)")
                    elif "Time" in df_table.columns:
                        ordered_cols.append("Time")
                elif c in df_table.columns:
                    ordered_cols.append(c)
            if ordered_cols:
                df_table = df_table[ordered_cols]

        cols = list(df_table.columns)
        table_font = font_size or 12
        pad_v = padding_v or 8
        pad_h = padding_h or 14

        html = ['<div class="cyan-bar-table-box" style="max-height:1100px; overflow-y:auto; overflow-x:auto; border:2px solid rgb(0, 229, 255); border-radius:10px; box-shadow:0 0 24px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.45); background:#060a12; margin: 8px 0 16px 0;">']
        html.append(f'<table style="width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; text-align:left; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\', monospace; font-size:{table_font}px;">')
        html.append('<thead style="position:sticky; top:0; z-index:10;"><tr style="background:#0c1829; border-bottom:2px solid rgb(0, 229, 255);">')
        for col in cols:
            w_val = col_widths.get(col) if (col_widths and col in col_widths) else None
            w_style = f"width:{w_val}px; min-width:{w_val}px;" if w_val else ""
            html.append(f'<th style="padding:{pad_v + 2}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} color:rgb(0, 229, 255); font-weight:900; letter-spacing:0.5px; white-space:nowrap; text-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.6); position:relative;">{col}</th>')
        html.append('</tr></thead><tbody>')

        for idx, row in df_table.iterrows():
            bg = "#090f18" if idx % 2 == 0 else "#060a12"
            html.append(f'<tr style="background:{bg}; border-bottom:1px solid #142033;">')
            for col in cols:
                w_val = col_widths.get(col) if (col_widths and col in col_widths) else None
                w_style = f"width:{w_val}px; min-width:{w_val}px;" if w_val else ""
                val = str(row[col])
                color = "#cbd5e1"
                if "ΔOI" in col:
                    if "-" in val:
                        color = "rgb(0, 229, 255)" if "Call" in col else "#ffffff"
                    elif "+" in val:
                        color = "#ff3b57" if "Call" in col else "#00ff88"
                elif "Net" in col:
                    color = "#facc15"
                elif "Chg" in col:
                    color = "#00ff88" if "+" in val else ("#ff3b57" if "-" in val else "#cbd5e1")
                elif "Buy %" in col:
                    try:
                        num = float(val.replace("%", "").strip())
                        color = "#00ff88" if num >= 50 else "#ff3b57"
                    except Exception:
                        color = "#cbd5e1"
                elif col == "PCR":
                    try:
                        p_val = float(val)
                        color = "#00ff88" if p_val >= 1.0 else "#ff3b57"
                    except Exception:
                        color = "#cbd5e1"
                elif "Buildup Signal 2" in col or "Signal 2" in col:
                    if "⚡" in val or "Trap" in val:
                        badge = f'<span style="background:rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.22); border:1px solid #f59e0b; color:#fde047; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800; text-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.5);">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    elif "🟢" in val:
                        color = "#00ff88"
                    elif "🔴" in val:
                        color = "#ff3b57"
                    elif "🔵" in val:
                        color = "#00e5ff"
                    else:
                        color = "#cbd5e1"
                elif "Flow & Asymmetry" in col or "Asymmetry" in col:
                    if "⚡" in val or "TRAP" in val or "Trap" in val:
                        b_col = "#ff2e55" if ("BULL" in val or "Bull" in val) else "#fde047"
                        b_bg = "rgba(255, 46, 85, 0.18)" if ("BULL" in val or "Bull" in val) else "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.22)"
                        b_bdr = "#ff2e55" if ("BULL" in val or "Bull" in val) else "#f59e0b"
                        badge = f'<span style="background:{b_bg}; border:1px solid {b_bdr}; color:{b_col}; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800; text-shadow:0 0 8px {b_col};">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    elif "🚀" in val or "Squeeze" in val:
                        badge = f'<span style="background:rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.16); border:1px solid #00e5ff; color:#00e5ff; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800; text-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.4);">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    elif "🚨" in val or "Bear" in val or "Dump" in val:
                        badge = f'<span style="background:rgba(255, 59, 87, 0.16); border:1px solid #ff3b57; color:#ff3b57; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800; text-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(255, 59, 87, 0.4);">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    elif "⚖️" in val or "Symm" in val:
                        badge = f'<span style="background:rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.16); border:1px solid #64748b; color:#cbd5e1; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800;">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    elif "🏁" in val or "OPEN" in val:
                        badge = f'<span style="background:rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.18); border:1px solid #38bdf8; color:#38bdf8; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800;">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    else:
                        color = "#cbd5e1"
                elif "Vol Delta" in col or "Delta Lots" in col:
                    if "+" in val:
                        badge = f'<span style="background:rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.16); border:1px solid #00ff88; color:#00ff88; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; text-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(0, 255, 136, 0.4);">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    elif "-" in val:
                        badge = f'<span style="background:rgba(255, 59, 87, 0.16); border:1px solid #ff3b57; color:#ff3b57; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:800; font-family:\'JetBrains Mono\',monospace; text-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(255, 59, 87, 0.4);">{val}</span>'
                        html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{badge}</td>')
                        continue
                    else:
                        color = "#cbd5e1"
                elif "Buy Lots" in col or "Buy Vol" in col:
                    color = "#00ff88"
                elif "Sell Lots" in col or "Sell Vol" in col:
                    color = "#ff3b57"
                html.append(f'<td style="padding:{pad_v}px {pad_h}px; {w_style} color:{color}; white-space:nowrap; font-weight:700;">{val}</td>')
            html.append('</tr>')

        html.append('</tbody></table></div>')
        st.markdown("".join(html), unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # --- LOAD REAL-TIME ORDER FLOW ENGINE & BAR CACHE ---
    of_map_5m = {}
    of_map_15m = {}
    try:
        from order_flow_engine import get_order_flow_singleton
        of_engine = get_order_flow_singleton(lot_size=65)
        target_date_of = selected_date.isoformat() if not is_today_data else datetime.date.today().isoformat()
        of_map_5m = of_engine.get_5m_order_flow_map(target_date_of)
        of_map_15m = of_engine.get_15m_macro_order_flow_map(target_date_of)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[OrderFlow] In-memory load notice: {e}")

    # --- BAR-BY-BAR OI DELTA & BUILDUP TABLE ---
    table_rows = []
    n_bars = len(candle_df)
    for i in range(n_bars):
        t_str = candle_df["time_str"].iloc[i]
        c_val = candle_df["close"].iloc[i]
        o_val = candle_df["open"].iloc[i]
        p_chg = c_val - o_val
        p_sign = "+" if p_chg >= 0 else ""

        # Call and Put OI deltas
        c_oi_txt = "—"
        p_oi_txt = "—"
        net_oi_txt = "—"

        has_call = call_oi_chg_lakhs is not None and i < len(call_oi_chg_lakhs) and not np.isnan(call_oi_chg_lakhs[i])
        has_put  = put_oi_chg_lakhs is not None and i < len(put_oi_chg_lakhs) and not np.isnan(put_oi_chg_lakhs[i])

        if has_call:
            c_oi_txt = f"{call_oi_chg_lakhs[i]:+.2f}L"
        if has_put:
            p_oi_txt = f"{put_oi_chg_lakhs[i]:+.2f}L"

        if has_call and has_put:
            net_lakhs = put_oi_chg_lakhs[i] - call_oi_chg_lakhs[i]
            net_oi_txt = f"{net_lakhs:+.2f}L"
        elif not np.isnan(candle_df["corridor_oi_chg"].iloc[i]):
            net_oi_txt = f"{candle_df['corridor_oi_chg'].iloc[i] / 1e5:+.2f}L"

        # Signal 1 (Pure OI Classification - dynamically calculated from actual Call/Put deltas)
        c_val_oi = call_oi_chg_lakhs[i] * 1e5 if (has_call and not np.isnan(call_oi_chg_lakhs[i])) else 0.0
        p_val_oi = put_oi_chg_lakhs[i] * 1e5 if (has_put and not np.isnan(put_oi_chg_lakhs[i])) else 0.0
        if has_call and has_put and not np.isnan(call_oi_chg_lakhs[i]) and not np.isnan(put_oi_chg_lakhs[i]):
            sig_raw, _, icon_sig = classify_pure_oi_signal(c_val_oi, p_val_oi, spot_change=p_chg)
            sig_display = f"{icon_sig} {sig_raw}"
        else:
            sig = candle_df["buildup_tag"].iloc[i]
            if sig and sig != "—":
                if "Long Build" in sig or "Long Buildup" in sig:
                    sig_display = f"🟢 {sig}"
                elif "Short Cov" in sig:
                    sig_display = f"🔵 {sig}"
                elif "Short Build" in sig or "Short Buildup" in sig:
                    sig_display = f"🔴 {sig}"
                elif "Long Unwind" in sig:
                    sig_display = f"⚪ {sig}"
                else:
                    sig_display = f"⚪ {sig}"
            else:
                sig_display = "—"

        # Futures Volume Metrics — base from candle_df
        bv_raw = candle_df["fut_buy_vol"].iloc[i] if "fut_buy_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        sv_raw = candle_df["fut_sell_vol"].iloc[i] if "fut_sell_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        bp_raw = candle_df["fut_buy_pct"].iloc[i] if "fut_buy_pct" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        vi_raw = candle_df["fut_vol_index"].iloc[i] if "fut_vol_index" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        has_fut = not (np.isnan(bp_raw) if isinstance(bp_raw, float) else pd.isna(bp_raw))

        bv = bv_raw if has_fut else 0
        sv = sv_raw if has_fut else 0
        bp = bp_raw if has_fut else 50.0

        lot_sz = 65
        if has_fut and pd.notna(bv) and pd.notna(sv) and (bv > 0 or sv > 0):
            buy_lots_val = round(bv / lot_sz, 1)
            sell_lots_val = round(sv / lot_sz, 1)
            delta_lots_val = round(buy_lots_val - sell_lots_val, 1)
            bp_val = bp_raw
        else:
            of_item = of_map_5m.get(t_str) if of_map_5m else None
            has_real_of = of_item is not None and (float(of_item.get("buy_volume", 0)) > 0 or float(of_item.get("sell_volume", 0)) > 0)
            if has_real_of:
                bv = float(of_item.get("buy_volume", 0.0))
                sv = float(of_item.get("sell_volume", 0.0))
                buy_lots_val = round(bv / lot_sz, 1)
                sell_lots_val = round(sv / lot_sz, 1)
                delta_lots_val = round(buy_lots_val - sell_lots_val, 1)
                tot_v = bv + sv
                bp_val = round(100.0 * bv / tot_v, 1) if tot_v > 0 else 50.0
                bp = bp_val
                bv_raw = bv
                sv_raw = sv
                has_fut = True
            else:
                buy_lots_val = np.nan
                sell_lots_val = np.nan
                delta_lots_val = np.nan
                bp_val = np.nan

        buy_lots_txt = f"{buy_lots_val:,.0f}" if pd.notna(buy_lots_val) else "—"
        sell_lots_txt = f"{sell_lots_val:,.0f}" if pd.notna(sell_lots_val) else "—"
        if pd.notna(delta_lots_val):
            d_sign = "+" if delta_lots_val > 0 else ""
            delta_txt = f"{d_sign}{delta_lots_val:,.0f}"
        else:
            delta_txt = "—"

        pcr_val = round(candle_df["pcr"].iloc[i], 2) if "pcr" in candle_df.columns and not np.isnan(candle_df["pcr"].iloc[i]) else 1.0
        prev_pcr_val = round(candle_df["pcr"].iloc[i-1], 2) if (i > 0 and "pcr" in candle_df.columns and not np.isnan(candle_df["pcr"].iloc[i-1])) else pcr_val
        pcr_chg_val = round(pcr_val - prev_pcr_val, 2)
        pcr_chg_sign = "+" if pcr_chg_val >= 0 else ""

        # Smart Buildup Signal 2 (with 98% Extreme Trap Detector)
        sig2_val = candle_df["buildup_tag_2"].iloc[i] if "buildup_tag_2" in candle_df.columns else "—"
        if not sig2_val or sig2_val == "—" or "Consolidation" in sig2_val:
            sig2_val, _, _ = classify_smart_buildup_signal(c_val_oi, p_val_oi, spot_change=p_chg, fut_buy_pct=bp, net_delta=(bv - sv))

        table_rows.append({
            "Time": t_str,
            "Spot Close": f"{c_val:,.2f}",
            "Candle Chg": f"{p_sign}{p_chg:.2f}",
            "Call ΔOI": c_oi_txt,
            "Put ΔOI": p_oi_txt,
            "Net ΔOI (PE-CE)": net_oi_txt,
            "Buildup Signal": sig_display,
            "Buy %": f"{bp_val:.1f}%" if pd.notna(bp_val) else "—",
            "PCR": f"{pcr_val:.2f}",
            "PCR Chg": f"{pcr_chg_sign}{pcr_chg_val:.2f}",
            "Buy Lots": buy_lots_txt,
            "Sell Lots": sell_lots_txt,
            "Vol Delta": delta_txt,
            "Fut Buy Vol": f"{bv_raw:,.0f}" if has_fut else "—",
            "Fut Sell Vol": f"{sv_raw:,.0f}" if has_fut else "—",
            "Vol Index": f"{vi_raw:.2f}x" if has_fut else "—"
        })

    if table_rows:
        bar_df = pd.DataFrame(table_rows).iloc[::-1]  # Latest bar at top

        last_i = n_bars - 1
        last_t = candle_df["time_str"].iloc[last_i]
        last_bv_raw = candle_df["fut_buy_vol"].iloc[last_i] if "fut_buy_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        last_sv_raw = candle_df["fut_sell_vol"].iloc[last_i] if "fut_sell_vol" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        last_bp_raw = candle_df["fut_buy_pct"].iloc[last_i] if "fut_buy_pct" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        last_sp_raw = candle_df["fut_sell_pct"].iloc[last_i] if "fut_sell_pct" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        last_vi_raw = candle_df["fut_vol_index"].iloc[last_i] if "fut_vol_index" in candle_df.columns else np.nan
        last_has_fut = not (np.isnan(last_bp_raw) if isinstance(last_bp_raw, float) else pd.isna(last_bp_raw))

        last_of = of_map_5m.get(last_t) if of_map_5m else None
        if last_of:
            last_delta_val = last_of.get("delta_lots", 0.0)
            last_d_sign = "+" if last_delta_val > 0 else ""
            last_delta_txt = f"{last_d_sign}{last_delta_val:,.0f} Lots"
        elif last_has_fut and pd.notna(last_bv_raw) and pd.notna(last_sv_raw):
            last_delta_val = round((last_bv_raw - last_sv_raw) / 65, 0)
            last_d_sign = "+" if last_delta_val > 0 else ""
            last_delta_txt = f"{last_d_sign}{last_delta_val:,.0f} Lots"
        else:
            last_delta_txt = "—"

        delta_color = "#00ff88" if "+" in last_delta_txt else ("#ff3b57" if "-" in last_delta_txt else "#00e5ff")

        buy_txt = f"🟢 Buy Vol: {last_bv_raw:,.0f} ({last_bp_raw:.1f}%)" if last_has_fut else "🟢 Buy Vol: —"
        sell_txt = f"🔴 Sell Vol: {last_sv_raw:,.0f} ({last_sp_raw:.1f}%)" if last_has_fut else "🔴 Sell Vol: —"
        vi_txt = f"📊 Vol Index: {last_vi_raw:.2f}x" if last_has_fut else "📊 Vol Index: —"

        st.markdown(f"""
        <div class="cyber-box" style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:16px; margin: 15px 0 10px 0; background:#090c14; border:1.5px solid rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.45); border-radius:8px; padding:10px 16px; align-items:center;">
            <span style="color:#cbd5e1; font-weight:800; font-size:12px; text-transform:uppercase;">⚡ {fut_sym} LATEST BAR ({last_t}):</span>
            <span style="color:#00ff88; font-weight:700; font-size:12px;">{buy_txt}</span>
            <span style="color:#ff3b57; font-weight:700; font-size:12px;">{sell_txt}</span>
            <span style="color:{delta_color}; font-weight:800; font-size:12px; font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace;">⚡ NET DELTA: {last_delta_txt}</span>
            <span style="color:#00e5ff; font-weight:700; font-size:12px;">{vi_txt}</span>
        </div>
        """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

        st.markdown("""
        <style>
        /* Targeted Override right before Tabs: Force both Tab text labels & highlight bar into Glowing Cyan RGB */
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] button[role="tab"] p,
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] button[role="tab"] span,
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] button[role="tab"] div,
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] button p,
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] button span,
        [data-baseweb="tab-list"] button p,
        [data-baseweb="tab-list"] button span,
        [data-baseweb="tab-list"] button div,
        [data-baseweb="tab"] p,
        [data-baseweb="tab"] span {
            color: #00e5ff !important;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: #00e5ff !important;
            font-weight: 900 !important;
            font-size: 15px !important;
            letter-spacing: 0.3px !important;
            text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.75) !important;
        }
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] [data-baseweb="tab-highlight"],
        [data-baseweb="tab-highlight"] {
            background-color: #00e5ff !important;
            height: 3px !important;
            box-shadow: 0 0 12px #00e5ff !important;
        }
        div[data-testid="stColumn"]:has(iframe) {
            display: flex !important;
            justify-content: flex-end !important;
            align-items: center !important;
        }
        div[data-testid="stColumn"] iframe {
            width: 100% !important;
        }
        </style>
        """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

        head_tbl1, head_tbl2 = st.columns([3.8, 1.2], vertical_alignment="center")
        with head_tbl1:
            st.markdown("""
            <h3 style="color:rgb(0, 229, 255); font-weight:900; letter-spacing:0.5px; margin: 0; padding: 8px 0; text-shadow:0 0 14px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.45); white-space: nowrap; font-size: 1.35rem; display: flex; align-items: center;">
                📋 Bar-by-Bar OI & Futures Volume Tables
            </h3>
            """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
        with head_tbl2:
            if hasattr(st, "iframe"):
                st.iframe(render_screenshot_button_html(), height=42)
            else:
                components.html(render_screenshot_button_html(), height=42)

        # --- DYNAMIC COLUMN ORDERING CONTROLS (MOVE LEFT / RIGHT) ---
        DEFAULT_BAR_COLUMNS = [
            "Time",
            "Spot Close",
            "Candle Chg",
            "Call ΔOI",
            "Put ΔOI",
            "Net ΔOI (PE-CE)",
            "Buildup Signal",
            "Vol Delta",
            "Buy Lots",
            "Sell Lots",
            "Buy %",
            "PCR",
            "PCR Chg",
            "Vol Index"
        ]

        if "bar_table_columns_order" not in st.session_state or not st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] or st.session_state.get("_bar_table_order_version") != "v4_lots_right_of_delta":
            st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] = list(DEFAULT_BAR_COLUMNS)
            st.session_state["_bar_table_order_version"] = "v4_lots_right_of_delta"

        current_cols = [c for c in st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] if c in DEFAULT_BAR_COLUMNS]
        for c in DEFAULT_BAR_COLUMNS:
            if c not in current_cols:
                current_cols.append(c)
        st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] = current_cols

        st.markdown("""
        <style>
        /* Force tabs container to be relative and tab panels static so absolute positioning targets the tab bar */
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] {
            position: relative !important;
        }
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] div[data-baseweb="tab-list"] {
            min-height: 44px !important;
            display: flex !important;
            align-items: center !important;
        }
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] div[data-baseweb="tab-panel"] {
            position: static !important;
            padding-top: 10px !important;
            overflow: visible !important;
        }

        /* ⚙️ Columns button: position cleanly on the tab bar line at the FAR RIGHT */
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] div.stElementContainer:has([data-testid="stPopover"]),
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] div:has(> [data-testid="stPopover"]) {
            position: absolute !important;
            top: 5px !important;
            right: 0px !important;
            left: auto !important;
            z-index: 9999 !important;
            width: auto !important;
            height: 0px !important;
            margin: 0 !important;
            padding: 0 !important;
            border: none !important;
        }

        div[data-testid="stTabs"] [data-testid="stPopover"] {
            position: absolute !important;
            top: 5px !important;
            right: 0px !important;
            left: auto !important;
            z-index: 9999 !important;
            width: max-content !important;
            min-width: 125px !important;
            margin: 0 !important;
            padding: 0 !important;
            display: inline-block !important;
        }

        div[data-testid="stTabs"] [data-testid="stPopover"] > button,
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] [data-testid="stPopover"] button {
            height: 32px !important;
            min-height: 32px !important;
            max-height: 32px !important;
            width: auto !important;
            min-width: 125px !important;
            padding: 0 16px !important;
            border-radius: 6px !important;
            background: #09121f !important;
            border: 1.5px solid #00e5ff !important;
            color: #00e5ff !important;
            font-weight: 800 !important;
            font-size: 12.5px !important;
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.25) !important;
            white-space: nowrap !important;
            word-break: keep-all !important;
            display: inline-flex !important;
            align-items: center !important;
            justify-content: center !important;
            gap: 6px !important;
            cursor: pointer !important;
            line-height: 30px !important;
            transition: all 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1) !important;
        }

        div[data-testid="stTabs"] [data-testid="stPopover"] button * {
            white-space: nowrap !important;
            word-break: keep-all !important;
            font-size: 12.5px !important;
            font-weight: 800 !important;
            color: #00e5ff !important;
        }

        div[data-testid="stTabs"] [data-testid="stPopover"] > button:hover,
        div[data-testid="stTabs"] [data-testid="stPopover"] button:hover {
            background: #0f1f33 !important;
            border-color: #38bdf8 !important;
            box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.6) !important;
            transform: translateY(-1px) !important;
        }
        </style>
        """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

        tab_current, tab_15m = st.tabs([f"📊 {timeframe.upper()} Bar-by-Bar Table", "⏱️ 15-Min Bar-by-Bar Table (Macro View)"])

        with tab_current:
            with st.popover("⚙️ Columns", width="content"):
                st.markdown("""
                <div style="font-size:11.5px; color:#94a3b8; margin-bottom:8px;">
                    Select any column to move <b style="color:#00e5ff;">⬅️ Left</b> or <b style="color:#00e5ff;">➡️ Right</b>:
                </div>
                """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

                saved_sel = st.session_state.get("bar_table_selected_col", current_cols[0])
                sel_idx = current_cols.index(saved_sel) if saved_sel in current_cols else 0

                chosen_col = st.selectbox(
                    f"Select {timeframe.upper()} Column to Move:",
                    options=current_cols,
                    index=sel_idx,
                    key="select_bar_col",
                    label_visibility="collapsed"
                )
                st.session_state["bar_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col

                curr_idx = current_cols.index(chosen_col)

                cb1, cb2, cb3, cb4 = st.columns(4)
                with cb1:
                    if st.button("⬅️ Left", key="btn_bar_left", width="stretch", disabled=(curr_idx == 0)):
                        current_cols[curr_idx], current_cols[curr_idx - 1] = current_cols[curr_idx - 1], current_cols[curr_idx]
                        st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] = current_cols
                        st.session_state["bar_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col
                        st.rerun()

                with cb2:
                    if st.button("➡️ Right", key="btn_bar_right", width="stretch", disabled=(curr_idx == len(current_cols) - 1)):
                        current_cols[curr_idx], current_cols[curr_idx + 1] = current_cols[curr_idx + 1], current_cols[curr_idx]
                        st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] = current_cols
                        st.session_state["bar_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col
                        st.rerun()

                with cb3:
                    if st.button("⏮️ Front", key="btn_bar_front", width="stretch", disabled=(curr_idx == 0)):
                        current_cols.pop(curr_idx)
                        current_cols.insert(0, chosen_col)
                        st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] = current_cols
                        st.session_state["bar_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col
                        st.rerun()

                with cb4:
                    if st.button("🔄 Reset", key="btn_bar_reset", width="stretch"):
                        st.session_state["bar_table_columns_order"] = list(DEFAULT_BAR_COLUMNS)
                        st.session_state["bar_table_selected_col"] = DEFAULT_BAR_COLUMNS[0]
                        st.rerun()

                # Visual preview badges
                b_html = ['<div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:5px; margin-top:10px; align-items:center;">']
                b_html.append('<span style="font-size:10.5px; font-weight:800; color:#00e5ff; margin-right:4px;">ORDER:</span>')
                for order_i, col_name in enumerate(current_cols):
                    is_sel = (col_name == chosen_col)
                    bg_color = "rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.22)" if is_sel else "#0a101d"
                    bdr_color = "#00e5ff" if is_sel else "#1e293b"
                    txt_color = "#00e5ff" if is_sel else "#94a3b8"
                    b_html.append(f'<span style="padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px; font-size:10px; font-weight:700; background:{bg_color}; border:1px solid {bdr_color}; color:{txt_color}; font-family:monospace;">{order_i + 1}. {col_name}</span>')
                    if order_i < len(current_cols) - 1:
                        b_html.append('<span style="color:#475569; font-size:9px;">➔</span>')
                badge_html = "".join(b_html)
                st.markdown(badge_html, unsafe_allow_html=True)

            st.caption(f"Granular {timeframe} window analysis combining 6-strike active writing OI deltas with real {fut_sym} Buy/Sell volumes • Latest candle at top")
            render_cyan_bar_table(bar_df, custom_order=st.session_state.get("bar_table_columns_order"))

        with tab_15m:
            DEFAULT_BAR15_ORDER = [
                "Time (15m)",
                "Spot Close",
                "Candle Chg",
                "Call ΔOI",
                "Put ΔOI",
                "Net ΔOI (PE-CE)",

            if "bar15_table_columns_order" not in st.session_state or not st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] or st.session_state.get("_bar15_table_order_version") != "v3_clean_no_fut_vols":
                st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] = list(DEFAULT_BAR15_ORDER)
                st.session_state["_bar15_table_order_version"] = "v3_clean_no_fut_vols"

            current_cols_15 = [c for c in st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] if c in DEFAULT_BAR15_ORDER]
            for c in DEFAULT_BAR15_ORDER:
