                    current_cols_15.append(c)
            st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] = current_cols_15

            with st.popover("⚙️ Columns", width="content"):
                st.markdown("""
                <div style="font-size:11.5px; color:#94a3b8; margin-bottom:8px;">
                    Select any column to move <b style="color:#00e5ff;">⬅️ Left</b> or <b style="color:#00e5ff;">➡️ Right</b>:
                </div>
                """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

                saved_sel_15 = st.session_state.get("bar15_table_selected_col", current_cols_15[0])
                sel_idx_15 = current_cols_15.index(saved_sel_15) if saved_sel_15 in current_cols_15 else 0

                chosen_col_15 = st.selectbox(
                    "Select 15M Column to Move:",
                    options=current_cols_15,
                    index=sel_idx_15,
                    key="select_bar15_col",
                    label_visibility="collapsed"
                )
                st.session_state["bar15_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col_15

                curr_idx_15 = current_cols_15.index(chosen_col_15)

                c15_b1, c15_b2, c15_b3, c15_b4 = st.columns(4)
                with c15_b1:
                    if st.button("⬅️ Left", key="btn_bar15_left", width="stretch", disabled=(curr_idx_15 == 0)):
                        current_cols_15[curr_idx_15], current_cols_15[curr_idx_15 - 1] = current_cols_15[curr_idx_15 - 1], current_cols_15[curr_idx_15]
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] = current_cols_15
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col_15
                        st.rerun()

                with c15_b2:
                    if st.button("➡️ Right", key="btn_bar15_right", width="stretch", disabled=(curr_idx_15 == len(current_cols_15) - 1)):
                        current_cols_15[curr_idx_15], current_cols_15[curr_idx_15 + 1] = current_cols_15[curr_idx_15 + 1], current_cols_15[curr_idx_15]
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] = current_cols_15
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col_15
                        st.rerun()

                with c15_b3:
                    if st.button("⏮️ Front", key="btn_bar15_front", width="stretch", disabled=(curr_idx_15 == 0)):
                        current_cols_15.pop(curr_idx_15)
                        current_cols_15.insert(0, chosen_col_15)
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] = current_cols_15
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_selected_col"] = chosen_col_15
                        st.rerun()

                with c15_b4:
                    if st.button("🔄 Reset", key="btn_bar15_reset", width="stretch"):
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_columns_order"] = list(DEFAULT_BAR15_ORDER)
                        st.session_state["bar15_table_selected_col"] = DEFAULT_BAR15_ORDER[0]
                        st.rerun()

                # Visual preview badges
                badge_html_15 = ['<div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:5px; margin-top:10px; align-items:center;">']
                badge_html_15.append('<span style="font-size:10.5px; font-weight:800; color:#00e5ff; margin-right:4px;">ORDER:</span>')
                for order_i, col_name in enumerate(current_cols_15):
                    is_sel = (col_name == chosen_col_15)
                    bg_color = "rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.22)" if is_sel else "#0a101d"
                    bdr_color = "#00e5ff" if is_sel else "#1e293b"
                    txt_color = "#00e5ff" if is_sel else "#94a3b8"
                    badge_html_15.append(f'<span style="padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px; font-size:10px; font-weight:700; background:{bg_color}; border:1px solid {bdr_color}; color:{txt_color}; font-family:monospace;">{order_i + 1}. {col_name}</span>')
                    if order_i < len(current_cols_15) - 1:
                        badge_html_15.append('<span style="color:#475569; font-size:9px;">➔</span>')
                badge_html_15_str = "".join(badge_html_15)
                st.markdown(badge_html_15_str, unsafe_allow_html=True)

            st.caption("Aggregated 15-minute macro window analysis derived in-memory • Zero extra API latency • Latest 15m bar at top")
            try:
                target_date_str = selected_date.isoformat() if not is_today_data else datetime.date.today().isoformat()
                f_15m = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_15m_{target_date_str}.csv"
                rows15 = []

                # Safely load today's OI snapshots & Full Options chain for frontline ATM calculation
                f_oi_snap = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_OI_{target_date_str}.csv"
                if not f_oi_snap.exists():
                    all_oi = sorted(DATA_DIR.glob("NIFTY_OI_*.csv"))
                    f_oi_snap = all_oi[-1] if all_oi else None
                df_oi_snap = pd.read_csv(f_oi_snap) if (f_oi_snap and f_oi_snap.exists()) else None

                f_full_opt = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_FULL_OPTIONS_{target_date_str}.csv"
                if not f_full_opt.exists():
                    all_fo = sorted(DATA_DIR.glob("NIFTY_FULL_OPTIONS_*.csv"))
                    f_full_opt = all_fo[-1] if all_fo else None
                df_full_opt = pd.read_csv(f_full_opt) if (f_full_opt and f_full_opt.exists()) else None

                # Precompute exact 15-minute Frontline Flow Asymmetry matching Tactical Action Card (using Full Options)
                flow_map_15m = {}
                ref_df_flow = df_full_opt if (df_full_opt is not None and not df_full_opt.empty) else df_oi_snap
                if ref_df_flow is not None and not ref_df_flow.empty and "Time" in ref_df_flow.columns:
                    try:
                        all_ts_15 = sorted([t for t in ref_df_flow["Time"].str.slice(0, 5).unique() if t[3:] in ("00", "15", "30", "45")])
                        latest_time_str = ref_df_flow["Time"].iloc[-1][:5]
                        if latest_time_str not in all_ts_15:
                            all_ts_15.append(latest_time_str)
                            
                        for i_s in range(len(all_ts_15) - 1):
                            t_s = all_ts_15[i_s]
                            t_e = all_ts_15[i_s + 1]
                            s_b = ref_df_flow[ref_df_flow["Time"].str.startswith(t_s)]
                            s_r = ref_df_flow[ref_df_flow["Time"].str.startswith(t_e)]
                            if s_b.empty or s_r.empty:
                                continue
                            sub_ref_atm = df_oi_snap[df_oi_snap["Time"].str.startswith(t_e)] if df_oi_snap is not None else pd.DataFrame()
                            atm_val = int(sub_ref_atm["ATM"].iloc[0]) if (not sub_ref_atm.empty and "ATM" in sub_ref_atm.columns and pd.notna(sub_ref_atm["ATM"].iloc[0])) else int(summary.get("atm_strike", 22700))
                            c_stk = [atm_val, atm_val + 50, atm_val + 100]
                            p_stk = [atm_val - 100, atm_val - 50, atm_val]

                            if "Option_Type" in ref_df_flow.columns:
                                cb = s_b[(s_b["Strike"].isin(c_stk)) & (s_b["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["OI"].sum()
                                cc = s_r[(s_r["Strike"].isin(c_stk)) & (s_r["Option_Type"] == "CE")]["OI"].sum()
                                pb = s_b[(s_b["Strike"].isin(p_stk)) & (s_b["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["OI"].sum()
                                pc = s_r[(s_r["Strike"].isin(p_stk)) & (s_r["Option_Type"] == "PE")]["OI"].sum()
                            else:
                                com_c = [s for s in c_stk if s in s_b["Strike"].values and s in s_r["Strike"].values]
                                com_p = [s for s in p_stk if s in s_b["Strike"].values and s in s_r["Strike"].values]
                                cb = s_b[s_b["Strike"].isin(com_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
                                cc = s_r[s_r["Strike"].isin(com_c)]["CE OI"].sum()
                                pb = s_b[s_b["Strike"].isin(com_p)]["PE OI"].sum()
                                pc = s_r[s_r["Strike"].isin(com_p)]["PE OI"].sum()

                            dce = (cc - cb) / 1e5
                            dpe = (pc - pb) / 1e5
                            net_f = dpe - dce
                            if abs(dce) > 0 and abs(dpe) > 0:
                                asy = abs(dce) / abs(dpe) if abs(dce) >= abs(dpe) else abs(dpe) / abs(dce)
                            else:
                                asy = 1.0
                            if asy > 2.0 and net_f > 0:
                                flow_map_15m[t_s] = f"🚀 {asy:.1f}x Short Squeeze"
                            elif asy > 2.0 and net_f < 0:
                                flow_map_15m[t_s] = f"🚨 {asy:.1f}x Bearish Dump"
                            else:
                                flow_map_15m[t_s] = f"⚖️ {asy:.1f}x Symm Flow"
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                if f_15m.exists():
                    df15_base = pd.read_csv(f_15m)
                    # Instant boundary catch-up during live session:
                    # When a 15-minute candle closes (:00, :15, :30, :45), if the file doesn't have the
                    # newly formed candle yet during the first 5 seconds, wait 0.4s and re-read once.
                    if is_today_data and not df15_base.empty and "time_str" in df15_base.columns:
                        now_dt = datetime.datetime.now()
                        if 9 <= now_dt.hour <= 15 and now_dt.second <= 6:
                            exp_min = (now_dt.minute // 15) * 15
                            exp_t_str = f"{now_dt.hour:02d}:{exp_min:02d}"
                            last_t_str = str(df15_base["time_str"].iloc[-1])
                            if last_t_str < exp_t_str:
                                time.sleep(0.4)
                                try:
                                    df_fresh = pd.read_csv(f_15m)
                                    if not df_fresh.empty:
                                        df15_base = df_fresh
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
                    if "epoch" in df15_base.columns:
                        df15_base = df15_base.drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last").sort_values("epoch").reset_index(drop=True)
                        raw_dts_15 = pd.to_datetime(df15_base["epoch"], unit="s", utc=True).dt.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata").tolist()
                    elif "datetime" in df15_base.columns:
                        df15_base = df15_base.drop_duplicates(subset=["datetime"], keep="last").sort_values("datetime").reset_index(drop=True)
                        raw_dts_15 = pd.to_datetime(df15_base["datetime"]).tolist()
                    else:
                        raw_dts_15 = pd.date_range(end=datetime.datetime.now(), periods=len(df15_base), freq="15m").tolist()

                    c15_calls, c15_puts = oi_engine.get_time_bucket_oi_delta(raw_dts_15, 15, "NIFTY")
                    prev_pcr_15 = None
                    for idx15, r15 in df15_base.iterrows():
                        t15_str = str(r15.get("time_str", ""))
                        c_15 = float(r15.get("close", 0))
                        o_15 = float(r15.get("open", 0))
                        p_chg15 = c_15 - o_15
                        p_sign15 = "+" if p_chg15 >= 0 else ""

                        c_oi15 = c15_calls[idx15] if c15_calls is not None and idx15 < len(c15_calls) and not np.isnan(c15_calls[idx15]) else None
                        p_oi15 = c15_puts[idx15] if c15_puts is not None and idx15 < len(c15_puts) and not np.isnan(c15_puts[idx15]) else None

                        c15_txt = f"{c_oi15:+.2f}L" if c_oi15 is not None else "—"
                        p15_txt = f"{p_oi15:+.2f}L" if p_oi15 is not None else "—"

                        if c_oi15 is not None and p_oi15 is not None:
                            net_l15 = p_oi15 - c_oi15
                            net15_txt = f"{net_l15:+.2f}L"
                        elif pd.notna(r15.get("corridor_oi_chg")):
                            net15_txt = f"{float(r15['corridor_oi_chg']) / 1e5:+.2f}L"
                        else:
                            net15_txt = "—"

                        bv15 = float(r15.get("fut_buy_vol", 0))
                        sv15 = float(r15.get("fut_sell_vol", 0))
                        bp15_raw = r15.get("fut_buy_pct", np.nan)
                        has_fut15 = pd.notna(bp15_raw)
                        bp15 = float(bp15_raw) if has_fut15 else 50.0

                        c_val_oi = c_oi15 * 1e5 if c_oi15 is not None else 0.0
                        p_val_oi = p_oi15 * 1e5 if p_oi15 is not None else 0.0

                        # Signal 1 (Pure OI Classification - dynamically calculated from actual Call/Put deltas)
                        if c_oi15 is not None and p_oi15 is not None:
                            sig_raw, _, icon_sig = classify_pure_oi_signal(c_val_oi, p_val_oi, spot_change=p_chg15)
                            sig15 = f"{icon_sig} {sig_raw}"
                        else:
                            sig15 = str(r15.get("buildup_tag", "—"))
                            if sig15 and sig15 != "—":
                                if "Long Build" in sig15 or "Long Buildup" in sig15:
                                    sig15 = f"🟢 {sig15}"
                                elif "Short Cov" in sig15:
                                    sig15 = f"🔵 {sig15}"
                                elif "Short Build" in sig15 or "Short Buildup" in sig15:
                                    sig15 = f"🔴 {sig15}"
                                elif "Long Unwind" in sig15:
                                    sig15 = f"⚪ {sig15}"
                                else:
                                    sig15 = f"⚪ {sig15}"
                            else:
                                sig15 = "—"

                        # Signal 2 & Flow Asymmetry (Smart Order-Flow & Trap Radar)
                        if c_oi15 is not None and p_oi15 is not None:
                            sig2_15, _, _ = classify_smart_buildup_signal(c_val_oi, p_val_oi, spot_change=p_chg15, fut_buy_pct=bp15, net_delta=(bv15 - sv15))
                        else:
                            sig2_15 = str(r15.get("buildup_tag_2", "—"))

                        # 15M Flow & Asymmetry: 100% Strictly Synchronized from Tactical Action Matrix Card!
                        flow_asym_15 = st.session_state.get("tactical_15m_flow_map", {}).get(t15_str)
                        if not flow_asym_15:
                            flow_asym_15 = flow_map_15m.get(t15_str)
                        if not flow_asym_15:
                            if c_oi15 is not None and p_oi15 is not None:
                                c_abs15 = abs(c_oi15)
                                p_abs15 = abs(p_oi15)
                                if c_abs15 > 0 and p_abs15 > 0:
                                    asym15 = round(c_abs15 / p_abs15, 1) if c_abs15 >= p_abs15 else round(p_abs15 / c_abs15, 1)
                                elif c_abs15 > 0 or p_abs15 > 0:
                                    asym15 = round(max(c_abs15, p_abs15), 1)
                                else:
                                    asym15 = 1.0

                                net_diff_15 = p_oi15 - c_oi15
                                if asym15 > 2.0 and net_diff_15 > 0:
                                    flow_asym_15 = f"🚀 {asym15:.1f}x Short Squeeze"
                                elif asym15 > 2.0 and net_diff_15 < 0:
                                    flow_asym_15 = f"🚨 {asym15:.1f}x Bearish Dump"
                                else:
                                    flow_asym_15 = f"⚖️ {asym15:.1f}x Symm Flow"
                            else:
                                flow_asym_15 = "—"

                        lot_sz = 65
                        if has_fut15 and pd.notna(bv15) and pd.notna(sv15) and (bv15 > 0 or sv15 > 0):
                            buy_lots_15 = round(bv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                            sell_lots_15 = round(sv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                            delta_lots_15 = round(buy_lots_15 - sell_lots_15, 1)
                        else:
                            of_item_15 = of_map_15m.get(t15_str) if of_map_15m else None
                            has_real_of_15 = of_item_15 is not None and (float(of_item_15.get("buy_volume", 0)) > 0 or float(of_item_15.get("sell_volume", 0)) > 0)
                            if has_real_of_15:
                                bv15 = float(of_item_15.get("buy_volume", 0.0))
                                sv15 = float(of_item_15.get("sell_volume", 0.0))
                                buy_lots_15 = round(bv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                                sell_lots_15 = round(sv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                                delta_lots_15 = round(buy_lots_15 - sell_lots_15, 1)
                                tot_v15 = bv15 + sv15
                                bp15 = round(100.0 * bv15 / tot_v15, 1) if tot_v15 > 0 else 50.0
                                has_fut15 = True
                            else:
                                buy_lots_15 = np.nan
                                sell_lots_15 = np.nan
                                delta_lots_15 = np.nan

                        buy_lots_txt15 = f"{buy_lots_15:,.0f}" if pd.notna(buy_lots_15) else "—"
                        sell_lots_txt15 = f"{sell_lots_15:,.0f}" if pd.notna(sell_lots_15) else "—"
                        if pd.notna(delta_lots_15):
                            d_sign15 = "+" if delta_lots_15 > 0 else ""
                            delta_txt15 = f"{d_sign15}{delta_lots_15:,.0f}"
                        else:
                            delta_txt15 = "—"

                        pcr15 = round(float(r15.get("pcr", 1.0)), 2) if pd.notna(r15.get("pcr")) else 1.0
                        pcr_chg15 = round(pcr15 - prev_pcr_15, 2) if prev_pcr_15 is not None else 0.0
                        prev_pcr_15 = pcr15
                        pcr_sign15 = "+" if pcr_chg15 >= 0 else ""

                        rows15.append({
                            "Time (15m)": t15_str,
                            "Spot Close": f"{c_15:,.2f}",
                            "Candle Chg": f"{p_sign15}{p_chg15:.2f}",
                            "Call ΔOI": c15_txt,
                            "Put ΔOI": p15_txt,
                            "Net ΔOI (PE-CE)": net15_txt,
                            "Buildup Signal": sig15,
                            "Buildup Signal 2": sig2_15,
                            "Flow & Asymmetry": flow_asym_15,
                            "Vol Delta": delta_txt15,
                            "Buy %": f"{bp15:.1f}%" if has_fut15 else "—",
                            "PCR": f"{pcr15:.2f}",
                            "PCR Chg": f"{pcr_sign15}{pcr_chg15:.2f}",
                            "Buy Lots": buy_lots_txt15,
                            "Sell Lots": sell_lots_txt15
                        })
                else:
                    # Fallback to 5-minute aggregation if 15m file not found
                    if timeframe == "5m":
                        df_calc = candle_df.copy()
                    else:
                        f_5m = DATA_DIR / f"NIFTY_CANDLES_5m_{target_date_str}.csv"
                        df_calc = pd.read_csv(f_5m) if f_5m.exists() else candle_df.copy()

                    if "epoch" in df_calc.columns:
                        df_calc["dt_15"] = pd.to_datetime(df_calc["epoch"], unit="s", utc=True).dt.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata")
                    elif "datetime" in df_calc.columns:
                        df_calc["dt_15"] = pd.to_datetime(df_calc["datetime"])
                    else:
                        df_calc["dt_15"] = pd.date_range(end=datetime.datetime.now(), periods=len(df_calc), freq="5m")

                    df_calc["15m_group"] = df_calc["dt_15"].dt.floor("15min")
                    group_list = list(df_calc.groupby("15m_group", sort=False))
                    group_dts = [g_dt for g_dt, _ in group_list]
                    c15_calls, c15_puts = oi_engine.get_time_bucket_oi_delta(group_dts, 15, "NIFTY")

                    prev_pcr_15 = None
                    for idx15, (group_dt, grp) in enumerate(group_list):
                        t15_str = group_dt.strftime("%H:%M")
                        o_15 = grp["open"].iloc[0]
                        c_15 = grp["close"].iloc[-1]
                        p_chg15 = c_15 - o_15
                        p_sign15 = "+" if p_chg15 >= 0 else ""

                        c_oi15 = c15_calls[idx15] if c15_calls is not None and idx15 < len(c15_calls) and not np.isnan(c15_calls[idx15]) else None
                        p_oi15 = c15_puts[idx15] if c15_puts is not None and idx15 < len(c15_puts) and not np.isnan(c15_puts[idx15]) else None

                        c15_txt = f"{c_oi15:+.2f}L" if c_oi15 is not None else "—"
                        p15_txt = f"{p_oi15:+.2f}L" if p_oi15 is not None else "—"

                        if c_oi15 is not None and p_oi15 is not None:
                            net_l15 = p_oi15 - c_oi15
                            net15_txt = f"{net_l15:+.2f}L"
                        else:
                            net15_txt = "—"

                        bv15 = grp["fut_buy_vol"].sum() if "fut_buy_vol" in grp.columns else 0
                        sv15 = grp["fut_sell_vol"].sum() if "fut_sell_vol" in grp.columns else 0
                        # Check if ANY bar in this 15m group has real futures data
                        has_fut15 = bool(grp["has_real_fut"].any()) if "has_real_fut" in grp.columns else False
                        tot15 = bv15 + sv15
                        bp15 = (bv15 / tot15 * 100) if (tot15 > 0 and has_fut15) else 50.0

                        if c_oi15 is not None and p_oi15 is not None:
                            sig_tag15, sig_bias15, sig_icon15 = classify_pure_oi_signal(c_oi15 * 1e5, p_oi15 * 1e5, p_chg15)
                            sig15 = f"{sig_icon15} {sig_tag15}"
                            sig2_15, _, _ = classify_smart_buildup_signal(c_oi15 * 1e5, p_oi15 * 1e5, p_chg15, fut_buy_pct=bp15, net_delta=(bv15 - sv15))
                        else:
                            sig15 = "—"
                            sig2_15 = "—"

                        flow_asym_15 = st.session_state.get("tactical_15m_flow_map", {}).get(t15_str)
                        if not flow_asym_15:
                            flow_asym_15 = flow_map_15m.get(t15_str)
                        if not flow_asym_15:
                            if c_oi15 is not None and p_oi15 is not None:
                                c_abs15 = abs(c_oi15)
                                p_abs15 = abs(p_oi15)
                                if c_abs15 > 0 and p_abs15 > 0:
                                    asym15 = round(c_abs15 / p_abs15, 1) if c_abs15 >= p_abs15 else round(p_abs15 / c_abs15, 1)
                                elif c_abs15 > 0 or p_abs15 > 0:
                                    asym15 = round(max(c_abs15, p_abs15), 1)
                                else:
                                    asym15 = 1.0

                                net_diff_15 = p_oi15 - c_oi15
                                if asym15 > 2.0 and net_diff_15 > 0:
                                    flow_asym_15 = f"🚀 {asym15:.1f}x Short Squeeze"
                                elif asym15 > 2.0 and net_diff_15 < 0:
                                    flow_asym_15 = f"🚨 {asym15:.1f}x Bearish Dump"
                                else:
                                    flow_asym_15 = f"⚖️ {asym15:.1f}x Symm Flow"
                            else:
                                flow_asym_15 = "—"

                        # Check order flow engine data for true tick-level 15m macro buy/sell lots
                        lot_sz = 65
                        if has_fut15 and pd.notna(bv15) and pd.notna(sv15) and (bv15 > 0 or sv15 > 0):
                            buy_lots_15 = round(bv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                            sell_lots_15 = round(sv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                            delta_lots_15 = round(buy_lots_15 - sell_lots_15, 1)
                        else:
                            of_item_15 = of_map_15m.get(t15_str) if of_map_15m else None
                            has_real_of_15 = of_item_15 is not None and (float(of_item_15.get("buy_volume", 0)) > 0 or float(of_item_15.get("sell_volume", 0)) > 0)
                            if has_real_of_15:
                                bv15 = float(of_item_15.get("buy_volume", 0.0))
                                sv15 = float(of_item_15.get("sell_volume", 0.0))
                                buy_lots_15 = round(bv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                                sell_lots_15 = round(sv15 / lot_sz, 1)
                                delta_lots_15 = round(buy_lots_15 - sell_lots_15, 1)
                                tot_v15 = bv15 + sv15
                                bp15 = round(100.0 * bv15 / tot_v15, 1) if tot_v15 > 0 else 50.0
                                has_fut15 = True
                            else:
                                buy_lots_15 = np.nan
                                sell_lots_15 = np.nan
                                delta_lots_15 = np.nan

                        buy_lots_txt15 = f"{buy_lots_15:,.0f}" if pd.notna(buy_lots_15) else "—"
                        sell_lots_txt15 = f"{sell_lots_15:,.0f}" if pd.notna(sell_lots_15) else "—"
                        if pd.notna(delta_lots_15):
                            d_sign15 = "+" if delta_lots_15 > 0 else ""
                            delta_txt15 = f"{d_sign15}{delta_lots_15:,.0f}"
                        else:
                            delta_txt15 = "—"

                        pcr15 = round(float(grp["pcr"].iloc[-1]), 2) if ("pcr" in grp.columns and pd.notna(grp["pcr"].iloc[-1])) else 1.0
                        pcr_chg15 = round(pcr15 - prev_pcr_15, 2) if prev_pcr_15 is not None else 0.0
                        prev_pcr_15 = pcr15
                        pcr_sign15 = "+" if pcr_chg15 >= 0 else ""

                        rows15.append({
                            "Time (15m)": t15_str,
                            "Spot Close": f"{c_15:,.2f}",
                            "Candle Chg": f"{p_sign15}{p_chg15:.2f}",
                            "Call ΔOI": c15_txt,
                            "Put ΔOI": p15_txt,
                            "Net ΔOI (PE-CE)": net15_txt,
                            "Buildup Signal": sig15,
                            "Buildup Signal 2": sig2_15,
                            "Flow & Asymmetry": flow_asym_15,
                            "Vol Delta": delta_txt15,
                            "Buy %": f"{bp15:.1f}%" if has_fut15 else "—",
                            "PCR": f"{pcr15:.2f}",
                            "PCR Chg": f"{pcr_sign15}{pcr_chg15:.2f}",
                            "Buy Lots": buy_lots_txt15,
                            "Sell Lots": sell_lots_txt15
                        })

                bar15_df = pd.DataFrame(rows15).iloc[::-1] if rows15 else pd.DataFrame()
                render_cyan_bar_table(
                    bar15_df,
                    custom_order=st.session_state.get("bar15_table_columns_order", DEFAULT_BAR15_ORDER)
                )
            except Exception as ex15:
                st.warning(f"Unable to render 15-min aggregated table: {ex15}")

        # --- OI SUBPLOT COLOR LEGEND (AT THE BOTTOM OF DASHBOARD) ---
        st.markdown("""
<div style="background:#07090e; border:1px solid #1e2638; border-radius:8px; padding:10px 20px; margin:16px 0 10px 0; font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace;">
  <div style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10px; letter-spacing:0.08em; margin-bottom:8px;">
    📊 OI DATA SUBPLOT — COLOR LEGEND &nbsp;|&nbsp; Put &#916;OI (above zero) + Call &#916;OI (below zero)
  </div>
  <table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:0 4px; width:100%;">
    <tr>
      <td style="padding:4px 12px; background:#0d1117; border:1px solid #1e2638; border-radius:6px; white-space:nowrap; font-size:13px;">
        🟢 GREEN + 🔵 BLUE
      </td>
      <td style="padding:4px 14px; color:#94a3b8; font-size:10px;">Put Add + Call Cover</td>
      <td style="padding:4px 16px; color:#00e676; font-weight:900; font-size:12px;">&#9650; STRONGLY BULLISH</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style="padding:4px 12px; background:#0d1117; border:1px solid #1e2638; border-radius:6px; white-space:nowrap; font-size:13px;">
        ⚪ WHITE + 🔴 RED
      </td>
      <td style="padding:4px 14px; color:#94a3b8; font-size:10px;">Put Unwind + Call Add</td>
      <td style="padding:4px 16px; color:#ff3b57; font-weight:900; font-size:12px;">&#9660; STRONGLY BEARISH</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style="padding:4px 12px; background:#0d1117; border:1px solid #1e2638; border-radius:6px; white-space:nowrap; font-size:13px;">
        🟢 GREEN + 🔴 RED
      </td>
      <td style="padding:4px 14px; color:#94a3b8; font-size:10px;">Both Sides Writing</td>
      <td style="padding:4px 16px; color:#94a3b8; font-weight:900; font-size:12px;">&#9670; NEUTRAL / SQUEEZE</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td style="padding:4px 12px; background:#0d1117; border:1px solid #1e2638; border-radius:6px; white-space:nowrap; font-size:13px;">
        ⚪ WHITE + 🔵 BLUE
      </td>
      <td style="padding:4px 14px; color:#94a3b8; font-size:10px;">Both Sides Exiting</td>
      <td style="padding:4px 16px; color:#facc15; font-weight:900; font-size:12px;">&#9888; WEAK / CONFUSED</td>
    </tr>
  </table>
</div>
""", unsafe_allow_html=True)








# Footer Actions & Auto-refresh logic
st.markdown("---")
col_foot1, col_foot2 = st.columns([1, 4])
with col_foot1:
    if st.button("🔄 Manual Refresh Now", key="btn_manual_refresh", width="stretch"):
        st.rerun()

now_dt = get_ist_now()
is_weekend = now_dt.weekday() >= 5  # Saturday or Sunday
is_after_market = now_dt.time() >= datetime.time(15, 40) or now_dt.time() < datetime.time(9, 0)

if is_weekend or is_after_market:
    with col_foot2:
        reason = "Weekend" if is_weekend else "Market Closed (Post 03:40 PM IST)"
        st.caption(f"⏸️ **Auto-Refresh Paused:** {reason}. Data is static. Use **Manual Refresh Now** if needed.")
else:
    # Auto-refresh loop during active market session (09:00 AM - 03:40 PM IST)
    # ZERO-LATENCY BOUNDARY SNAPPING: Reloads immediately when any 3m, 5m, or 15m timeframe starts!
    if auto_refresh:
        now_t = get_ist_now()
        cur_sec = now_t.second
        cur_ms = now_t.microsecond / 1000.0
        cur_min = now_t.minute

        # ZERO-LATENCY 1-MINUTE CLOSE RELOAD: Snaps precisely to second 00.600 of EVERY single minute!
        sec_to_next_min = (60.0 - cur_sec) - (cur_ms / 1000.0) + 0.6  # snaps to 00.600s
        
        # Mid-minute tick at :30s, and exact candle close reload at :00.6s
        if cur_sec < 28:
            target_interval_sec = min(30.0 - cur_sec, sec_to_next_min)
        else:
            target_interval_sec = max(1.0, sec_to_next_min)

        refresh_ms = int(target_interval_sec * 1000)

        # 1. Official Streamlit Autorefresh Component (Static key ensures uninterrupted React timer loop)
        try:
            from streamlit_autorefresh import st_autorefresh
            st_autorefresh(interval=refresh_ms, key="nifty_dashboard_autorefresh_timer")
        except Exception:
            time.sleep(target_interval_sec)
            st.rerun()

## 📘 Detailed Bug Records & Root Cause Analysis

### BUG-001: 15-Min Scrubber Missing 09:15 AM & 1-Bar (15m) Replay Lag
* **Date:** 2026-09-28 12:01 PM IST | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Scrubber started at `09:30`, omitting `09:15` AM. When selecting `09:30`, it rendered `09:15` data. Every milestone was 15 minutes behind.
* **Root Cause:**
  1. Milestone loop initialized `h_milestone = 9, m_milestone = 30`, skipping `09:15`.
  2. For `09:30`, nearest tick was `09:29:01` (`t_ref[:5] = "09:29"`). Filtering candle file with `time_str <= "09:29"` evaluated `False` for the `"09:30"` candle, excluding it and loading `"09:15"`.
* **Fix Applied:**
  * Initialized milestone loop at `h=9, m=15` with window search `<="HH:MM:59"`.
  * Synchronized replay filters directly with `selected_slot` (`as_of_time=selected_slot`), guaranteeing 1-to-1 sync.
* **Prevention Rule:** Never use truncated wall-clock seconds (`t_ref[:5]`) to filter discrete candle bucket boundaries.

---

### BUG-002: AI Quant Signal Under-Triggering SELL in Downward Grinds
* **Date:** 2026-09-28 11:20 AM IST | **File:** [ml_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/ml_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** In a continuous morning slide from 22,865 to 22,815, model stayed in `⚪ WAIT / HOLD (70%)` without generating `🔴 SELL / BUY PUT (PE)`.
* **Root Cause:**
  * Labeling threshold was $\pm 35.0$ pts in a single 15-min bar.
  * Historical distribution analysis revealed 15-min mean move is only 15.28 pts (median: 10.15 pts). 35 pts was at the 90.4th percentile, artificially skewing the dataset so that **91.2% of all historical bars were labeled HOLD (Class 2)**.
* **Fix Applied:**
  * Calibrated dynamic labeling for 15-min / 30-min swing windows: 15m move $\ge 18$ pts OR 30m cumulative move $\ge 25$ pts.
  * Retrained LightGBM + CatBoost classifiers with balanced classes (14% BUY, 25% SELL, 61% HOLD).
  * Upgraded decision engine: When Put writing unwinds and Call writing dominates ($p_{\text{sell}} \ge 36\%$ with lead over BUY), model triggers `🔴 SELL / BUY PUT (PE)` on 22950 PE Deep ITM with 1:2 Risk-Reward.
* **Prevention Rule:** Calibrate ML thresholds against real historical volatility percentiles (50th–75th percentile), never arbitrary round figures.

---

### BUG-003: 15-Min Macro Table Showing Stale "⚪ Consolidation" Labels
* **Date:** 2026-09-28 11:00 AM IST | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** `Buildup Signal` (Signal 1) displayed `⚪ Consolidation` across 10:30, 10:45, and 11:00 bars despite massive Call writing (+41L, +50L).
* **Root Cause:** `tab_15m` read static CSV string `r15.get("buildup_tag")` written at candle open when $\Delta\text{OI}$ was 0, instead of calculating dynamically from live Call & Put $\Delta\text{OI}$.
* **Fix Applied:** Updated `tab_15m` to dynamically compute `classify_pure_oi_signal(c_val_oi, p_val_oi, spot_change=p_chg15)` directly from the bar's actual Call and Put $\Delta\text{OI}$.
* **Prevention Rule:** Tables must dynamically calculate signals from live delta columns at render time.

---

### BUG-004: Corridor Session Log Cache-Key Mismatch In-Memory
* **Date:** 2026-09-28 11:12 AM IST | **File:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** `get_time_bucket_oi_delta` in long-running processes was not picking up fresh snapshot ticks written by `snapshot_corridor_oi`.
* **Root Cause:**
  * `snapshot_corridor_oi` stored entries under `self.corridor_session_log["NIFTY"]`.
  * `get_time_bucket_oi_delta` queried `self.corridor_session_log["NIFTY_2026-09-28"]`.
* **Fix Applied:**
  * Standardized cache key to `f"{sym_short}_{today_str}"` in both functions.
  * Added disk file modification time (`mtime`) auto-reload so separate processes stay in lockstep without restarts.
* **Prevention Rule:** Shared memory dictionaries across producer-consumer functions must use identical parameterized cache keys.

---

### BUG-005: 09:15 AM Opening Bar Blank "—" in Macro Table
* **Date:** 2026-09-28 09:30 AM IST | **File:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** First 15-minute candle (09:15 to 09:30) had blank `—` in Call $\Delta\text{OI}$ and Put $\Delta\text{OI}$.
* **Root Cause:**
  * Market opened at 23,053 (ATM 23,050) and fell 150 pts by 09:30 (ATM 22,900). Old corridor logic stored only 3 strikes each (calls $\ge$ ATM, puts $\le$ ATM), making the 09:15 and 09:30 strike sets disjoint (`common_calls = []`).
* **Fix Applied:** Stored all 5 active corridor strikes (ATM$\pm$2) for both Calls & Puts so a 150-pt move preserves overlapping strikes.
* **Prevention Rule:** Active writing corridors must capture ATM$\pm$2 symmetrically across both Call and Put legs.

---

### BUG-006: Extreme Traps Tagged as Bearish Divergence (Signal 2 Blind Spot)
* **Date:** 2026-09-26 | **File:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py), [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** At 13:45 on 25-Sep, Nifty climbed +20 pts with **98.2% aggressive Futures Buy Volume**, but legacy logic tagged it as `Short Buildup (Divergence)` in red, blinding the trader to an impending +100 point short squeeze.
* **Root Cause:** Legacy logic evaluated buildup purely based on $\Delta OI$, assuming all Call additions are sellers (Resistance).
* **Fix Applied:** Created `classify_smart_buildup_signal()` (Signal 2) that cross-checks Price Direction, Option Corridor $\Delta OI$, Futures Buy %, and Net Delta, outputting `⚡ Bear Trap` and `⚡ Bull Trap`.
* **Prevention Rule:** Never assume option delta direction without validating against aggressive futures market-order flow.

---

### BUG-007: Missing 3m & 15m Historical Candle Files Across Past Dates
* **Date:** 2026-09-26 | **File:** [storage_manager.py](file:///c:/Nifty/storage_manager.py), [record_service.py](file:///c:/Nifty/record_service.py)
* **Symptom:** Toggling to 3m chart on historical dates fell back to 5m files because 3m files were missing or truncated.
* **Root Cause:** Recorder only logged 5m candles, omitting 3m and 15m from background persistence.
* **Fix Applied:** Updated `record_service.py` to continuously record all three timeframes (`3m`, `5m`, and `15m`) every minute. Recovered historical files from Fyers API.
* **Prevention Rule:** All supported timeframes must have active, independent persistence paths in background daemons.

---

### BUG-008: Action Matrix `UnboundLocalError` (`DATA_DIR` Shadowing)
* **Date:** 2026-09-26 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Opening Action Matrix raised `UnboundLocalError: local variable 'DATA_DIR' referenced before assignment`.
* **Root Cause:** An inner `from config import DATA_DIR` deep inside the function caused Python to treat `DATA_DIR` as an unassigned local variable for the entire function.
* **Fix Applied:** Declared `global DATA_DIR` at the function header and removed all inner shadowed imports.
* **Prevention Rule:** Do not use inner local imports that shadow module-level constants.

---

### BUG-009: NoneType `AttributeError` in EOD Straddle Evaluation
* **Date:** 2026-09-26 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Dashboard crashed with `AttributeError: 'NoneType' object has no attribute 'empty'`.
* **Root Cause:** `not fo_ref.empty` was evaluated without checking `fo_ref is not None`.
* **Fix Applied:** Added strict null-check `fo_ref is not None and not fo_ref.empty`.
* **Prevention Rule:** Always guard DataFrame `.empty` checks with `df is not None`.

---

### BUG-010: ML Intraday Class Probability Positional Index Misalignment
* **Date:** 2026-09-26 | **File:** [ml_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/ml_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** `predict_intraday_signal` produced incorrect probabilities or raised `IndexError`.
* **Root Cause:** Hardcoded positional array indices `p_avg[0]`, `p_avg[1]`, `p_avg[2]` instead of binding to trained model `classes_`.
* **Fix Applied:** Bound probabilities through integer class ID mapping (`p_map = {0: 0.0, 1: 0.0, 2: 0.0}`).
* **Prevention Rule:** Model prediction probabilities must always map via `model.classes_`.

---

### BUG-011: Weekend / Holiday Auto-Fallback Opening Blank Saturday Files
* **Date:** 2026-09-26 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Opening dashboard on Saturday displayed empty or corrupted test data instead of Friday's settled close.
* **Root Cause:** `app.py` attempted to log ticks whenever `selected_date == today`, writing off-market files on Saturdays.
* **Fix Applied:** Created `get_latest_valid_trading_date()` that scans for genuine completed weekday sessions and runs in 100% read-only mode on weekends.
* **Prevention Rule:** Strictly guard live API polling and file logging with `if is_today and is_live_market_hours:`.

---

### BUG-012: EOD Radar Not Rendering on Historical Replay (`NameError: is_bearish`)
* **Date:** 2026-09-26 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Selecting 23-Sep or 25-Sep left EOD Radar unrendered with silent failure.
* **Root Cause:** Variable `is_bearish` was accidentally replaced during a previous edit, raising `NameError` caught silently by `except Exception:`.
* **Fix Applied:** Restored and refined `is_bearish`, `is_bullish`, and `is_capped_ceiling` boolean logic.
* **Prevention Rule:** Never allow bare `except Exception: pass` without logging error traceback during development.

---

### BUG-013: Fyers History API Chunk Duplication (2x Candles & Doubled CVD)
* **Date:** 2026-09-25 | **File:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py), [storage_manager.py](file:///c:/Nifty/storage_manager.py)
* **Symptom:** Session CVD accumulated to ~199k contracts instead of ~99k (2x double). Subplots plotted 150 candles instead of 75.
* **Root Cause:** Fyers API `history()` returned the trading day's candles repeated twice.
* **Fix Applied:** Added strict `.drop_duplicates(subset=["epoch"], keep="last").sort_values("epoch")` across all candle ingestion points.
* **Prevention Rule:** Ingested time-series data must be strictly deduplicated by unique timestamp/epoch before processing.

---

### BUG-014: Dual Concurrent Snapshot Logging between Daemon & Foreground
* **Date:** 2026-09-25 | **File:** [storage_manager.py](file:///c:/Nifty/storage_manager.py)
* **Symptom:** Two snapshots per minute written to CSV files (e.g. at :02s and :45s).
* **Root Cause:** Both `record_service.py` (daemon) and `app.py` (browser foreground) logged ticks without inter-process coordination.
* **Fix Applied:** Made storage idempotent 1-per-minute: checks last logged minute and skips redundant secondary writes.
* **Prevention Rule:** CSV append pipelines must enforce idempotency per discrete time interval.

---

### BUG-015: EOD Flow Inverted Ratio Causing False Gap-Up Alerts on MIS Exits
* **Date:** 2026-09-24 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Automated broker MIS intraday square-offs (e.g. -18L CE exit) falsely triggered "Gap-up Potential" when session was overwhelmingly Call dominant (-97L).
* **Root Cause:** Inverted ratio ignored the net session directional force.
* **Fix Applied:** Added Net Session Force (`net_force`) filter and two-stage verdict milestones (15:10 pre-square-off and 15:40 settled).
* **Prevention Rule:** Derivative flow ratios must be gated by net directional force sign.

---

### BUG-016: 15M Grouping Sliding Window Jitter & Missing PCR Columns
* **Date:** 2026-09-23 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** 15-Min table shifted Buy % on timeframe toggles and was missing PCR change tracking.
* **Root Cause:** Passed full 5m timestamp list with `tf_minutes=15`, causing a sliding window instead of discrete buckets.
* **Fix Applied:** Passed unique group bucket timestamps (`group_dts`) directly, standardized Buy % on 5m granular base, and added PCR / PCR Chg columns.
* **Prevention Rule:** Macro aggregation tables must resample strictly from fixed-interval granular bases.

---

### BUG-017: Top Status Bar Raw HTML Pre-tag Indentation Artifacts
* **Date:** 2026-09-23 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Dashboard rendered raw HTML code (`<div class="conn-badge-live">...</div>`) as literal text on screen.
* **Root Cause:** Markdown specification parses 4 or more leading spaces as a `<pre><code>` block. Multiline f-strings had 4 spaces of indentation.
* **Fix Applied:** Converted multiline f-strings to unindented single-line / flush HTML strings.
* **Prevention Rule:** Never indent HTML strings passed to `st.markdown(..., unsafe_allow_html=True)`.

---

### BUG-018: 09:15 AM Wall-Clock Boundary Match Failure (Seconds Lag)
* **Date:** 2026-09-23 | **File:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** 09:15 AM candle remained permanently blank on market open.
* **Root Cause:** `t <= boundary_time` required an exact wall-clock match at `09:15:00.000`. Real Fyers snapshots arrive at `09:15:03`, failing strict `<=`.
* **Fix Applied:** For `boundary_time <= market_open`, use the earliest available post-market-open (`>= 09:15:00`) snapshot as baseline.
* **Prevention Rule:** Boundary lookups at market open must accommodate real-world exchange polling latency.

---

### BUG-019: Pre-Market Snapshots Contaminating Tier 3 Fallback Baseline
* **Date:** 2026-09-23 | **File:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** Stale previous-day OI baseline reintroduced into Tier 3 option chain fallbacks.
* **Root Cause:** `last_corridor_snap` took `list(corridor_data.values())[-1]`, which returned pre-market (`< 09:15 AM`) snapshots.
* **Fix Applied:** Strictly filtered snapshots to `t >= 09:15:00 AM`.
* **Prevention Rule:** Exclude pre-market snapshots from intraday delta calculations.

---

### BUG-020: User Zoom & Pan Resetting on Every 15-Second Auto-Refresh
* **Date:** 2026-09-22 | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Streamlit auto-refreshes reset user zoom level and camera pan on Plotly charts.
* **Root Cause:** Missing `uirevision` layout property in Plotly figure definitions.
* **Fix Applied:** Added uniform `uirevision='nifty_terminal_persist_view'` across layout, X-axes, and Y-axes.
* **Prevention Rule:** All interactive Plotly figures in auto-refresh dashboards must specify `uirevision`.

### BUG-023: Cross-Day Contamination in Morning Open (Showing Yesterday Candles)

* **Symptom:** Between 09:15 and 09:29 AM, dashboard displayed yesterday's 80 candles instead of today's first candles.
* **Root Cause:** Guard `if not df_today.empty and len(df_today) >= 3:` fell back to previous day when today had fewer than 3 candles.
* **Fix Applied:** During trading hours, engine strictly isolates `df_today` from Candle 1 at 09:15:00 AM.
* **Prevention Rule:** Never fall back to previous-day candles during active market hours.

### BUG-024: Session CVD Accumulating Over 5 Days Without Morning Reset

* **Root Cause:** `cumsum()` ran across the entire multi-day dataframe without date grouping.
* **Fix Applied:** Grouped by calendar date so `session_cvd` strictly resets to `0` at 09:15 AM every morning.
* **Prevention Rule:** Session-level accumulators (CVD, volume, net deltas) must reset at session open.

### BUG-025: Missing `oich` Producing Silent Zeroes & False Consolidation

* **Root Cause:** `extract_item_oi_chg` defaulted to `0` on missing keys.
* **Fix Applied:** Built 3-tier mathematical fallback (Direct key $\rightarrow$ native `oi - prev_oi` $\rightarrow$ snapshot boundary diff) and guarded missing feeds with `None`.
* **Prevention Rule:** Missing data feeds must return `None` (triggering "Awaiting Feed"), never silent `0` values.

* **Fix Applied:** Added `.legend-pill-gold` base CSS with dark amber background (`#181305`), gold border (`1px solid #eab308`), and vibrant gold color (`#facc15`), matching the styling, geometry, and hover glow of the Green, Red, Blue, and White pills.
* **Prevention Rule:** Every UI element class in HTML markup must have both its base rule and pseudo-state rules (`:hover`, `:active`) fully declared.

### BUG-028: AI Quant Probability Text Tiny (9.5px) & Low Ergonomic Visibility

* **Fix Applied:** Re-engineered probability metrics into three distinct cyberpunk micro-chips (`15px` bold numbers, `11px` bold labels) with dark translucent backgrounds and neon green/amber/red borders, accompanied by a 5px segmented progress bar.

### BUG-029: 15-Min Scrubber Label Ambiguity (12:00 vs 12:15 vs Live) & Window Misalignment
* **Date:** 2026-09-28 12:30 PM IST | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** User observed severe ambiguity between `12:00`, `12:15`, and `🔴 LIVE`, unable to discern whether `12:15` was the start of the 12:15–12:30 bar or the end of the 12:00–12:15 bar, and what timeframe `🔴 LIVE` was comparing against.
* **Root Cause:**
  1. Naked timestamps (`12:00`, `12:15`) conflicted with exchange candlestick conventions (where a candle stamped `12:00` represents `12:00 ➔ 12:15`).
  2. The scrubber milestone treated `12:15` as the END of the previous bar, while the live candle labeled `12:15` was currently in progress on charts.
  3. `🔴 LIVE` was applying an unaligned rolling 15-minute lookback (`t_ref - 15m`) rather than binding to the discrete 15-minute candle opening boundary.
* **Fix Applied:**
  * Re-architected all scrubber milestones to explicit, unambiguous range labels: `11:45–12:00`, `12:00–12:15`, `12:15–12:30`, and `🔴 LIVE [12:30–Now]`.
  * Synchronized `t_base_15m` and `t_ref` to the exact start and end ticks of each discrete bar.
  * Added a dedicated **Active Timeframe Explainer Box** in the Action Matrix displaying `Active Window`, `Status Badge (Closed & Confirmed vs Live Forming)`, `Baseline ➔ Snapshot`, `Spot Price`, and description.
* **Prevention Rule:** Financial timeframe scrubbers must always declare explicit window spans (`Start–End`) and real-time forming statuses rather than bare snapshot timestamps.

### BUG-030: False Positive Bull Trap on Bearish OI Bars via Micro Green Candle (`spot_change >= 0`)
* **Date:** 2026-09-30 02:25 PM IST | **File:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** At `11:00 AM`, Spot Close was `22,689.50` (`+1.55` pts), Call $\Delta$OI was `+5.50L`, Put $\Delta$OI was `-4.52L` (Net $\Delta$OI `-10.02L`), and Futures Sell was `100.0%`. `Buildup Signal 2` falsely displayed `⚡ Bull Trap (100.0% Sell)` instead of `🔴 Short Buildup`.
* **Root Cause:** In `classify_smart_buildup_signal()`, the Bull Trap detector had `if bp <= 25.0: ... if net_oi >= 0 or spot_change >= 0: return '⚡ Bull Trap'`. A trivial +1.55 pt bounce candle satisfied `spot_change >= 0` even when options positioning was heavily bearish (`net_oi = -10.02L`). Since both options and futures were bearish, no bulls were actually trapped; it was genuine short buildup.
* **Fix Applied:** Enforced genuine divergence requirement:
  * Bull Trap requires `is_bullish_options = (net_oi > 0) or (pe_oi_change > 0 and pe_oi_change >= ce_oi_change) or (ce_oi_change < 0)`. If options are also bearish, it returns `🔴 Short Buildup`.
  * Bear Trap requires `is_bearish_options = (net_oi < 0) or (ce_oi_change > 0 and ce_oi_change >= pe_oi_change) or (pe_oi_change < 0)`. If options are also bullish, it returns `🟢 Long Buildup`.
* **Prevention Rule:** Traps must represent strict directional divergence between options positioning and futures order flow. Concordant flow between options and futures must always be classified as confirmed trend buildup.

### BUG-031: 15M Table Vol Delta In-Memory WebSocket Overwrite Mismatch (`+192` vs `-553`)
* **Date:** 2026-09-30 02:55 PM IST | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** At `13:00`, `Fut Buy Vol` showed `223` and `Fut Sell Vol` showed `36,112` (99.4% Sell), but `Buy Lots` showed `1,357`, `Sell Lots` showed `1,165`, and `Vol Delta` showed `+192` (Net positive buying). The columns contradicted each other within the same row.
* **Root Cause:** In `app.py` 15M table builder, an in-memory cache lookup (`of_item_15 = of_map_15m.get(t15_str)`) was selectively overriding `buy_lots`, `sell_lots`, and `delta_lots` with partial websocket ticks while leaving `bv15` and `sv15` as the candle file values (`223` and `36,112`).
* **Fix Applied:** If recorded futures volumes (`bv15` and `sv15`) exist on the candle, always compute `buy_lots`, `sell_lots`, and `delta_lots` directly from `bv15` and `sv15` using the official lot size (`65`). Only fallback to `of_map_15m` if candle futures volume is missing.
* **Prevention Rule:** Never mix volume contracts from one data feed with lot counts from a different streaming cache in the same display row.

### BUG-032: Outdated Nifty Futures Lot Size (25 vs Revised NSE 65 Shares)
* **Date:** 2026-09-30 02:45 PM IST | **Files:** [config.py](file:///c:/Nifty/config.py), [order_flow_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/order_flow_engine.py), [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Nifty Futures `Buy Lots`, `Sell Lots`, and `Vol Delta` were computed dividing contracts by outdated lot size `25`, inflating lot counts (e.g. 103,285 contracts displayed as 4,131 lots instead of 1,589 lots).
* **Root Cause:** Hardcoded `lot_size = 25` / `lot_sz = 25` in `order_flow_engine.py` and `app.py`.
* **Fix Applied:** Added `NIFTY_LOT_SIZE = 65` in `config.py` and updated all lot divisors in `order_flow_engine.py` and `app.py` (5m and 15m tables and footers) to `65`.
* **Prevention Rule:** Centralize instrument contract parameters (lot size, strike step, expiry cycle) in `config.py` to prevent fragmented hardcoded constants.

### BUG-033: Cloud Server UTC Timezone Drift Causing False "Market Closed" & Disabled Live Stream on Streamlit Cloud
* **Date:** 2026-10-01 12:55 PM IST | **Files:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py), [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py)
* **Symptom:** When deployed to Streamlit Community Cloud (`niftyscrubber.streamlit.app`), the terminal falsely displayed `🏁 Market Closed — Session: 01-Oct-2026`, `⚠️ No recorded session history found for 01-Oct-2026`, and `⏸️ Auto-Refresh Paused: Market Closed (Post 03:40 PM IST)`. Meanwhile, localhost on the local Windows PC was actively streaming live market data during peak trading hours.
* **Root Cause:** Streamlit Community Cloud Linux server containers run in standard UTC time (`07:22 AM UTC` when IST is `12:52 PM`). Unadorned calls to `datetime.datetime.now().time()` and `datetime.date.today()` evaluated against UTC rather than Indian Standard Time (IST / `Asia/Kolkata`). Because `07:22 AM < 09:15 AM`, `is_live_market_hours` and `is_trading_hours` evaluated to `False`. This triggered the off-hours static branch, paused the auto-refresh loop, suppressed live Fyers API option chain queries, and attempted to load static local CSV files that were not present on the ephemeral cloud container.
* **Fix Applied:**
  * Created explicit IST time helpers `get_ist_now()` and `get_ist_date()` leveraging `zoneinfo.ZoneInfo("Asia/Kolkata")` with UTC offset fallback (`UTC + 5:30`).
  * Replaced all raw `datetime.datetime.now()` and `datetime.date.today()` calls in market hour checks (`09:15 – 15:35 IST`), session calendar selectors, and boundary-snapping auto-refresh loops across [`app.py`](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py) and [`oi_engine.py`](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py).
  * Added an automated FYERS historical API fallback for candlestick data when local recorded files are not present on disk on cloud instances.
---

### BUG-034: Dummy 0-OI Simulation Snapshot Corruption Causing Missing Call/Put ΔOI (`— —`)
* **Date:** 2026-10-01 02:12 PM IST | **Files:** [oi_engine.py](file:///c:/Nifty/oi_engine.py), [corridor_oi_NIFTY_2026-10-01.json](file:///c:/Nifty/data/oi_history/corridor_oi_NIFTY_2026-10-01.json)
* **Symptom:** In the 5-Minute and 15-Minute Bar-by-Bar tables, `Call ΔOI` and `Put ΔOI` suddenly displayed `— —` (missing em-dashes) for `14:00` and `14:05` bars on both localhost and Streamlit Cloud, even though `Spot Close`, `Candle Chg`, and `Vol Delta` were updating normally.
* **Root Cause:** A transient fallback tick during an API re-initialization injected 3 dummy simulation snapshots (`13:09:01`, `13:58:01`, `14:04:01`) into `corridor_oi_NIFTY_2026-10-01.json` with simulation strikes (`24450..24650`) and 0 open interest. When `get_time_bucket_oi_delta()` performed boundary matching for the 14:00 and 14:05 candles (`bar_end = 14:05:00`), it selected `14:04:01` as the end snapshot. Comparing genuine Nifty strikes (`22150..22350`) against dummy strikes (`24450..24650`) yielded zero overlapping strikes (`common_calls = []`), returning `np.nan` (`— —`).
* **Fix Applied:**
  * Purged the corrupted snapshot keys (`13:09:01`, `13:58:01`, `14:04:01`) from `corridor_oi_NIFTY_2026-10-01.json`.
  * Added validation in `get_time_bucket_oi_delta()`: any snapshot with `sum(calls.values()) == 0` or `sum(puts.values()) == 0` is strictly skipped from boundary readings.
  * Verified delta recovery: `14:00` restored to `Call ΔOI: +13.22L | Put ΔOI: -9.74L`, `14:05` restored to `Call ΔOI: +12.88L | Put ΔOI: -0.82L`.
---

### BUG-035: Dynamic Key in `st_autorefresh` Freezing Localhost Timer Loop
* **Date:** 2026-10-01 03:24 PM IST | **File:** [app.py](file:///c:/Nifty/app.py)
* **Symptom:** Localhost dashboard stopped auto-updating, while Streamlit Cloud was refreshing normally. Data froze on the last rendered minute.
* **Root Cause:** In `app.py`, `st_autorefresh` was called with a dynamic key (`key=f"nifty_1m_snap_{cur_min}_{cur_sec}"`). In Streamlit's React component lifecycle, changing the key on every render destroys the previous component instance and unmounts the internal JavaScript `setInterval` timer, causing the timer loop to halt after a single execution.
* **Fix Applied:** Replaced dynamic key with a permanent static key: `st_autorefresh(interval=refresh_ms, key="nifty_dashboard_autorefresh_timer")`. Removed the redundant iframe script to prevent cross-origin DOM console warnings.
* **Prevention Rule:** Never use dynamic timestamps or varying variables in `st_autorefresh(key=...)`. The component key must remain strictly static across reruns to preserve the React timer interval.

"""
Order Flow Engine for NiftyScrubber
Implements real-time Level 2 Order Flow & Cumulative Volume Delta (CVD) tracking
using the FYERS API v3 WebSocket (Lee-Ready algorithm with Tick Rule fallback).
Thread-safe, non-blocking background daemon with JSON persistence and 15m macro aggregation.
"""

import os
import json
import time
import datetime
from datetime import timedelta
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from config import DATA_DIR, load_env_vars
from fyers_auth import FyersAuthManager
from oi_engine import get_nifty_future_symbol

try:
    from fyers_apiv3.FyersWebsocket import data_ws
except ImportError:
    data_ws = None

_ENGINE_INSTANCE = None
_ENGINE_LOCK = threading.Lock()

def get_5min_floor(dt: datetime.datetime) -> datetime.datetime:
    """Rounds a datetime object down to the nearest 5-minute interval."""
    discard = timedelta(minutes=dt.minute % 5, seconds=dt.second, microseconds=dt.microsecond)
    return dt - discard

class OrderFlowEngine:
    def __init__(self, lot_size: int = 65):
        self.lot_size = lot_size
        self.auth_manager = FyersAuthManager()
        self.symbol: Optional[str] = None
        self.ws = None
        self.ws_thread = None
        self.is_running = False
        self.is_connected = False
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

        # State tracking
        self.current_bar = {
            "start_time": None,
            "buy_volume": 0.0,
            "sell_volume": 0.0,
            "last_price": None,
            "bid": None,
            "ask": None
        }
        self.closed_bars: List[Dict] = []
        self._load_today_cached_bars()

    def _get_cache_filepath(self, date_str: str) -> Path:
        DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return DATA_DIR / f"order_flow_5m_{date_str}.json"

    def _load_today_cached_bars(self):
        """Loads already recorded bars from disk if available for today."""
        today_str = datetime.date.today().isoformat()
        fpath = self._get_cache_filepath(today_str)
        if fpath.exists():
            try:
                with open(fpath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    if isinstance(data, list):
                        self.closed_bars = data
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[OrderFlow] Error loading cached bars: {e}")

    def _save_bar_to_cache(self, bar_data: Dict):
        """Appends a closed bar to today's JSON storage."""
        date_str = bar_data.get("date_str", datetime.date.today().isoformat())
        fpath = self._get_cache_filepath(date_str)
        try:
            existing = []
            if fpath.exists():
                try:
                    with open(fpath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        existing = json.load(f)
                except Exception:
                    existing = []

            # Deduplicate by time_str
            filtered = [b for b in existing if b.get("time_str") != bar_data.get("time_str")]
            filtered.append(bar_data)
            filtered.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("time_str", ""))

            with open(fpath, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(filtered, f, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[OrderFlow] Error persisting bar: {e}")

    def process_trade_tick(self, trade_price: float, trade_qty: float) -> Tuple[str, float]:
        """
        Lee-Ready algorithm classification with Tick Rule fallback:
        1. trade_price >= ask -> Aggressive BUY
        2. trade_price <= bid -> Aggressive SELL
        3. Inside spread -> Tick Rule comparison against previous last_price
        """
        bid = self.current_bar.get("bid")
        ask = self.current_bar.get("ask")
        last_price = self.current_bar.get("last_price")

        # 1. Spread test
        if bid is not None and ask is not None and ask > bid:
            if trade_price >= ask:
                return "BUY", trade_qty
            elif trade_price <= bid:
                return "SELL", trade_qty

        # 2. Tick Rule Fallback
        if last_price is not None:
            if trade_price > last_price:
                return "BUY", trade_qty
            elif trade_price < last_price:
                return "SELL", trade_qty

        return "UNKNOWN", 0.0

    def _on_message(self, message):
        """Callback for incoming WebSocket trade ticks."""
        if not message:
            return

        packets = message if isinstance(message, list) else [message]

        with self._lock:
            for pkt in packets:
                if not isinstance(pkt, dict):
                    continue

                # Extract timestamp
                exch_time = pkt.get("last_traded_time") or pkt.get("exch_feed_time")
                if exch_time and isinstance(exch_time, (int, float)) and exch_time > 1000000000:
                    now = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(exch_time)
                else:
                    now = datetime.datetime.now()

                bar_start = get_5min_floor(now)

                # Initialize first bar
                if self.current_bar["start_time"] is None:
                    self.current_bar["start_time"] = bar_start

                # Candle Rollover Check
                if bar_start > self.current_bar["start_time"]:
                    b_vol = self.current_bar["buy_volume"]
                    s_vol = self.current_bar["sell_volume"]
                    tot_vol = b_vol + s_vol

                    buy_lots = round(b_vol / self.lot_size, 2)
                    sell_lots = round(s_vol / self.lot_size, 2)
                    delta_lots = round(buy_lots - sell_lots, 2)
                    buy_pct = round(100.0 * b_vol / tot_vol, 1) if tot_vol > 0 else 50.0

                    bar_data = {
                        "time_str": self.current_bar["start_time"].strftime("%H:%M"),
                        "date_str": self.current_bar["start_time"].strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
                        "buy_volume": b_vol,
                        "sell_volume": s_vol,
                        "buy_lots": buy_lots,
                        "sell_lots": sell_lots,
                        "delta_lots": delta_lots,
                        "buy_pct": buy_pct
                    }
                    if b_vol > 0 or s_vol > 0:
                        self.closed_bars.append(bar_data)
                        self._save_bar_to_cache(bar_data)

                    # Reset accumulators for the new 5-minute candle
                    self.current_bar = {
                        "start_time": bar_start,
                        "buy_volume": 0.0,
                        "sell_volume": 0.0,
                        "last_price": self.current_bar["last_price"],
                        "bid": self.current_bar["bid"],
                        "ask": self.current_bar["ask"]
                    }

                # Update quotes (bid / ask)
                bid_val = pkt.get("bid_price", pkt.get("bid"))
                ask_val = pkt.get("ask_price", pkt.get("ask"))
                if bid_val is not None:
                    try:
                        self.current_bar["bid"] = float(bid_val)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                if ask_val is not None:
                    try:
                        self.current_bar["ask"] = float(ask_val)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # Process traded volume tick
                ltp_val = pkt.get("ltp", pkt.get("last_price"))
                qty_val = pkt.get("last_traded_qty")

                if ltp_val is not None and qty_val is not None:
                    try:
                        trade_price = float(ltp_val)
                        trade_qty = float(qty_val)

                        if trade_qty > 0:
                            side, qty = self.process_trade_tick(trade_price, trade_qty)
                            if side == "BUY":
                                self.current_bar["buy_volume"] += qty
                            elif side == "SELL":
                                self.current_bar["sell_volume"] += qty

                        self.current_bar["last_price"] = trade_price
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    def _on_connect(self):
        self.is_connected = True
        print(f"[OrderFlow] Connected to FYERS WebSocket. Subscribing to {self.symbol}...")
        try:
            self.ws.subscribe(symbols=[self.symbol], data_type="SymbolUpdate")
            print(f"[OrderFlow] Subscribed successfully to {self.symbol}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[OrderFlow] Subscription error: {e}")

    def _on_close(self, message=None):
        self.is_connected = False
        print(f"[OrderFlow] WebSocket connection closed: {message}")

    def _on_error(self, message):
        print(f"[OrderFlow] WebSocket Error: {message}")

    def start(self):
        """Starts the WebSocket listener in a background daemon thread."""
        if self.is_running:
            return

        if data_ws is None:
            print("[OrderFlow] fyers_apiv3.FyersWebsocket not available.")
            return

        # Resolve credentials
        token_data = self.auth_manager._read_cached_token()
        if not token_data or not token_data.get("access_token"):
            print("[OrderFlow] No valid cached Fyers token found. Attempting headless auth...")
            fyers, status = self.auth_manager.get_active_client()
            token_data = self.auth_manager._read_cached_token()

        if not token_data or not token_data.get("access_token"):
            print("[OrderFlow] Authentication failed. Cannot start WebSocket.")
            return

        client_id = token_data.get("client_id", self.auth_manager.get_credentials().get("client_id", ""))
        access_token = token_data.get("access_token", "")
        formatted_token = f"{client_id}:{access_token}" if ":" not in access_token else access_token

        # Resolve active futures symbol
        try:
            self.symbol = get_nifty_future_symbol()
        except Exception:
            self.symbol = "NSE:NIFTY26OCTFUT"

        print(f"[OrderFlow] Initializing OrderFlowEngine for {self.symbol} (Lot size: {self.lot_size})...")

        try:
            log_dir = Path("fyers_logs")
            log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            self.ws = data_ws.FyersDataSocket(
                access_token=formatted_token,
                log_path=str(log_dir.resolve()),
                litemode=False,
                reconnect=True,
                on_connect=self._on_connect,
                on_close=self._on_close,
                on_error=self._on_error,
                on_message=self._on_message
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[OrderFlow] Error instantiating FyersDataSocket: {e}")
            return

        def _run():
            self.is_running = True
            try:
                self.ws.connect()
                self.ws.keep_running()
                while self.is_running:
                    time.sleep(1)
            except Exception as ex:
                print(f"[OrderFlow] WebSocket runner error: {ex}")
            finally:
                self.is_running = False
                self.is_connected = False

        self.ws_thread = threading.Thread(target=_run, daemon=True, name="OrderFlowWSThread")
        self.ws_thread.start()
        print("[OrderFlow] WebSocket thread launched successfully.")

    def get_5m_order_flow_map(self, target_date_str: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """
        Returns a dictionary mapping 'HH:MM' -> bar orderflow data for the specified date.
        Includes closed bars from cache/memory plus the current live active bar.
        """
        if not target_date_str:
            target_date_str = datetime.date.today().isoformat()

        res = {}
        # 1. Read from disk for that date
        fpath = self._get_cache_filepath(target_date_str)
        if fpath.exists():
            try:
                with open(fpath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    cached = json.load(f)
                    for item in cached:
                        t = item.get("time_str")
                        if t:
                            res[t] = item
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 2. Overlay in-memory closed bars if today
        if target_date_str == datetime.date.today().isoformat():
            with self._lock:
                for b in self.closed_bars:
                    t = b.get("time_str")
                    if t:
                        res[t] = b

                # 3. Add live active accumulating bar only if it has real trade volume
                if self.current_bar["start_time"] is not None:
                    b_vol = self.current_bar["buy_volume"]
                    s_vol = self.current_bar["sell_volume"]
                    if b_vol > 0 or s_vol > 0:
                        active_t = self.current_bar["start_time"].strftime("%H:%M")
                        tot_vol = b_vol + s_vol
                        buy_lots = round(b_vol / self.lot_size, 2)
                        sell_lots = round(s_vol / self.lot_size, 2)
                        delta_lots = round(buy_lots - sell_lots, 2)
                        buy_pct = round(100.0 * b_vol / tot_vol, 1) if tot_vol > 0 else 50.0

                        res[active_t] = {
                            "time_str": active_t,
                            "date_str": target_date_str,
                            "buy_volume": b_vol,
                            "sell_volume": s_vol,
                            "buy_lots": buy_lots,
                            "sell_lots": sell_lots,
                            "delta_lots": delta_lots,
                            "buy_pct": buy_pct,
                            "is_live": True
                        }

        return res

    def get_15m_macro_order_flow_map(self, target_date_str: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """
        Aggregates 5-minute order flow into standard 15-minute macro intervals:
        Window 09:15 encompasses (09:15, 09:20, 09:25).
        """
        five_map = self.get_5m_order_flow_map(target_date_str)
        if not five_map:
            return {}

        macro_15m = {}
        for t_str, b_data in five_map.items():
            try:
                hh, mm = map(int, t_str.split(":"))
                # Floor minute to 15m
                macro_mm = (mm // 15) * 15
                macro_key = f"{hh:02d}:{macro_mm:02d}"

                if macro_key not in macro_15m:
                    macro_15m[macro_key] = {
                        "time_str": macro_key,
                        "date_str": b_data.get("date_str", target_date_str),
                        "buy_volume": 0.0,
                        "sell_volume": 0.0,
                        "buy_lots": 0.0,
                        "sell_lots": 0.0,
                        "delta_lots": 0.0,
                        "buy_pct": 50.0,
                        "bars_count": 0
                    }

                m = macro_15m[macro_key]
                m["buy_volume"] += b_data.get("buy_volume", 0.0)
                m["sell_volume"] += b_data.get("sell_volume", 0.0)
                m["buy_lots"] += b_data.get("buy_lots", 0.0)
                m["sell_lots"] += b_data.get("sell_lots", 0.0)
                m["bars_count"] += 1
            except Exception:
                continue

        # Finalize deltas & percentages
        for m in macro_15m.values():
            m["buy_lots"] = round(m["buy_lots"], 2)
            m["sell_lots"] = round(m["sell_lots"], 2)
            m["delta_lots"] = round(m["buy_lots"] - m["sell_lots"], 2)
            tot_v = m["buy_volume"] + m["sell_volume"]
            m["buy_pct"] = round(100.0 * m["buy_volume"] / tot_v, 1) if tot_v > 0 else 50.0

        return macro_15m

def get_order_flow_singleton(lot_size: int = 65) -> OrderFlowEngine:
    """Thread-safe singleton getter."""
    global _ENGINE_INSTANCE
    with _ENGINE_LOCK:
        if _ENGINE_INSTANCE is None:
            _ENGINE_INSTANCE = OrderFlowEngine(lot_size=lot_size)
            # Automatically start in background if valid token exists
            try:
                _ENGINE_INSTANCE.start()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[OrderFlow] Singleton startup notice: {e}")
        return _ENGINE_INSTANCE
